📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • CHB 患者表现出未转换记忆和双阴性 1 B 细胞亚群的扩张,并伴有类别转换受损、共刺激分子表达改变、克隆扩张增加、IGHV 基因使用偏向、体细胞超突变增加、氧化磷酸化增加以及 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞中谷氨酰胺代谢减少。
  • 参与者中自我报告的 HepB 诊断很少,0.6% (13/2,358) 报告之前有过 诊断。自我报告阳性疫苗接种病史的比例为56.3%(1,225/2,175)。不同亚组的积极 疫苗接种 历史各不相同,与农村地区相比,男性、受教育程度较高的参与者以及大城市参与者的比例较高。随着已知肝炎事实数量的增加,疫苗接种 病史阳性的比例也随之增加。
  • 显微镜检查显示,PTB 抑制含有内含子的 CPS-PRE 小基因转录本的核输出,导致其核保留。RNA 免疫沉淀证明 CPS-PRE 转录本与 NXF1-NXT1 输出机制存在稳定的相互作用,而未检测到与核转运蛋白 CRM1 途径的相互作用。
  • 该文章最初报道的研究结果表明,大戟提取物通过 α 丝氨酸/苏氨酸蛋白激酶和 Caspase-3 抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的肝细胞癌。然而,由于担心可重复性,这些结果现在被撤回。 [10.1007/s00432-026-06572-z]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • CHB patients showed expansion of unswitched memory and double-negative 1 B cell subsets with impaired class switching, altered co-stimulatory molecule expression, increased clonal expansion, biased IGHV gene usage, elevated somatic hypermutation, increased oxidative phosphorylation, and reduced glutamine metabolism in HBsAg-specific B cells.
  • Self-reported HepB diagnoses were rare among participants, with 0.6% (13/2,358) reporting a previous diagnosis. The proportion of self-reported positive vaccination history was 56.3% (1,225/2,175).
  • Microscopy showed that PTB inhibits nuclear export of the intron-containing CPS-PRE minigene transcript, resulting in its nuclear retention. [10.1007/s13337-026-00978-6].
  • The article originally reported findings suggesting that Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract suppresses hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma via alpha serine/threonine-protein kinase and Caspase-3.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of acquired immune deficiency syndromes (1999)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1097/qai.0000000000003896  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 101, 9, 964-972
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HIV 治疗 网站的乙型和丙型肝炎预防、筛查和诊断服务:评估艾滋病的国际 流行病学 数据库。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.
作者： Mark H Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的预防、筛查和诊断服务可以预防接受 HIV 护理的人群发病和 死亡率。然而,有关全球艾滋病毒诊所提供的乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务的信息有限。方法:国际 流行病学 艾滋病评估数据库 (IeDEA) 对来自 7 个地区的参与 HIV 治疗 中心的服务提供和实践进行了调查。我们使用 2023 年调查数据来衡量 HBV 疫苗接种、HBV 和 HCV 筛查、HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg)、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 检测的可用性。使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务的关联。结果:204 个 HIV 治疗 站点中 67.7% 的 HBV 疫苗接种 可现场获得。据报告,72.1% 和 50% 的机构在 HIV 护理登记时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查。HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测率分别为 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6%。主要服务于农村(相对于城市)人口的站点不太可能报告 HBV DNA 的现场可用性 [比值比 (OR):0.07;95%置信区间:0.01至0.68;P = 0.02]、HCV 抗体(OR = 0.18;95% CI:0.04 至 0.92;P = 0.04)和 HCV RNA(OR = 0.10;95% CI:0.01 至 0.90;P = 0.04)检测。结论:参与 IeDEA 网络的大多数 HIV 治疗 站点均可现场提供 HBV 疫苗接种、HBsAg 和 HCV 抗体检测等挽救生命的服务。农村地区的可用性较低表明,扩大服务范围对于消除乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎作为接受艾滋病毒护理的人群的公共卫生问题非常重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了以下公共卫生问题:尽管提供了有效的预防和诊断服务,但在接受艾滋病毒护理的人群中,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的发病率和 死亡率 仍然很高。作者发现了艾滋病诊所在全球范围内提供乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
国际 流行病学 艾滋病评估数据库 (IeDEA) 于 2023 年对全球七个地区的 204 个 HIV 治疗 中心的服务提供和实践进行了横断面调查。
核心方法
该调查测量了 HBV 疫苗接种、HBV 和 HCV 筛查以及特定测试(HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体、HCV RNA)的现场可用性。使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务可用性之间的关联。
主要结果
67.7% 的研究中心可现场进行 HBV 疫苗接种,分别有 72.1% 和 50% 的研究中心报告在入组时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查,分别有 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6% 的研究中心进行了 HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测。主要服务于农村人口的站点不太可能提供现场 HBV DNA(OR 0.07;95% CI 0.01-0.68;P=0.02)、HCV 抗体(OR 0.18;95% CI 0.04-0.92;P=0.04)和 HCV RNA(OR 0.10;95% CI 0.01-0.90;P=0.04)。P=0.04)测试。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,大多数参与 HIV 治疗 的站点都可以现场提供挽救生命的 HBV 和 HCV 服务,但农村地区的可用性较低,凸显了可能阻碍消除工作的服务差距。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,扩大乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务,特别是在农村地区,对于在接受艾滋病毒护理的人群中实现消除目标至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告调查数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能高估了服务的可用性;由于潜在的报告偏差和 IeDEA 网络站点之外的普遍性有限,证据强度中等。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.

Authors: Mark H Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Prevention, screening, and diagnostic services for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) can prevent morbidity and mortality in people receiving HIV care. However, there is limited information about the availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics globally. METHODS: The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted surveys of service delivery and practices at participating HIV treatment centers from 7 regions. We used 2023 survey data to measure availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, HBV surface antigen (HBsAg), HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing. Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations of site characteristics with HBV and HCV services. RESULTS: HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of 204 HIV treatment sites. Screening for HBV and HCV at HIV care enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively. HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively. Sites serving predominately rural (vs. urban) populations were less likely to report on-site availability of HBV DNA [odds ratio (OR):0.07; 95% confidence interval: 0.01 to 0.68; P = 0.02], HCV antibody (OR = 0.18; 95% CI: 0.04 to 0.92; P = 0.04), and HCV RNA (OR = 0.10; 95% CI: 0.01 to 0.90; P = 0.04) testing. CONCLUSION: Life-saving services such as HBV vaccination, HBsAg, and HCV antibody testing were available on-site at most HIV treatment sites participating in the IeDEA network. Lower availability at rural sites suggests that expansion of services is important to eliminate HBV and HCV as public health problems in people receiving HIV care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem that morbidity and mortality from hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) remain high among people receiving HIV care, despite the availability of effective prevention and diagnostic services. The authors identified a knowledge gap regarding the global availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics.
Design and population
The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted a cross-sectional survey of service delivery and practices at 204 HIV treatment centers across seven global regions in 2023.
Core methods
The survey measured on-site availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, and specific tests (HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, HCV RNA). Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations between site characteristics and the availability of HBV and HCV services.
Main results
HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of sites, screening for HBV and HCV at enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively, and HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively. Sites serving predominantly rural populations were less likely to offer on-site HBV DNA (OR 0.07; 95% CI 0.01–0.68; P=0.02), HCV antibody (OR 0.18; 95% CI 0.04–0.92; P=0.04), and HCV RNA (OR 0.10; 95% CI 0.01–0.90; P=0.04) testing.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that life-saving HBV and HCV services were available on-site at most participating HIV treatment sites, but lower availability in rural settings highlights a service gap that could impede elimination efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that expanding HBV and HCV services, particularly in rural areas, is critical to achieving elimination goals among people receiving HIV care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported survey data limit causal inference and may overestimate service availability; the evidence strength is moderate due to potential reporting bias and limited generalizability beyond IeDEA network sites.

使用反射测试系统在初级保健中对乙型和丙型肝炎进行机会性筛查:微消除模型。

Opportunistic Screening for Hepatitis B and C in Primary Care Using a Reflex Testing System: A Microelimination Model.
作者： Imma Valls, María Santos, Rosa Soriano, Montserrat Mullol, Ariadna Rando-Segura, Elena Vargas-Accarino, María Buti
摘要中文翻译简介:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 感染仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。要在 2030 年实现世界卫生组织 (WHO) 消除目标,需要比基于风险因素的筛查更有效的检测策略。本研究评估了初级保健中实施的机会性筛查计划,其中包含自动诊断和转诊途径。患者和方法:在巴塞罗那初级保健中心进行的通用 HBsAg 和抗 HCV 筛查的前瞻性研究。2023 年 2 月至 2025 年 2 月期间因任何原因需要进行血液检查的成年人也包括在内。阳性病例通过自动化护理途径直接转诊至肝病科。结果:共有 3,103 人接受了筛查(41% 为男性;平均年龄 51 岁)。抗HCV阳性患病率为2.0%,其中0.35%为活动性感染;82% 的病毒血症患者启动 抗病毒药物 治疗。HBsAg 患病率 为 0.8%,92% 的转诊病例接受了专科护理;17% 发起了 治疗。较多的危险因素与抗 HCV 阳性相关,但未发现 HBsAg 阳性的预测因子。值得注意的是,45% 的可检测到 HCV RNA 的患者和 44% 的 HBsAg 阳性个体既没有公认的危险因素,也没有升高的 ALT 水平。结论:初级保健中的自动机会性筛查是可行且有效的,可识别使用基于危险因素的筛查策略可能未被发现的 HBV 和 HCV 感染。这种综合方法可能会对病毒性肝炎的微观消除做出重大贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题,到 2030 年实现世界卫生组织消除目标需要比基于危险因素的筛查更有效的检测策略。本研究评估了初级保健中的机会性筛查计划,以评估其在识别 HBV 感染方面的可行性和有效性。
设计与对象
巴塞罗那一家初级保健中心开展了一项针对 HBsAg 的前瞻性普遍筛查研究,研究对象包括 2023 年 2 月至 2025 年 2 月期间因任何原因需要进行血液检测的成年人。阳性病例通过自动化护理途径直接转诊至肝病科。
核心方法
该研究使用自动诊断和转诊途径在初级保健机构中实施了普遍的 HBsAg 筛查。阳性病例的识别和转诊无需额外的风险因素评估。
主要结果
共筛查了 3,103 人,HBsAg 患病率 为 0.8%。在 HBsAg 阳性个体中,92% 的人接受了专科护理,17% 的人开始了 治疗。值得注意的是,44% 的 HBsAg 阳性个体既没有公认的危险因素,也没有升高的 ALT 水平。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明初级保健中的自动机会性筛查是可行且有效的,可以识别使用基于危险因素的筛查策略可能未被发现的乙​​型肝炎病毒感染。他们强调了整合自动化护理途径以简化检测和转诊的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这种综合方法可能通过提高初级保健机构的检出率,特别是在没有公认的危险因素或 ALT 水平升高的个体中,为 HBV 的微消除做出重大贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计和缺乏比较组的限制,限制了普遍性和评估因果影响的能力。由于这些设计限制,证据强度中等。

Opportunistic Screening for Hepatitis B and C in Primary Care Using a Reflex Testing System: A Microelimination Model.

Authors: Imma Valls, María Santos, Rosa Soriano, Montserrat Mullol, Ariadna Rando-Segura, Elena Vargas-Accarino, María Buti
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections remain a significant public health concern. Achieving the World Health Organization (WHO) elimination targets by 2030 requires more effective detection strategies than risk factor-based screening. This study evaluates an opportunistic screening program implemented in Primary Care, incorporating an automated diagnostic and referral pathway. PATIENTS AND METHODS: Prospective study of universal HBsAg and anti-HCV screening conducted in a Primary Care center in Barcelona. Adults requiring a blood test for any reason between February 2023 and February 2025 were included. Positive cases were directly referred to the Hepatology Department through an automated care pathway. RESULTS: A total of 3,103 individuals were screened (41% male; mean age 51 years). The prevalence of anti-HCV positivity was 2.0%, with 0.35% having active infection; 82% of viremic patients initiated antiviral treatment. HBsAg prevalence was 0.8%, and 92% of referred cases attended specialist care; 17% initiated treatment. A higher number of risk factors was associated with anti-HCV positivity, whereas no predictors of HBsAg positivity were identified. Notably, 45% of patients with detectable HCV RNA and 44% of HBsAg-positive individuals had neither recognized risk factors nor elevated ALT levels. CONCLUSIONS: Automated opportunistic screening in Primary Care is feasible and effective, identifying HBV and HCV infections that would likely remain undetected using risk factor-based screening strategies. This integrated approach may contribute substantially to the micro-elimination of viral hepatitis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a significant public health concern, and achieving WHO elimination targets by 2030 requires more effective detection strategies than risk factor-based screening. This study evaluates an opportunistic screening program in Primary Care to assess its feasibility and effectiveness in identifying HBV infections.
Design and population
A prospective universal screening study for HBsAg was conducted in a Primary Care center in Barcelona, including adults requiring a blood test for any reason between February 2023 and February 2025. Positive cases were directly referred to the Hepatology Department through an automated care pathway.
Core methods
The study implemented universal HBsAg screening in a Primary Care setting using an automated diagnostic and referral pathway. Positive cases were identified and referred without additional risk factor assessment.
Main results
A total of 3,103 individuals were screened, with HBsAg prevalence of 0.8%. Among HBsAg-positive individuals, 92% attended specialist care and 17% initiated treatment. Notably, 44% of HBsAg-positive individuals had neither recognized risk factors nor elevated ALT levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that automated opportunistic screening in Primary Care is feasible and effective, identifying HBV infections that would likely remain undetected using risk factor-based screening strategies. They highlight the novelty of integrating an automated care pathway to streamline detection and referral.
Scientific and public-health significance
This integrated approach may contribute substantially to the micro-elimination of HBV by improving detection rates in primary care settings, particularly among individuals without recognized risk factors or elevated ALT levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design and lack of a comparator group, which constrains generalizability and the ability to assess causal impact. The evidence strength is moderate due to these design limitations.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

替诺福韦艾拉酚胺和恩替卡韦之间的乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体动力学差异:乙型肝炎 e 抗原阳性患者的肝细胞癌风险分层。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.
作者： Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管核(酸)类似物疗法可抑制 HBV DNA,但残留肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在,因此需要更好的风险分层标记。该研究询问 on-治疗 乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体 (HBsAgGi) 动力学是否可以区分 HCC 风险,以及替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 在调节这些动力学方面是否优于恩替卡韦 (ETV)。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,纳入了 90 名未接受过 NA 的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者,开始使用 TAF (n=17) 或 ETV (n=73),并随访 48 周。该人群包括接受标准护理 抗病毒药物 治疗的 HBeAg 阳性和 HBeAg 阴性成年人。
核心方法
使用基因型 C 特异性 O-糖基化测定对 HBsAgGi 进行定量;有利的反应被定义为降低或维持低 HBsAgGi 水平。带有惩罚估计的逻辑回归评估了有利的 HBsAgGi 动力学的预测因子,Cox 模型评估了 HCC 风险关联。
主要结果
TAF independently predicted a favourable HBsAgGi response versus ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p=0.030). In HBeAg-positive patients, poor HBsAgGi response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% CI 1.96–20.35, p=0.001), whereas no association was observed in HBeAg-negative patients.
解释与创新
作者将 治疗 HBsAgGi 反应解释为 HBeAg 阳性患者残留 HCC 风险的特异性替代标志物,并得出结论,TAF 比 ETV 诱导更有利的 HBsAgGi 动力学。这是第一份将 NA 治疗期间 HBsAgGi 动力学与 HCC 风险分层联系起来的报告。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 HBsAgGi 反应纳入现有 HCC 风险评分(例如 aMAP)可以完善 HBeAg 阳性患者的长期风险预测。它们还意味着 TAF 相对于 ETV 在通过改善 HBsAgGi 动力学降低 HCC 风险方面具有潜在优势。
局限与证据强度
C 基因型特异性检测限制了对其他 HBV 基因型的普遍适用性。HCC 风险比的宽置信区间需要谨慎解释,并且该研究适度的样本量和单中心设计限制了外部有效性。总体证据强度中等。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.

Authors: Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
Original abstract (English)
Nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy suppresses hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, but residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists. O-glycosylated hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) uniquely reflects virion burden. We evaluated the impact of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV) on 48-week HBsAgGi kinetics and its utility in stratifying HCC risk in 90 NA-naïve patients (ETV: 73; TAF: 17). A favourable response was defined as a reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030). A significant interaction occurred between HBeAg status and HBsAgGi response in relation to HCC development (p = 0.038). In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs. This association was absent in HBeAg-negative cases. Adding HBsAgGi response to the aMAP score improved long-term HCC prediction exclusively in the HBeAg-positive cohort. As the assay targets genotype C-specific O-glycosylation, the findings cannot be generalised to genotypes A, B, or D. In conclusion, on-treatment HBsAgGi response is a specific surrogate marker for stratifying residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients, and TAF is more effective than ETV in inducing these favourable kinetics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite nucleos(t)ide analog therapy suppressing HBV DNA, residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists, creating a need for better risk-stratification markers. The study asked whether on-treatment hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) kinetics could distinguish HCC risk and whether tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) outperforms entecavir (ETV) in modulating these kinetics.
Design and population
This was a prospective cohort study of 90 NA-naïve chronic hepatitis B patients initiating either TAF (n=17) or ETV (n=73) and followed for 48 weeks. The population comprised HBeAg-positive and HBeAg-negative adults receiving standard-of-care antiviral therapy.
Core methods
HBsAgGi was quantified using a genotype C-specific O-glycosylation assay; a favourable response was defined as reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. Logistic regression with penalised estimation evaluated predictors of favourable HBsAgGi kinetics, and Cox models assessed HCC risk associations.
Main results
TAF independently predicted a favourable HBsAgGi response versus ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p=0.030). In HBeAg-positive patients, poor HBsAgGi response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% CI 1.96–20.35, p=0.001), whereas no association was observed in HBeAg-negative patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret on-treatment HBsAgGi response as a specific surrogate marker for residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients and conclude that TAF induces more favourable HBsAgGi kinetics than ETV. This is the first report linking HBsAgGi kinetics to HCC risk stratification during NA therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating HBsAgGi response into existing HCC risk scores (e.g., aMAP) could refine long-term risk prediction in HBeAg-positive patients. They also imply a potential advantage of TAF over ETV in reducing HCC risk via improved HBsAgGi kinetics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The genotype C-specific assay limits generalisability to other HBV genotypes. The wide confidence intervals around the HCC hazard ratio warrant cautious interpretation, and the study’s modest sample size and single-centre design constrain external validity. Overall evidence strength is moderate.

通过综合单细胞分析揭示 慢性乙型肝炎 中 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞失调的 BCR 库和代谢状态。

Dysregulated BCR repertoire and metabolic state of HBsAg-specific B cells in chronic hepatitis B revealed by integrated single-cell analysis.
作者： Mengying He, Liangjun Shen, Yaming Li, Menghan Hao, Bing Wang, Yi Zhang, Min Wu, Zhan-qing Zhang, Zhong Fang, Zhenghong Yuan
摘要中文翻译背景:针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 的体液免疫对于病毒清除至关重要。然而,慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞的表型、功能和代谢特征仍未完全确定。方法:使用寡核苷酸标记的抗原探针富集 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞,并通过集成单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 和单细胞 B 细胞受体测序 (scBCR-seq) 对 HBeAg 阳性 CHB 患者、恢复期个体和接种疫苗的健康对照进行分析。独立的 队列研究 进一步评估了主要发现。生成重组单克隆抗体 (mAb) 用于功能验证,并进行离体测定以评估谷氨酰胺调节对 B 细胞反应的影响。结果:CHB 患者表现出未转换记忆 (USW) 和双阴性 1 (DN1) B 细胞亚群的扩张,其特征是免疫球蛋白类别转换受损、共刺激分子表达改变和克隆扩张增加。B 细胞受体 (BCR) 库分析揭示了特定同种型中免疫球蛋白重链可变 (IGHV) 基因使用的偏差和体细胞超突变的增加。单细胞代谢分析表明,CHB HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞中氧化磷酸化 (OXPHOS) 增加,谷氨酰胺代谢减少。重组单克隆抗体表现出异质性和构象依赖性抗原结合,中和活性有限。在离体试验中,补充谷氨酰胺可增强抗体的产生,并与 OXPHOS 特征的减少和干扰素信号传导的增加有关,在总免疫球蛋白分泌中观察到类似的效果。结论:CHB 中的 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞在亚群组成、BCR 库和代谢状态方面表现出协调变化。这些发现确定了 B 细胞失调的可重复特征,并表明免疫代谢失衡与体液反应受损之间存在潜在联系,为进一步的机制研究提供了框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管针对 慢性乙型肝炎 (HBV) 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 的体液免疫对于病毒清除非常重要,但 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然存在,但 CHB 中 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞的表型、库和代谢特征仍未完全确定。
设计与对象
该研究使用寡核苷酸标记的抗原探针以及集成的单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 和单细胞 B 细胞受体测序 (scBCR-seq) 对 HBeAg 阳性 CHB 患者、恢复期个体和接种疫苗的健康对照组的 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞进行了分析。
核心方法
通过 scRNA-seq 和 scBCR-seq 富集和分析 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞,生成重组单克隆抗体用于功能验证,并通过离体测定评估谷氨酰胺调节对 B 细胞反应的影响。
主要结果
CHB 患者表现出未转换记忆和双阴性 1 B 细胞亚群的扩张,并伴有类别转换受损、共刺激分子表达改变、克隆扩张增加、IGHV 基因使用偏向、体细胞超突变增加、氧化磷酸化增加以及 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞中谷氨酰胺代谢减少。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 CHB 中 HBsAg 特异性 B 细胞的子集组成、BCR 库和代谢状态的协调变化,识别了 B 细胞失调的可重复特征,并表明免疫代谢失衡与体液反应受损之间存在联系。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为调节 B 细胞代谢或增强 CHB 体液免疫的策略提供信息,从而可能改善 疫苗 反应或治疗性抗体的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点是 HBeAg 阳性慢性乙型肝炎患者,并且需要进一步进行机制研究以确定免疫代谢失衡与体液反应受损之间的因果关系,因此需要中等可信度。

Dysregulated BCR repertoire and metabolic state of HBsAg-specific B cells in chronic hepatitis B revealed by integrated single-cell analysis.

Authors: Mengying He, Liangjun Shen, Yaming Li, Menghan Hao, Bing Wang, Yi Zhang, Min Wu, Zhan-qing Zhang, Zhong Fang, Zhenghong Yuan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Humoral immunity targeting hepatitis B virus (HBV) surface antigen (HBsAg) is critical for viral clearance. However, the phenotypic, repertoire, and metabolic features of HBsAg-specific B cells in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remain incompletely defined. Methods: HBsAg-specific B cells were enriched using oligonucleotide-tagged antigen probes and profiled by integrated single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) and single-cell B cell receptor sequencing (scBCR-seq) in HBeAg-positive CHB patients, convalescent individuals, and vaccinated healthy controls. Key findings were further evaluated in an independent cohort. Recombinant monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) were generated for functional validation, and ex vivo assays were performed to assess the effects of glutamine modulation on B cell responses. Results: CHB patients exhibited expansion of unswitched memory (USW) and double-negative 1 (DN1) B cell subsets, characterized by impaired immunoglobulin class switching, altered co-stimulatory molecule expression, and increased clonal expansion. B cell receptor (BCR) repertoire analysis revealed biased immunoglobulin heavy chain variable (IGHV) gene usage and elevated somatic hypermutation in specific isotypes. Single-cell metabolic analysis demonstrated increased oxidative phosphorylation (OXPHOS) and reduced glutamine metabolism in CHB HBsAg-specific B cells. Recombinant mAbs showed heterogeneous and conformation-dependent antigen binding, with limited neutralizing activity. In ex vivo assays, glutamine supplementation enhanced antibody production and was associated with reduced OXPHOS signatures and increased interferon signaling, with similar effects observed in total immunoglobulin secretion. Conclusions: HBsAg-specific B cells in CHB display coordinated alterations in subset composition, BCR repertoire, and metabolic state. These findings identify reproducible features of B-cell dysregulation and suggest a potential link between immunometabolic imbalance and impaired humoral responses, providing a framework for further mechanistic investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) persists despite the importance of humoral immunity targeting hepatitis B virus (HBV) surface antigen (HBsAg) for viral clearance, yet the phenotypic, repertoire, and metabolic features of HBsAg-specific B cells in CHB remain incompletely defined.
Design and population
The study profiled HBsAg-specific B cells using oligonucleotide-tagged antigen probes and integrated single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) and single-cell B cell receptor sequencing (scBCR-seq) in HBeAg-positive CHB patients, convalescent individuals, and vaccinated healthy controls.
Core methods
HBsAg-specific B cells were enriched and analyzed by scRNA-seq and scBCR-seq, recombinant monoclonal antibodies were generated for functional validation, and ex vivo assays assessed the effects of glutamine modulation on B cell responses.
Main results
CHB patients showed expansion of unswitched memory and double-negative 1 B cell subsets with impaired class switching, altered co-stimulatory molecule expression, increased clonal expansion, biased IGHV gene usage, elevated somatic hypermutation, increased oxidative phosphorylation, and reduced glutamine metabolism in HBsAg-specific B cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as coordinated alterations in subset composition, BCR repertoire, and metabolic state of HBsAg-specific B cells in CHB, identifying reproducible features of B-cell dysregulation and suggesting a link between immunometabolic imbalance and impaired humoral responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform strategies to modulate B-cell metabolism or repertoire to enhance humoral immunity in CHB, potentially improving vaccine responses or therapeutic antibody development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on HBeAg-positive CHB patients and the need for further mechanistic investigation to establish causality between immunometabolic imbalance and impaired humoral responses, warranting moderate confidence.

2011 年至 2021 年英国丁型肝炎病毒特征

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021
作者： Ruth Simmons, S. Ijaz, R. Jackson, Kathryn Jack, R. Mulongeni, R. Raghu, A. Riddell, Ahmed M. Elsharkawy, A. Uriel, A. Koffas
摘要中文翻译摘要 在英国,人们对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 双重感染知之甚少。建议对所有 HBV 感染者进行 HDV 筛查,因为合并感染会导致疾病进展加快。利用英国 11 个报告进行 HDV 检测的实验室共享的 HDV 检测数据以及全国 HBV 检测数据,我们建立了 HDV 检测个体的 队列研究,调查检测途径,描述 HDV-RNA 阳性的特征和结果,为检测、诊断 和护理联系的针对性干预措施提供信息。人口统计信息、临床评估、治疗 和 NHS 信托机构的最新实验室结果,并通过链接到国家医疗数据集收集 HDV-RNA 阳性个体。2011 年至 2021 年间,英格兰新诊断出的 HBV 患者中有 42% 与 HDV 检测有关。抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性。在没有抗 HDV 结果的情况下,HDV-RNA 阳性率为 6.4%。在 HDV-RNA 阳性的个体中,94.3% 为非英国出生,报告有 24 种不同的主要语言,49% 有 治疗 证据,48% 有肝硬化证据。10%的人死于肝病,其中58.4%死于肝病。总之,我们的研究结果表明,在英国,针对 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测相对于检测指南(包括抗 HDV 阳性者的 RNA 检测)来说并不是最佳的。需要做大量的工作来改善患者护理途径,以确保患者得到适当的诊断、联系和保留护理,并有足够的机会获得 治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了英国对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 双重感染缺乏了解的问题,突显了对 HBV 感染者中 HDV 的负担和结果了解方面的差距。
设计与对象
使用来自 11 个英国实验室的 HDV 检测数据和国家 HBV 检测数据进行了一项全国回顾性 队列研究 研究,以建立 2011 年至 2021 年间接受 HDV 检测的个体的 队列研究。
核心方法
人口统计、临床、治疗 和实验室数据是从 NHS 信托机构收集的,并与 HDV-RNA 阳性个体的国家医疗保健数据集相关联,以描述检测途径和结果。
主要结果
在英格兰新诊断出的 HBV 患者中,42% 的人接受过 HDV 检测,抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 的人曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性,其中 6.4% 在没有抗 HDV 结果的情况下 HDV-RNA 呈阳性。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,与指南相比,英国 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测效果欠佳,包括抗 HDV 阳性个体的 RNA 检测,这表明需要改进诊断途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来改善丁型肝炎病毒检测、诊断,以及与英国 HBV 感染者的护理联系起来,以减少疾病进展和 诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于有限数量的实验室的回顾性数据,缺乏有关测试适应症的详细信息,这可能会引入选择偏差并限制普遍性,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021

Authors: Ruth Simmons, S. Ijaz, R. Jackson, Kathryn Jack, R. Mulongeni, R. Raghu, A. Riddell, Ahmed M. Elsharkawy, A. Uriel, A. Koffas
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Little is known about hepatitis B virus (HBV) and Hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection in the UK. HDV screening is recommended among all individuals with HBV, with coinfection associated with increased disease progression. Using data on HDV testing, shared by 11 laboratories in the UK who reported undertaking HDV testing, and national testing data for HBV we established a cohort of individuals tested for HDV, investigating testing pathways, describing characteristics and outcomes of those HDV‐RNA positive to inform targeted interventions for testing, diagnosis and linkage to care. Demographic information, clinical assessments, treatment and recent laboratory results from NHS trusts, and through linkage to national healthcare datasets was collected for HDV‐RNA positive individuals. Between 2011 and 2021, 42% of individuals newly diagnosed with HBV in England were linked to an HDV test. Anti‐HDV positivity was 5.0%, 55.8% were HDV‐RNA tested and 45.7% were ever HDV‐RNA positive. HDV‐RNA positivity in the absence of an anti‐HDV result was 6.4%. Among individuals who were HDV‐RNA positive, 94.3% were non‐UK born, with 24 different primary languages reported, 49% had evidence of treatment, and 48% had evidence of cirrhosis. 10% had died of which 58.4% died of liver disease. In conclusion, our findings indicate that HDV testing in HBV positive individuals in the UK is sub‐optimal against testing guidelines, including RNA testing among those who are anti‐HDV positive. Considerable work is needed to improve the patient care pathway, to ensure patients are appropriately diagnosed, linked and retained in care, with adequate access to treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the lack of knowledge about hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection in the UK, highlighting a gap in understanding the burden and outcomes of HDV among HBV-infected individuals.
Design and population
A national retrospective cohort study was conducted using HDV testing data from 11 UK laboratories and national HBV testing data to establish a cohort of individuals tested for HDV between 2011 and 2021.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, treatment, and laboratory data were collected from NHS trusts and linked to national healthcare datasets for HDV-RNA positive individuals to describe testing pathways and outcomes.
Main results
Among individuals newly diagnosed with HBV in England, 42% were linked to an HDV test, anti-HDV positivity was 5.0%, 55.8% were HDV-RNA tested, and 45.7% were ever HDV-RNA positive, with 6.4% HDV-RNA positive in the absence of an anti-HDV result.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that HDV testing in HBV-positive individuals in the UK is sub-optimal compared to guidelines, including RNA testing among anti-HDV positive individuals, indicating a need for improved diagnostic pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve HDV testing, diagnosis, and linkage to care among HBV-infected individuals in the UK to reduce disease progression and mortality.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on retrospective data from a limited number of laboratories and lacks detailed information on testing indications, which may introduce selection bias and limit generalisability, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

在赞比亚处理 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的一线医护人员中验证肝炎护理点超声检查。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.
作者： Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
摘要中文翻译为弥合资源有限环境中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染患者护理实施中的差距,我们评估了一种新型肝脏床旁超声(POCUS)肝炎筛查方案(PUSH)在赞比亚一线非放射科医护人员中的准确性。在2024年3月至6月期间,于赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,我们培训了4名护士和6名有HBV管理经验但无超声(US)经验的医生,采用PUSH方案,包括在三个窗口(上腹部、肋下和右侧肋间)观察肝脏,以识别提示肝硬化的特征及疑似肝细胞癌(HCC)的肝脏病变。随后,连续纳入仅患有乙型肝炎(PWHB)或HBV/HIV合并感染的成年参与者,进行PUSH检查,操作者对临床数据不知情。我们使用瞬时弹性成像(TE)作为参考标准(RST)评估PUSH对显著纤维化和肝硬化的准确性,并使用经验丰富放射技师进行的全面腹部超声作为可能HCC肝脏病变的RST。采用非参数法分析PUSH的受试者工作特征曲线(ROC),并按操作者类型(医生vs护士)、性别和HIV状态进行调整。在197名分析的PWHB中,69.5%正在接受HBV抗病毒药物治疗,27.9%为HBV/HIV合并感染。根据RST结果,17.7%的PWHB存在显著纤维化,11.6%存在肝硬化,3.5%存在肝脏病变。PUSH对显著纤维化的准确性较低,敏感性为8.3%(17.5-41.4),特异性为100.0%(96.1-100),曲线下面积(AUROC)为0.54(0.43-0.65);对肝硬化的准确性为中等,敏感性为60.9%(38.5-80.3),特异性为97.7%(94.2-99.4),AUROC为0.79(0.69-0.89);对肝脏病变的准确性较高,敏感性为85.7%(42.1-99.6),特异性为98.4%(95.5-99.7),AUROC为0.92(0.78-1.00)。在赞比亚,经过快速培训的前线护士和医生能够使用PUSH以中等准确性诊断肝硬化,以高准确性诊断肝脏病变,尽管性能估计的置信区间较宽。在缺乏实验室系统和TE的情况下,包含PUSH的肝脏POCUS可能有助于HBV管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于诊断访问障碍,慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染在资源有限的环境中仍然管理不善,这促使需要准确的护理点工具。该研究旨在评估新型肝脏即时超声 (POCUS) 方案 (PUSH) 是否能够准确识别赞比亚一线医护人员管理的慢性乙肝成人患者中的肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC)。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 6 月,在赞比亚卢萨卡的大学教学医院,四名具有 HBV 管理经验但没有接受过超声培训的护士和六名医生接受了 PUSH 方案的培训,该方案在三个窗口中可视化肝脏,以检测肝硬化提示特征和 HCC 可疑病变。连续患有慢性 HBV 或 HBV/HIV 双重感染的成年人在不了解临床数据的情况下接受了 PUSH。
核心方法
以瞬时弹性成像 (TE) 作为参考标准评估显着纤维化和肝硬化的 PUSH 准确性,同时由经验丰富的放射技师将肝脏病变与综合腹部超声进行比较。根据操作者类型、性别和 HIV 状态进行调整的非参数受试者工作曲线 (ROC) 分析用于评估诊断性能。
主要结果
根据参考标准,197 名参与者中,17.7% 患有明显纤维化,11.6% 患有肝硬化,3.5% 患有肝脏病变。PUSH 对显着纤维化的准确性较低(敏感性 8.3%,特异性 100.0%,AUROC 0.54),对肝硬化的准确性中等(敏感性 60.9%,特异性 97.7%,AUROC 0.79),对肝脏病变的准确性较高(敏感性 85.7%,特异性 98.4%,AUROC 0.92)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,经过短暂训练后,PUSH 可以以中等准确度诊断肝硬化,并以较高准确度诊断肝脏病变,这突显了其在实验室系统和 TE 不可用的情况下的潜在用途。他们强调了在资源有限的环境中验证非放射科医生执行的 POCUS 方案针对 HBV 相关并发症的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地表明,PUSH 可以支持分散的 HBV 护理,使一线提供者能够在无法获得高级诊断的情况下筛查肝硬化和 HCC,从而有可能改善赞比亚的早期检测和转诊。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,性能估计的置信区间较宽,限制了精度,而且单中心设计限制了普遍性。尽管存在这些局限性,但由于明确的目标特异性方法和临床相关结果,证据是温和的。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.

Authors: Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
Original abstract (English)
To close gaps in implementing care of people with hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in resource-limited settings, we evaluated the accuracy of a novel liver point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol for hepatitis (PUSH) among front-line non-radiology healthcare workers in Zambia. At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, we trained four nurses and six physicians with experience in HBV management, but not in ultrasound (US), in the PUSH protocol, which includes visualizing the liver in three windows (epigastric, subcostal and right transcostal) to identify cirrhosis-suggestive features and liver lesions suspicious for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Then, consecutive adult participants with chronic hepatitis B (PWHB) alone or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data. We evaluated the accuracy of PUSH for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis using transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard test (RST) and for liver lesions that were possible HCC using a comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer as the RST. Nonparametric analysis of the receiver operating curve (ROC) for PUSH was adjusted for operator type (doctor vs. nurse), sex and HIV status. Among 197 PWHBs analysed, 69.5% were taking HBV antivirals and 27.9% had HBV/HIV. According to RSTs, 17.7% of PWHBs had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis and 3.5% had liver lesions. PUSH had low accuracy for significant fibrosis, with sensitivity of 8.3% (17.5-41.4), specificity of 100.0% (96.1-100) and an area under the curve (AUROC) of 0.54 (0.43-0.65), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis, with sensitivity of 60.9% (38.5-80.3), specificity of 97.7% (94.2-99.4) and AUROC of 0.79 (0.69-0.89), and higher accuracy for liver lesions, with 85.7% (42.1-99.6) sensitivity, 98.4% (95.5-99.7) specificity, AUROC of 0.92 (0.78-1.00). After an expedited training, front-line nurses and physicians treating HBV in Zambia were able to use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, although confidence intervals around performance estimates were wide. Liver POCUS including PUSH may be useful in HBV management when laboratory systems and TE are lacking.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains under-managed in resource-limited settings due to diagnostic access barriers, prompting the need for accurate point-of-care tools. The study aimed to evaluate whether a novel liver point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol (PUSH) could accurately identify cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) among adults with chronic HBV managed by front-line healthcare workers in Zambia.
Design and population
At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, four nurses and six physicians with HBV management experience but no ultrasound training were trained in the PUSH protocol, which visualizes the liver in three windows to detect cirrhosis-suggestive features and HCC-suspicious lesions. Consecutive adults with chronic HBV or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data.
Core methods
PUSH accuracy for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis was assessed against transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard, while liver lesions were compared to comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer. Nonparametric receiver operating curve (ROC) analysis adjusted for operator type, sex, and HIV status was used to evaluate diagnostic performance.
Main results
Among 197 participants, 17.7% had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis, and 3.5% had liver lesions by reference standards. PUSH showed low accuracy for significant fibrosis (sensitivity 8.3%, specificity 100.0%, AUROC 0.54), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis (sensitivity 60.9%, specificity 97.7%, AUROC 0.79), and high accuracy for liver lesions (sensitivity 85.7%, specificity 98.4%, AUROC 0.92).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that PUSH can diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy after brief training, highlighting its potential utility where laboratory systems and TE are unavailable. They emphasized the novelty of validating a non-radiologist-performed POCUS protocol for HBV-related complications in a resource-limited setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings cautiously suggest that PUSH could support decentralized HBV care by enabling front-line providers to screen for cirrhosis and HCC when advanced diagnostics are inaccessible, potentially improving early detection and referral in Zambia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size and wide confidence intervals around performance estimates limit precision, and the single-centre design restricts generalizability. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate due to the clear target-specific methodology and clinically relevant outcomes.

Vorapaxar 和阿立哌唑通过不同的 宿主 信号通路抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 复制

Vorapaxar and aripiprazole suppress hepatitis B virus replication through distinct host signaling pathways
作者： Atsuya Yamashita, Hirotake Kasai, Haruyo Aoyagi, Kousho Wakae, Kaoru Kobayashi, Atsushi Miyajima, Yuichiro Higuchi, Hiroshi Suemizu, Ryo Fukushima, Masanori Isogawa
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景与目的 目前的核苷类似物可有效抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 复制,但对共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 和整合的 HBV DNA 的病毒转录影响有限。我们的目的是通过筛选 FDA 批准的药物来鉴定直接抑制 HBV 转录的临床适用化合物。方法和结果 使用 HBV 增强子 I/X 启动子报告系统对 1,470 种 FDA 批准的化合物进行筛选,确定沃拉帕沙和阿立哌唑是病毒启动子活性的有效抑制剂。这两种化合物均可抑制 HBV 产生细胞、HBV 感染的 HepG2-hNTCP 细胞和原代人肝细胞中的 HBV 复制。阿立哌唑通过 ERK/JNK 依赖性途径降低肝细胞核因子 4α (HNF4α) 蛋白水平并抑制 HBV 核心启动子活性,而沃拉帕沙的作用独立于 HNF4α。两种化合物均通过抑制 STAT3 信号传导来抑制增强子 I/X 启动子活性。Vorapaxar 抑制 PAR-1 介导的 SRC、EGFR 和 STAT3 激活,而阿立哌唑则独立于 EGFR 抑制 SRC-STAT3 信号传导。PAR-1 激活增强了 HBV 转录,而 PAR-1 敲低则降低了启动子活性和病毒 RNA 表达。这两种化合物还在临床相关暴露水平下减少了人肝嵌合小鼠中的 HBV 复制,且没有明显的严重毒性。结论 Vorapaxar 和阿立哌唑通过不同的 宿主 信号通路抑制 HBV 转录和复制。这些发现将 PAR-1–STAT3 信号转导确定为以前未被识别的 HBV 转录调节因子,并表明 宿主 靶向方法可以通过抑制 共价闭合环状DNA 和整合的 HBV DNA 的病毒基因表达来补充当前的治疗方法。影响和意义 目前的核苷(酸)类似物可有效抑制 HBV 逆转录,但对 共价闭合环状DNA 和整合的 HBV DNA 的病毒转录影响有限,这凸显了针对病毒基因表达的治疗的需要。我们将 PAR-1-STAT3 信号传导确定为以前未被识别的 HBV 转录调节因子,并证明两种临床批准的药物沃拉帕沙和阿立哌唑通过不同的 宿主 信号传导途径抑制 HBV 复制。这些发现与开发 宿主 靶向 抗病毒药物 的研究人员以及寻求当前核苷(酸)类似物治疗之外的补充治疗策略的临床医生相关。尽管还需要进一步的临床验证和联合研究,但我们的结果为重新利用已批准的药物和开发可能补充 慢性乙型肝炎 现有 治疗 的转录靶向疗法提供了理论基础。亮点 Vorapaxar 和阿立哌唑通过不同的 宿主 途径抑制 HBV。这两种药物均能在体外和人源化肝小鼠体内抑制 HBV 复制。PAR-1 抑制通过阻断 SRC/EGFR/STAT3 信号传导来减少 HBV 转录。PAR-1–STAT3 信号传导是 HBV 转录的新型调节因子。宿主 靶向 抗病毒药物 疗法补充了当前的 HBV 治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前的核苷类似物可有效抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 复制,但对共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 和整合的 HBV DNA 的病毒转录影响有限。本研究旨在通过筛选 FDA 批准的药物来鉴定直接抑制 HBV 转录的临床适用化合物。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 HBV 增强子 I/X 启动子报告系统筛选 1,470 种 FDA 批准的化合物,以确定病毒启动子活性的有效抑制剂。沃拉帕沙和阿立哌唑均被发现可抑制 HBV 产生细胞、HBV 感染的 HepG2-hNTCP 细胞和原代人肝细胞中的 HBV 复制。
核心方法
使用 HBV 增强子 I/X 启动子报告系统筛选 1,470 种 FDA 批准的化合物。该研究还包括体外实验和人肝嵌合小鼠测试,以评估沃拉帕沙和阿立哌唑在临床相关暴露水平下对乙型肝炎病毒复制的影响。
主要结果
他们还在临床相关暴露水平下减少了人肝嵌合小鼠中的 HBV 复制,且没有明显的严重毒性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,PAR-1-STAT3 信号传导是 HBV 转录的新型调节因子,他们证明了两种临床批准的药物,沃拉帕沙和阿立哌唑,通过不同的 宿主 信号传导途径抑制 HBV 复制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现与开发 宿主 靶向 抗病毒药物 的研究人员以及寻求当前核苷(酸)类似物治疗之外的补充治疗策略的临床医生相关。他们认为,宿主 靶向 抗病毒药物 疗法可能会补充 慢性乙型肝炎 的现有 治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和人源化肝小鼠实验,还需要进一步的临床验证和联合研究。证据是温和的,因为它基于仅抽象的数据。

Vorapaxar and aripiprazole suppress hepatitis B virus replication through distinct host signaling pathways

Authors: Atsuya Yamashita, Hirotake Kasai, Haruyo Aoyagi, Kousho Wakae, Kaoru Kobayashi, Atsushi Miyajima, Yuichiro Higuchi, Hiroshi Suemizu, Ryo Fukushima, Masanori Isogawa
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background & Aims Current nucleos(t)ide analogs efficiently suppress hepatitis B virus (HBV) replication but have limited effects on viral transcription from covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) and integrated HBV DNA. We aimed to identify clinically applicable compounds that directly inhibit HBV transcription by screening FDA-approved drugs. Approach & Results Screening of 1,470 FDA-approved compounds using an HBV enhancer I/X promoter reporter system identified vorapaxar and aripiprazole as potent inhibitors of viral promoter activity. Both compounds suppressed HBV replication in HBV-producing cells, HBV-infected HepG2-hNTCP cells, and primary human hepatocytes. Aripiprazole reduced hepatocyte nuclear factor 4α (HNF4α) protein levels through an ERK/JNK-dependent pathway and inhibited HBV core promoter activity, whereas vorapaxar acted independently of HNF4α. Both compounds suppressed enhancer I/X promoter activity through inhibition of STAT3 signaling. Vorapaxar inhibited PAR-1-mediated SRC, EGFR, and STAT3 activation, while aripiprazole suppressed SRC–STAT3 signaling independently of EGFR. PAR-1 activation enhanced HBV transcription, whereas PAR-1 knockdown reduced promoter activity and viral RNA expression. Both compounds also reduced HBV replication in human liver chimeric mice at clinically relevant exposure levels without apparent severe toxicity. Conclusions Vorapaxar and aripiprazole suppress HBV transcription and replication through distinct host signaling pathways. These findings identify PAR-1–STAT3 signaling as a previously unrecognized regulator of HBV transcription and suggest that host-targeting approaches may complement current therapies by suppressing viral gene expression from both cccDNA and integrated HBV DNA. Impact and implications Current nucleos(t)ide analogues effectively suppress HBV reverse transcription but have limited effects on viral transcription from cccDNA and integrated HBV DNA, highlighting the need for therapies targeting viral gene expression. We identify PAR-1– STAT3 signaling as a previously unrecognized regulator of HBV transcription and demonstrate that two clinically approved drugs, vorapaxar and aripiprazole, suppress HBV replication through distinct host signaling pathways. These findings are relevant to researchers developing host-targeting antivirals and to clinicians seeking complementary therapeutic strategies beyond current nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy. Although further clinical validation and combination studies are required, our results provide a rationale for repurposing approved drugs and for developing transcription-targeting therapies that may complement existing treatments for chronic hepatitis B. Highlights Vorapaxar and aripiprazole suppress HBV through distinct host pathways. Both drugs inhibit HBV replication in vitro and in humanized liver mice. PAR-1 inhibition reduces HBV transcription by blocking SRC/EGFR/STAT3 signaling. PAR-1–STAT3 signaling is a novel regulator of HBV transcription. Host-targeting antiviral therapy complements current HBV treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current nucleos(t)ide analogs efficiently suppress hepatitis B virus (HBV) replication but have limited effects on viral transcription from covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) and integrated HBV DNA. This study aimed to identify clinically applicable compounds that directly inhibit HBV transcription by screening FDA-approved drugs.
Design and population
The study involved screening 1,470 FDA-approved compounds using an HBV enhancer I/X promoter reporter system to identify potent inhibitors of viral promoter activity. Both vorapaxar and aripiprazole were found to suppress HBV replication in HBV-producing cells, HBV-infected HepG2-hNTCP cells, and primary human hepatocytes.
Core methods
An HBV enhancer I/X promoter reporter system was used to screen 1,470 FDA-approved compounds. The study also included in vitro experiments and testing in human liver chimeric mice to evaluate the effects of vorapaxar and aripiprazole on HBV replication at clinically relevant exposure levels.
Main results
They also reduced HBV replication in human liver chimeric mice at clinically relevant exposure levels without apparent severe toxicity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that PAR-1–STAT3 signaling is a novel regulator of HBV transcription, and they demonstrate that two clinically approved drugs, vorapaxar and aripiprazole, suppress HBV replication through distinct host signaling pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings are relevant to researchers developing host-targeting antivirals and to clinicians seeking complementary therapeutic strategies beyond current nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy. They suggest that host-targeting antiviral therapy may complement existing treatments for chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in humanized liver mice experiments, and further clinical validation and combination studies are required. The evidence is moderate as it is based on abstract-only data.

乙型肝炎 疫苗 在泰国北部山区部落和无国籍儿童中诱导免疫力

Hepatitis B vaccine-induced immunity among hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand
作者： Tawatchai Apidechkul, Manassawin Kampun, Buathanya Srikua, Fartima Yeemard, Watchareewan Apidechkul, Chalitar Chomchoei, Panupong Upala, Ratipark Tamornpark
摘要中文翻译本研究的目的是确定泰国北部完全和部分接种疫苗的山地部落和五岁无国籍儿童之间血清保护的差异,以及自 1992 年开始实施 疫苗 以来这些儿童缺乏抗 HBs 的相关因素。进行了一项病例对照研究,收集居住在清莱府 Mae Fah Luang 区村庄的山地部落和无国籍个人的信息。未事先免疫、延迟免疫或不完全乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的儿童被纳入其中。完全接种疫苗的儿童被纳入对照。发放调查问卷并采集3 mL血样,用于乙型肝炎生物标志物的定性和定量检测。应用逻辑回归来检测与抗-HBs缺乏相关的因素。研究共纳入188例病例和188例对照;54%为男性,54.3%属于阿卡族。抗-HBs总体患病率为59.3%;完全型疫苗接种患儿(其中2例抗-HBc阳性)中抗-HBs检出率为55.3%,不完全型疫苗接种患儿中检出率为55.3%。出生体重<2500 g(AOR = 3.52;95% CI = 1.39–9.12)、母亲属于瑶族(AOR = 16.90;95% CI = 2.00–142.57)、母亲信奉基督教(AOR = 2.30;95% CI = 1.18–4.46)、父亲高龄的儿童30-39 岁(AOR = 2.20;95% CI = 1.01-4.80)更有可能表现出抗-HBs 缺失。山区部落和无国籍儿童中乙型肝炎 疫苗 引起的 免疫应答 率仍然不理想,需要有效的评估计划来监测这一问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了泰国北部边缘化山区部落和无国籍儿童因乙型肝炎 疫苗 引起的免疫力欠佳的公共卫生问题,疫苗接种 项目于 1992 年开始在泰国北部开展。该研究旨在量化完全和部分接种疫苗的儿童之间的血清保护差异,并确定与缺乏抗 HBs 抗体相关的因素。
设计与对象
在清莱府 Mae Fah Luang 区的村庄进行了一项病例对照研究,招募了山地部落和五岁的无国籍儿童。病例是既往未接种、延迟接种或不完全接种乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的儿童,而对照是完全接种疫苗的儿童。
核心方法
进行问卷调查并采集 3 mL 血样用于乙型肝炎生物标志物的定性和定量检测。使用逻辑回归来检测与抗-HBs 缺失相关的因素。
主要结果
总体抗-HBs 患病率 为59.3%,在完全接种和不完全接种的儿童中检出率为55.3%(完全接种的儿童中有2 名抗-HBc 阳性)。
解释与创新
作者将低血清保护率解释为不良现象,并强调孕妇的瑶族血统和低出生体重是抗 HBs 缺失的有力预测因素。他们的结论是,需要一个有效的评估计划来监测 疫苗 在这些人群中诱导的免疫力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有必要对泰国北部的山区部落和无国籍儿童进行有针对性的 疫苗接种 计划和 疫苗接种 后血清监测,以提高乙型肝炎免疫力并降低 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能低估了真正的血清保护差距。由于潜在的选择偏差和未测量的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Hepatitis B vaccine-induced immunity among hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand

Authors: Tawatchai Apidechkul, Manassawin Kampun, Buathanya Srikua, Fartima Yeemard, Watchareewan Apidechkul, Chalitar Chomchoei, Panupong Upala, Ratipark Tamornpark
Original abstract (English)
The aim of this study was to determine the difference in seroprotection between fully and partially vaccinated hill tribe and stateless children aged five years in northern Thailand along with the factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs in these children since vaccine implementation began in 1992. A case–control study was conducted to collect information from hill tribe and stateless individuals living in villages in Mae Fah Luang District, Chiang Rai Province. Children with no prior immunization, delayed immunization, or incomplete hepatitis B vaccination were included. Children who were completely vaccinated were included as controls. A questionnaire was distributed and 3 mL blood samples were collected for the qualitative and quantitative detection of hepatitis B biomarkers. Logistic regression was applied to detect factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs. A total of 188 cases and 188 controls were included in the study; 54% were male, and 54.3% belonged to the Akha group. The overall prevalence of anti-HBs was 59.3%; 55.3% of anti-HBs was detected in children with complete vaccination (with 2 children positive for anti-HBc), and 55.3% was detected in children with incomplete vaccination. Children whose birth weight was <2500 g (AOR = 3.52; 95% CI = 1.39–9.12), whose mother belonged to the Yao group (AOR = 16.90; 95% CI = 2.00–142.57), whose mother practiced Christianity (AOR = 2.30; 95% CI = 1.18–4.46), and whose father was aged 30–39 years (AOR = 2.20; 95% CI = 1.01–4.80) were more likely to exhibit an absence of anti-HBs. The hepatitis B vaccine-induced immune response rate among hill tribe and stateless children remains undesirable, and an effective assessment program is needed to monitor this issue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of suboptimal hepatitis B vaccine-induced immunity in marginalized hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand, where vaccination began in 1992. It aimed to quantify seroprotection differences between fully and partially vaccinated children and to identify factors linked to the absence of anti-HBs antibodies.
Design and population
A case–control study was conducted in villages of Mae Fah Luang District, Chiang Rai Province, enrolling hill tribe and stateless children aged five years. Cases were children with no prior, delayed, or incomplete hepatitis B vaccination, while controls were those who were completely vaccinated.
Core methods
A questionnaire was administered and 3 mL blood samples were collected for qualitative and quantitative detection of hepatitis B biomarkers. Logistic regression was used to detect factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs.
Main results
The overall anti-HBs prevalence was 59.3%, with 55.3% detected in both fully vaccinated and incompletely vaccinated children (2 children positive for anti-HBc among the fully vaccinated).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the low seroprotection rate as undesirable and highlighted maternal Yao ethnicity and low birth weight as strong predictors of anti-HBs absence. They concluded that an effective assessment program is needed to monitor vaccine-induced immunity in these populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that targeted vaccination programs and post-vaccination serosurveillance are warranted for hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand to improve hepatitis B immunity and reduce transmission risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported vaccination status limit causal inference and may underestimate true seroprotection gaps. The evidence is moderate due to potential selection bias and unmeasured confounders.

2016-2025 年中国重庆及时乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗接种 的空间和时间差异

Spatial and Temporal Disparities in Timely Hepatitis B Birth-Dose Vaccination in Chongqing, China, 2016–2025
作者： Binyue Xu, Ningyu Wan, Qing Wang, Ningpei Bai, Chunbei Zhou
摘要中文翻译背景:及时乙型肝炎 疫苗 出生剂量 (HepBV-BD) 疫苗接种 是预防 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 母婴 传播 的基石策略。尽管中国的乙型肝炎覆盖率已大幅提高,但关于及时出生剂量 疫苗接种 的长期地区级时空不平等的证据仍然有限,特别是在中国西部。本研究评估了 2016 年至 2025 年中国重庆市乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率的时空差异。方法:这项生态描述性研究使用了重庆市免疫信息管理系统的区县级 疫苗接种 监测 数据。分析了 HepBV-BD、及时出生剂量 (TBD)、HepBV2 和 HepBV3 覆盖率的时间趋势。使用全球 Moran’s I 和地方空间关联指标 (LISA) 评估区域差异、区域间不平等和空间聚集。结果:研究期间所有疫苗接种指标均大幅改善。TBD覆盖率从81.9%增加到95.4%,而HepBV3覆盖率从83.7%增加到98.7%。TBD 覆盖率与 HepBV3 覆盖率呈强正相关(r = 0.95,p < 0.001)。尽管随着时间的推移取得了显着改善,但山区少数民族地区的 TBD 覆盖率始终最低。区域间绝对不平等从 30.8 个百分点下降到 8.4 个百分点。尽管 Global Moran's I 没有统计显着性,但 LISA 分析发现东南山区持续存在局部低覆盖度集群,而重庆北部则出现了新兴的高-高集群。结论:2016 年至 2025 年间,重庆市乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率显着提高,同时地理不平等现象也有所下降。然而,在山区少数民族地区,及时出生剂量疫苗接种仍然存在持续的空间不平等。持续监测空间不平等,加上加强初级卫生保健能力、针对文化的健康教育和公平的资源分配,可能有助于支持有针对性的地理免疫战略,并加快实现世卫组织到 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎这一公共卫生威胁的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
及时乙型肝炎 疫苗 出生剂量 (HepBV-BD) 疫苗接种 对于预防 HBV 母婴 传播 至关重要,但及时出生剂量 疫苗接种 的长期地区级时空不平等仍然不够充分,特别是在中国西部。
设计与对象
本生态描述性研究分析了重庆市免疫信息管理系统2016年至2025年期间的区县疫苗接种 监测数据。
核心方法
评估了 HepBV-BD、及时出生剂量 (TBD)、HepBV2 和 HepBV3 覆盖率的时间趋势,并使用全球 Moran’s I 和局部空间关联指标 (LISA) 评估了区域差异、区域间不平等和空间聚类。
主要结果
研究期间所有疫苗接种指标均大幅改善,TBD覆盖率从81.9%上升至95.4%,HepBV3覆盖率从83.7%上升至98.7%。TBD 覆盖率与 HepBV3 覆盖率呈强正相关(r = 0.95,p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
研究发现,重庆市乙型肝炎覆盖率整体显着改善,地理不平等有所下降,但山区少数民族地区仍然存在持续的空间不平等,凸显了制定有针对性的地理免疫策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持继续监测空间不平等和有针对性的干预措施,例如加强初级保健能力、针对文化的健康教育和公平的资源分配,以加快实现世卫组织到 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎这一公共卫生威胁的目标。
局限与证据强度
生态设计和对 监测 数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能掩盖个体层面的决定因素,而缺乏统计显着性的 Global Moran's I 表明,尽管已识别出局部集群,但全局空间自相关性较弱。

Spatial and Temporal Disparities in Timely Hepatitis B Birth-Dose Vaccination in Chongqing, China, 2016–2025

Authors: Binyue Xu, Ningyu Wan, Qing Wang, Ningpei Bai, Chunbei Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Background: Timely hepatitis B vaccine birth-dose (HepBV-BD) vaccination is a cornerstone strategy for preventing mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus (HBV). Although hepatitis B vaccination coverage in China has improved substantially, evidence regarding long-term district-level spatiotemporal inequalities in timely birth-dose vaccination remains limited, particularly in western China. This study assessed spatiotemporal disparities in hepatitis B vaccination coverage across Chongqing, China, from 2016 to 2025. Methods: This ecological descriptive study used district- and county-level vaccination surveillance data from the Chongqing Immunization Information Management System. Temporal trends in HepBV-BD, timely birth-dose (TBD), HepBV2, and HepBV3 coverage were analyzed. Regional disparities, interregional inequalities, and spatial clustering were assessed using Global Moran’s I and Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA). Results: All vaccination indicators improved substantially during the study period. TBD coverage increased from 81.9% to 95.4%, whereas HepBV3 coverage increased from 83.7% to 98.7%. TBD coverage was strongly positively associated with HepBV3 coverage(r = 0.95, p < 0.001). Mountainous ethnic minority regions consistently exhibited the lowest TBD coverage despite marked improvement over time. Absolute interregional inequality decreased from 30.8 to 8.4 percentage points. Although Global Moran’s I was not statistically significant, LISA analysis identified persistent localized low-coverage clusters in southeastern mountainous regions and emerging high–high clusters in northern Chongqing. Conclusions: Hepatitis B vaccination coverage in Chongqing improved markedly between 2016 and 2025, accompanied by declining geographic inequality. Nevertheless, persistent spatial inequities in timely birth-dose vaccination remained in mountainous ethnic minority areas. Continued monitoring of spatial inequalities, together with strengthened primary healthcare capacity, culturally tailored health education, and equitable resource allocation, may help support geographically targeted immunization strategies and accelerate progress toward the WHO goal of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat by 2030.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Timely hepatitis B vaccine birth-dose (HepBV-BD) vaccination is critical for preventing mother-to-child transmission of HBV, yet long-term district-level spatiotemporal inequalities in timely birth-dose vaccination remain insufficiently characterized, particularly in western China.
Design and population
This ecological descriptive study analyzed district- and county-level vaccination surveillance data from the Chongqing Immunization Information Management System covering the period 2016 to 2025.
Core methods
Temporal trends in HepBV-BD, timely birth-dose (TBD), HepBV2, and HepBV3 coverage were assessed, and regional disparities, interregional inequalities, and spatial clustering were evaluated using Global Moran’s I and Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA).
Main results
All vaccination indicators improved substantially during the study period, with TBD coverage increasing from 81.9% to 95.4% and HepBV3 coverage rising from 83.7% to 98.7%. TBD coverage was strongly positively associated with HepBV3 coverage (r = 0.95, p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found marked overall improvement in hepatitis B vaccination coverage in Chongqing alongside declining geographic inequality, but persistent spatial inequities remained in mountainous ethnic minority areas, highlighting the need for geographically targeted immunization strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support continued monitoring of spatial inequalities and targeted interventions such as strengthening primary healthcare capacity, culturally tailored health education, and equitable resource allocation to accelerate progress toward WHO’s goal of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat by 2030.
Limitations and evidence strength
The ecological design and reliance on surveillance data limit causal inference and may obscure individual-level determinants, while the absence of statistically significant Global Moran’s I suggests weak global spatial autocorrelation despite identified localized clusters.

妊娠 17 周时的活体肝移植治疗 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的急性肝衰竭伴肝性脑病,并在 34 周时紧急剖宫产:病例报告

Living-donor liver transplantation at 17 weeks gestation for hepatitis B virus-related acute liver failure with hepatic encephalopathy with emergency cesarean delivery at 34 weeks: a case report
作者： H. Ishimatsu, E. Imai, W. Matsunaga, H. Fujita, K. Yoshinaga, M. Takeuchi, Winthrop Al
摘要中文翻译怀孕期间的肝移植很少见,但在胎儿存活之前发生急性肝衰竭的情况下可能可以挽救生命。一名感染 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 的初产妇在妊娠 17 周时出现 HBV 相关急性肝功能衰竭并伴有 III 级肝性脑病。由于分娩无法提高胎儿存活率,多学科团队在妊娠继续期间优先考虑移植。实验室检查显示严重的血小板减少症和低纤维蛋白原血症。麻醉管理以预先确定的母体生理和血液学目标为指导,以支持子宫胎盘灌注,包括临时门体分流。分期胎儿超声检查证实了其生存能力。手术持续10小时55分钟,失血2,410毫升。母亲康复了。早产胎膜早破、臀位促使我们在 34 周时进行紧急剖腹产,并使用脊髓麻醉,随后接受新生儿重症监护。该病例表明,当分娩无法挽救胎儿时,可以通过多学科决策和专业化围手术期管理,将紧急移植和继续妊娠结合起来。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了妊娠期间因 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染而导致的急性肝功能衰竭的临床挑战,其中胎儿存活率妨碍分娩作为一种救援策略。它探讨了是否可以在维持妊娠的同时进行活体肝移植,以优化母体和胎儿的结局。
设计与对象
这是一位患有慢性 HBV 感染的初产妇的单 病例报告,她在妊娠 17 周时出现了 HBV 相关的急性肝功能衰竭并伴有肝性脑病。该病例需要多学科治疗,包括在继续妊娠期间进行活体肝移植,然后在 34 周时进行紧急剖腹产。
核心方法
麻醉和手术管理涉及预先确定的母体生理和血液学目标,以支持子宫胎盘灌注,包括临时门体分流。通过移植优先考虑母体的生存能力,并在整个围手术期通过分阶段的胎儿超声检查来确认母体的生存能力。
主要结果
实验室检查显示严重的血小板减少症和低纤维蛋白原血症。手术持续了 10 小时 55 分钟,预计失血量为 2,410 毫升。母亲已完全康复,34周时紧急剖腹产后,由于胎膜早破和臀位,新生儿需要新生儿重症监护。
解释与创新
作者将此案例解读为,当分娩无法挽救胎儿时,通过专门的多学科和围手术期管理,紧急肝移植联合继续妊娠是可行的。新颖之处在于在怀孕期间进行移植的综合方法,以稳定母体与乙型肝炎相关的急性肝衰竭,同时保留胎儿的活力。
科研与公卫意义
该病例凸显了妊娠期肝移植治疗乙型肝炎相关急性肝功能衰竭的潜力,可以在分娩不可行的情况下提高孕产妇生存率,同时又不影响胎儿结局。它强调需要专门的中心和方案来管理此类高风险妊娠。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单个 病例报告,这限制了普遍性并排除了因果推理。尽管描述了详细的围手术期管理,但缺乏对照组和对单中心经验的依赖将证据强度降低至低水平。

Living-donor liver transplantation at 17 weeks gestation for hepatitis B virus-related acute liver failure with hepatic encephalopathy with emergency cesarean delivery at 34 weeks: a case report

Authors: H. Ishimatsu, E. Imai, W. Matsunaga, H. Fujita, K. Yoshinaga, M. Takeuchi, Winthrop Al
Original abstract (English)
Liver transplantation during pregnancy is rare but may be lifesaving in cases of acute liver failure occurring before fetal viability. A primigravida with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection developed HBV-related acute liver failure with grade III hepatic encephalopathy at 17 weeks gestation. Because delivery could not improve fetal survival, the multidisciplinary team prioritized transplantation while pregnancy continued. Laboratory investigations demonstrated severe thrombocytopenia and hypofibrinogenemia. Anesthetic management was guided by predefined maternal physiologic and hematologic targets to support uteroplacental perfusion and included temporary portosystemic shunting. Staged fetal ultrasonography confirmed viability. Surgery lasted 10 h 55 min, with 2,410 mL blood loss. The mother recovered. Following preterm premature rupture of membranes, breech presentation prompted an emergency cesarean delivery at 34 weeks with spinal anesthesia, followed by neonatal intensive care. This case shows that when delivery cannot offer fetal rescue, urgent transplantation and continued pregnancy can be integrated through multidisciplinary decision-making and specialized perioperative management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the clinical challenge of managing acute liver failure due to chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection during pregnancy, where fetal viability precludes delivery as a rescue strategy. It explores whether living-donor liver transplantation can be performed while maintaining pregnancy to optimize maternal and fetal outcomes.
Design and population
This is a single-case report of a primigravida with chronic HBV infection who developed HBV-related acute liver failure with hepatic encephalopathy at 17 weeks gestation. The case required multidisciplinary management including living-donor liver transplantation while pregnancy continued, followed by emergency cesarean delivery at 34 weeks.
Core methods
The anesthetic and surgical management involved predefined maternal physiologic and hematologic targets to support uteroplacental perfusion, including temporary portosystemic shunting. Maternal viability was prioritized through transplantation, with staged fetal ultrasonography confirming viability throughout the perioperative period.
Main results
Laboratory investigations revealed severe thrombocytopenia and hypofibrinogenemia. The surgical procedure lasted 10 hours 55 minutes with an estimated blood loss of 2,410 mL. The mother recovered fully, and the neonate required neonatal intensive care following emergency cesarean delivery at 34 weeks due to preterm premature rupture of membranes and breech presentation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the case as demonstrating that when delivery cannot rescue the fetus, urgent liver transplantation combined with continued pregnancy is feasible through specialized multidisciplinary and perioperative management. The novelty lies in the integrated approach of performing transplantation during pregnancy to stabilize maternal HBV-related acute liver failure while preserving fetal viability.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case highlights the potential for liver transplantation during pregnancy in HBV-related acute liver failure to improve maternal survival without compromising fetal outcomes when delivery is not a viable option. It underscores the need for specialized centers and protocols to manage such high-risk pregnancies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single case report, which restricts generalizability and precludes causal inference. The absence of a control group and reliance on a single-center experience reduce the strength of evidence to low, despite the detailed perioperative management described.

不确定期慢性乙型肝炎患者肝硬化影响因素分析

[Analysis of influencing factors associated with cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase chronic hepatitis B].
作者： Y. F. Guo, X Y Li, C. Guo, L. Li, Y. Liu, C. Y. Wang, J J Wang, F. S. Wang, D. Ji
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
不确定期的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 存在进展为肝硬化的风险,但影响这种转变的因素仍不充分。该研究旨在确定未接受治疗的不确定期慢性乙型肝炎患者中与肝硬化相关的人口统计学、病毒学和临床指标。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了2020年1月至2025年6月期间在中国单一三级中心未接受过抗病毒药物 治疗治疗的422名不确定期CHB患者。根据肝硬化诊断将患者分为肝硬化组(n=182)和非肝硬化组(n=240)。
核心方法
比较各组间人口统计学特征、病毒学指标(包括HBV DNA和HBsAg水平)、血常规参数以及肝功能相关临床指标的差异。使用限制三次样条分析来评估血小板计数和年龄与肝硬化风险的非线性关联,并使用多变量逻辑回归确定与肝硬化独立相关的因素。
主要结果
The cirrhosis group was older (47±9 vs 43±11 years, P<0.001) and had lower platelet counts [158 (94, 199) vs 205 (171, 244)×10^9/L, P<0.001] and HBsAg levels [2.9 (2.5, 3.2) vs 3.1 (2.7, 3.6) log U/ml, P<0.001]. HBV DNA distribution differed significantly (P=0.002), with higher proportions of intermediate HBV DNA load in the cirrhosis group. Age ≥45 years (OR=2.06, 95%CI: 1.32–3.24), platelet count <180×10^9/L (OR=3.99, 95%CI: 2.55–6.23), HBV DNA ≥2×10^3 U/ml (OR=2.12–4.02), and decreased HBsAg (OR=1.99, 95%CI: 1.35–2.93) were independently associated with cirrhosis.
解释与创新
该研究将年龄、血小板计数、HBV DNA 载量和 HBsAg 水平确定为与未经治疗的不确定期 CHB 肝硬化进展相关的关键因素,强调了年龄和血小板计数的非线性风险阈值。这些发现完善了未接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的人群的风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,对年龄、血小板计数、HBV DNA 和 HBsAg 水平进行常规监测可以改善对有肝硬化风险的 CHB 患者的早期识别,特别是在推迟 抗病毒药物 治疗的情况下。这可以为资源有限环境中的 监测 策略和患者咨询提供信息。
局限与证据强度
回顾性单中心设计和 抗病毒药物 治疗 数据的缺乏限制了因果推断和普遍性。由于潜在的未测量的混杂因素以及缺乏纵向随访来确认进展,证据强度中等。

[Analysis of influencing factors associated with cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase chronic hepatitis B].

Authors: Y. F. Guo, X Y Li, C. Guo, L. Li, Y. Liu, C. Y. Wang, J J Wang, F. S. Wang, D. Ji
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To analyze the influencing factors associated with cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Methods: CHB patients in the indeterminate phase who did not receive antiviral treatment at the Senior Department of Hepatology, the Fifth Medical Center of Chinese PLA General Hospital between January 2020 and June 2025 were retrospectively included. According to whether cirrhosis had occurred, the patients were divided into the cirrhosis group (patients diagnosed with cirrhosis) and the non-cirrhosis group (patients not diagnosed with cirrhosis). Differences in demographic characteristics, virological indicators, routine blood parameters, and liver function-related clinical indicators were compared between the two groups. Restricted cubic spline analysis was used to determine the threshold values of platelet and age in relation to cirrhosis risk. A multivariate logistic regression model was used to analyze factors associated with cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase CHB. Results: A total of 422 patients was included. There were 182 patients in the cirrhosis group with 128 males and 54 females, aged (47±9) years; and 240 patients in the non-cirrhosis group with 146 males and 94 females, aged (43±11) years. Patients in the cirrhosis group were older than those in the non-cirrhosis group (P<0.001). The distribution of HBV DNA differed statistically between the two groups (P=0.002). Compared with the non-cirrhosis group, the cirrhosis group had a lower proportion of low HBV DNA load (<2×103 U/ml) [14.8% (27/182) vs 29.6% (71/240), P<0.001] and a higher proportion of intermediate HBV DNA load (2×103-<2×107 U/ml) [67.6% (123/182) vs 57.5% (138/240), P=0.032], whereas the proportion of high HBV DNA load (≥2×107 U/ml) did not differ significantly [17.6% (32/182) vs 12.9% (31/240), P=0.160]. Platelet count [M (Q1, Q3), 158 (94, 199)×109/L vs 205 (171, 244)×109/L] and HBsAg levels (log HBsAg) [2.9 (2.5, 3.2) vs 3.1 (2.7, 3.6) U/ml] were lower in the cirrhosis group than those in the non-cirrhosis group (both P<0.001). Restricted cubic spline analysis showed non-linear associations of platelet count and age with cirrhosis risk in patients with indeterminate-phase CHB (P<0.05). The corresponding threshold values were 45 years and 180×109/L of age and platelet count, respectively. Multivariate logistic regression analysis showed that decreased HBsAg levels (log HbsAg) (OR=1.99, 95%CI: 1.35-2.93), HBV DNA between 2×103 and<2×107 U/ml (OR=2.12, 95%CI: 1.18-3.81), HBV DNA≥2×107 U/ml (OR=4.02, 95%CI: 1.80-9.00), age≥45 years (OR=2.06, 95%CI: 1.32-3.24), and platelet count<180×109/L (OR=3.99, 95%CI: 2.55-6.23) were associated with cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase CHB. Conclusion: Age≥45 years, platelet count<180×109/L, HBV DNA≥2×103 U/ml and decreased HBsAg are influencing factors for the progression to cirrhosis in patients with indeterminate-phase CHB who did not receive antiviral treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in the indeterminate phase poses a risk of progression to cirrhosis, yet factors influencing this transition remain insufficiently characterized. The study aimed to identify demographic, virological, and clinical indicators associated with cirrhosis in untreated patients with indeterminate-phase CHB.
Design and population
This retrospective study enrolled 422 patients with indeterminate-phase CHB who had not received antiviral treatment at a single tertiary center in China between January 2020 and June 2025. Patients were stratified into a cirrhosis group (n=182) and a non-cirrhosis group (n=240) based on cirrhosis diagnosis.
Core methods
Differences in demographic characteristics, virological indicators (including HBV DNA and HBsAg levels), routine blood parameters, and liver function-related clinical indicators were compared between groups. Restricted cubic spline analysis was used to assess non-linear associations of platelet count and age with cirrhosis risk, and multivariate logistic regression identified factors independently associated with cirrhosis.
Main results
The cirrhosis group was older (47±9 vs 43±11 years, P<0.001) and had lower platelet counts [158 (94, 199) vs 205 (171, 244)×10^9/L, P<0.001] and HBsAg levels [2.9 (2.5, 3.2) vs 3.1 (2.7, 3.6) log U/ml, P<0.001]. HBV DNA distribution differed significantly (P=0.002), with higher proportions of intermediate HBV DNA load in the cirrhosis group. Age ≥45 years (OR=2.06, 95%CI: 1.32–3.24), platelet count <180×10^9/L (OR=3.99, 95%CI: 2.55–6.23), HBV DNA ≥2×10^3 U/ml (OR=2.12–4.02), and decreased HBsAg (OR=1.99, 95%CI: 1.35–2.93) were independently associated with cirrhosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identifies age, platelet count, HBV DNA load, and HBsAg levels as key factors associated with cirrhosis progression in untreated indeterminate-phase CHB, highlighting non-linear risk thresholds for age and platelet count. These findings refine risk stratification in a population where antiviral therapy was not administered.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that routine monitoring of age, platelet count, HBV DNA, and HBsAg levels could improve early identification of CHB patients at risk of cirrhosis, particularly in settings where antiviral therapy is deferred. This may inform surveillance strategies and patient counseling in resource-limited environments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective single-center design and lack of antiviral treatment data limit causal inference and generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential unmeasured confounding and the absence of longitudinal follow-up to confirm progression.

BTLA/HVEM 轴作为 慢性乙型肝炎 中分层 功能性治愈 的关键调节器

The BTLA/HVEM axis as a key regulator for stratified functional cure in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Yuzhen Zhang, Shuqi Yang, Yanyan Lin, Yanyan Qiu, Xueping Yu
摘要中文翻译背景 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中的 功能性治愈 率在长期核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 治疗后仍低于 10%,并且 PD-1/PD-L1 阻断在大约 30% 的低基线 HBsAg (≤100 IU/mL) 选定患者中实现 HBsAg 清除。这种不足反映了对 T 细胞耗竭的过于简单化的看法,忽略了与 PD-1 并行运行的非冗余检查点。主要论点 我们综合了新出现的证据(2019-2025),将 B 淋巴细胞和 T 淋巴细胞衰减因子 (BTLA)/疱疹病毒进入介导因子 (HVEM) 轴定位为 HBV 感染分层治疗策略的新兴候选中心。BTLA 优先招募 SHP-1(相对于以 PD-1 为主的 SHP-2)并通过 PI3K-AKT 发出信号,以独立于 PD-1 状态维持终末衰竭。BTLA 优先招募 SHP-1(包含 Src 同源区 2 结构域的磷酸酶-1)[相对于以 PD-1 为主的 SHP-2(包含 Src 同源区 2 结构域的磷酸酶-2)] 并通过 PI3K–AKT 发出信号,以独立于 PD-1 状态限制 TPEX 增殖和效应器分化。在 CHB 中,外周和肝内 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞的 BTLA 上调,与 ALT/AST 和组织学活性相关;然而,这种相关性应谨慎解释,因为肝酶升高主要反映了活动性炎症期间免疫介导的肝细胞损伤,而不是纯粹的 抗病毒药物 免疫功能障碍。在 HBV 相关慢性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 中,CD4+ T 细胞 BTLA 表达独立预测 90 天 死亡率 和继发感染。可溶性 BTLA 已成为资源有限环境中风险分层的候选微创生物标志物。结论 在长期 NA 抑制后的低抗原负荷(HBsAg <100 IU/mL)下,生物标志物引导下的 BTLA/HVEM 联合 PD-1 联合治疗可能会将 功能性治愈 率扩大到单检查点阻断的上限之外。我们概述了具有预定义分层阈值的适应性 I 期试验概念,以实现 HBV 流行地区的可重复翻译。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决长期核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗后慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)中功能性治愈率低以及PD-1/PD-L1阻断在实现HBsAg清除方面取得的有限成功的问题。这一背景凸显了需要对 PD-1 之外的 T 细胞耗竭机制有更细致的了解。
设计与对象
该研究综合了 2019 年至 2025 年的新证据,重点关注人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,特别是 慢性乙型肝炎。它检查了 BTLA/HVEM 轴在 T 细胞耗竭中的作用及其作为治疗靶点的潜力。
核心方法
这些方法涉及分析有关 BTLA/HVEM 轴、其信号通路及其在 HBV 感染期间在 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞中表达的现有文献和数据。该研究还概述了未来研究的适应性第一阶段试验概念。
主要结果
研究报告称,BTLA 优先招募 SHP-1,而不是以 PD-1 为主的 SHP-2,并通过 PI3K-AKT 发出信号,以独立于 PD-1 状态维持终末衰竭。它还指出,BTLA 表达与 CHB 中的 ALT/AST 和组织学活性相关,但应谨慎解释这种相关性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 BTLA/HVEM 轴作为非冗余检查点发挥作用的证据,为 HBV 感染提供了一种新的治疗策略。这使得该轴成为分层治疗方法的新兴候选者。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对 BTLA/HVEM 轴与 PD-1 相结合可以提高慢性乙型肝炎的 功能性治愈 率,从而可能在 HBV 流行地区产生更好的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有文献的综合,不包括原始数据。应谨慎解释 BTLA 表达与肝酶之间的相关性,因为它可能反映免疫介导的损伤,而不是纯粹的 抗病毒药物 免疫功能障碍。

The BTLA/HVEM axis as a key regulator for stratified functional cure in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Yuzhen Zhang, Shuqi Yang, Yanyan Lin, Yanyan Qiu, Xueping Yu
Original abstract (English)
Background Functional cure rates in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remain below 10% after long-term nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy, and PD-1/PD-L1 blockade achieves HBsAg clearance in approximately 30% of selected patients with low baseline HBsAg (≤100 IU/mL). This shortfall reflects an oversimplified view of T-cell exhaustion that neglects nonredundant checkpoints operating in parallel with PD-1. Main arguments We synthesize emerging evidence (2019–2025) to position the B and T lymphocyte attenuator (BTLA)/herpesvirus entry mediator (HVEM) axis as an emerging candidate hub for layered therapeutic strategies in HBV infection. BTLA preferentially recruits SHP-1 (versus PD-1-predominant SHP-2) and signals via PI3K–AKT to sustain terminal exhaustion independently of PD-1 status. BTLA preferentially recruits SHP-1 (Src homology region 2 domain-containing phosphatase-1) [versus PD-1-predominant SHP-2 (Src homology region 2 domain-containing phosphatase-2)] and signals via PI3K–AKT to constrain TPEX proliferation and effector differentiation independently of PD-1 status. In CHB, BTLA is upregulated on peripheral and intrahepatic CD4+ and CD8+ T cells, correlating with ALT/AST and histologic activity; however, this correlation should be interpreted cautiously, as elevated liver enzymes primarily reflect immune-mediated hepatocellular injury during active inflammation rather than purely antiviral immune dysfunction. In HBV-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF), CD4+ T-cell BTLA expression independently predicts 90-day mortality and secondary infections. Soluble BTLA has emerged as a candidate minimally invasive biomarker for risk stratification in resource-limited settings. Conclusion Under low antigen load following long-term NA suppression (HBsAg <100 IU/mL), biomarker-guided combination therapy targeting BTLA/HVEM alongside PD-1 may expand functional cure rates beyond the ceiling of single-checkpoint blockade. We outline an adaptive Phase I trial concept with predefined stratification thresholds to enable reproducible translation in HBV-endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the low functional cure rates in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) after long-term nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy and the limited success of PD-1/PD-L1 blockade in achieving HBsAg clearance. The background highlights the need for a more nuanced understanding of T-cell exhaustion mechanisms beyond PD-1.
Design and population
The study is a synthesis of emerging evidence from 2019 to 2025, focusing on human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, particularly chronic hepatitis B. It examines the role of the BTLA/HVEM axis in T-cell exhaustion and its potential as a therapeutic target.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing existing literature and data on the BTLA/HVEM axis, its signaling pathways, and its expression in CD4+ and CD8+ T cells during HBV infection. The study also outlines an adaptive Phase I trial concept for future research.
Main results
The study reports that BTLA preferentially recruits SHP-1 over PD-1-predominant SHP-2 and signals via PI3K–AKT to sustain terminal exhaustion independently of PD-1 status. It also notes that BTLA expression correlates with ALT/AST and histologic activity in CHB, though this correlation should be interpreted cautiously.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that the BTLA/HVEM axis operates as a nonredundant checkpoint, offering a new therapeutic strategy for HBV infection. This positions the axis as an emerging candidate for layered therapeutic approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting the BTLA/HVEM axis in combination with PD-1 could improve functional cure rates in CHB, potentially leading to better treatment strategies in HBV-endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a synthesis of existing literature and does not include original data. The correlation between BTLA expression and liver enzymes should be interpreted cautiously, as it may reflect immune-mediated injury rather than purely antiviral immune dysfunction.

Multiplex gene editing to target the roots of hepatitis B virus infection
