埃塞俄比亚西北部贡德尔大学综合专科医院产前保健诊所孕妇 乙型肝炎病毒 感染的负担

Burden of Hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women attending antenatal care clinic at the University of Gondar Comprehensive Specialized Hospital, Northwest Ethiopia
作者： Sintayehu Admas, Fitsumbirhan Tajebe, Tadelo Wondmagegn, Nigusie Alemu, Muluneh Assefa, Bisrat Birke Teketelew, Abebaw Setegn, Mitkie Tigabie, Yenesew Mihret Wondmagegn, Getu Girmay
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染在全球范围内构成严重的公共卫生挑战。尽管乙型肝炎疫苗接种可提供超过95%的保护以预防慢性感染,但母婴传播仍是导致HBV感染患病率较高的主要原因。因此,本研究旨在确定西北埃塞俄比亚孕妇中HBV感染的患病率。2023年5月至8月,在埃塞俄比亚西北部的贡达尔大学综合专科医院产前保健(ANC)诊所,采用设施为基础的横断面研究方法,对292名孕妇进行了调查。采用简单随机抽样方法招募孕妇。使用结构化问卷收集社会人口学、临床及其他因素的数据。使用真空采血管采集5毫升静脉血。分离血清样本,并使用一步法乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)检测试纸进行检测,随后通过酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)试剂盒进行确认。使用SPSS 26版对数据进行分析,并采用Logistic回归分析计算HBV感染与预测变量之间的关联。p值≤0.05且95%置信区间被视为具有统计学意义。孕妇年龄范围为18至44岁,平均(标准差)年龄为28.3(5.5)岁。孕妇HBV感染的总体患病率为3.1%(95%置信区间:1.4–5.8)。孕妇中发现HBV感染的中等患病率。为降低这些研究人群中HBV感染的负担,亟需加强公众意识,扩大乙型肝炎疫苗接种项目,增加HBV筛查,并提供早期抗病毒药物治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,尽管 疫苗 疗效很高,但母婴 传播 仍然是慢性感染的主要驱动因素。该研究解决了埃塞俄比亚特定环境下孕妇乙型肝炎病毒感染负担的知识差距。
设计与对象
对 2023 年 5 月至 2023 年 8 月在埃塞俄比亚西北部贡德尔的贡德尔大学综合专科医院产前护理诊所就诊的 292 名孕妇进行了一项基于设施的横断面研究。使用简单的随机抽样技术招募参与者。
核心方法
通过结构化调查问卷收集社会人口统计学、临床和其他因素。采集5毫升静脉血,分离血清样本,并使用一步式HBsAg试纸条检测HBsAg,并通过ELISA进行确认。使用 SPSS 26 版对数据进行分析,并使用逻辑回归来评估 HBV 感染与预测变量之间的关联。
主要结果
孕妇中 HBV 感染的总体 患病率 为 3.1%(95% CI:1.4–5.8),表明 患病率 处于中等水平。
解释与创新
作者将中间 患病率 解释为该人群中持续存在 HBV 传播 风险的证据,并强调需要加强筛查和 疫苗接种 策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持加强 HBV 筛查计划、扩大 传播 覆盖范围并提高公众意识,以减少产前护理机构中的母婴 传播。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计和单中心抽样限制了因果推断和普遍性,并且仅依赖 HBsAg 检测可能会低估隐匿性 HBV 感染,因此需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

Burden of Hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women attending antenatal care clinic at the University of Gondar Comprehensive Specialized Hospital, Northwest Ethiopia

Authors: Sintayehu Admas, Fitsumbirhan Tajebe, Tadelo Wondmagegn, Nigusie Alemu, Muluneh Assefa, Bisrat Birke Teketelew, Abebaw Setegn, Mitkie Tigabie, Yenesew Mihret Wondmagegn, Getu Girmay
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses a serious public health challenge globally. Despite HBV vaccination providing over 95% protection against chronic infection, mother-to-child transmission remains the leading cause of a higher prevalence of HBV infection. Thus, this study aimed to determine the magnitude of HBV infection among pregnant women visiting an antenatal care clinic in northwest Ethiopia. A facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 292 pregnant women from May to August 2023 at the University of Gondar Comprehensive Specialized Hospital antenatal care (ANC) clinic, Gondar, Northwest Ethiopia. A simple random sampling technique was employed to recruit pregnant women. Sociodemographic, clinical, and other factors were collected using a structured questionnaire. Five milliliters of venous blood were collected using a Vacutainer tube. Serum samples were isolated and tested for HBsAg using a one-step HBsAg test strip, with further confirmation through an Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) test kit. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 26, and the association between HBV infection and predictor variables was computed using a logistic regression analysis. A p-value of ≤ 0.05 with a 95% confidence interval was considered statistically significant. The age range of pregnant women was from 18 to 44 years, with a mean (SD) age of 28.3 (5.5) years. The overall prevalence of HBV infection among pregnant women was 3.1% (95% CI: 1.4–5.8). An intermediate prevalence of HBV infection was found among pregnant women. To reduce the burden of HBV infection in these study groups, it is critical to enhance public awareness, expand HBV vaccination programs, increase HBV screening, and provide early treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health problem globally, and mother-to-child transmission persists as a leading driver of chronic infection despite high vaccine efficacy. The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding the burden of HBV infection among pregnant women in a specific Ethiopian setting.
Design and population
A facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 292 pregnant women attending the antenatal care clinic at the University of Gondar Comprehensive Specialized Hospital from May to August 2023 in Gondar, Northwest Ethiopia. Participants were recruited using a simple random sampling technique.
Core methods
Sociodemographic, clinical, and other factors were collected via a structured questionnaire. Five milliliters of venous blood were collected and serum samples were isolated and tested for HBsAg using a one-step HBsAg test strip, with confirmation by ELISA. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 26, and logistic regression was used to assess associations between HBV infection and predictor variables.
Main results
The overall prevalence of HBV infection among pregnant women was 3.1% (95% CI: 1.4–5.8), indicating an intermediate prevalence level.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the intermediate prevalence as evidence of ongoing HBV transmission risk in this population and highlighted the need for enhanced screening and vaccination strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support strengthening HBV screening programs, expanding vaccination coverage, and improving public awareness to reduce mother-to-child transmission in antenatal care settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and single-center sampling limit causal inference and generalizability, and reliance on HBsAg detection alone may underestimate occult HBV infection, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨大戟提取物通过特定分子机制抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌的潜力。
设计与对象
该撤回通知涉及之前在《癌症研究和临床肿瘤学杂志》上发表的一篇日期为 2024 年的文章,该文章声称证明了大戟提取物对 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌的作用。由于担心其结果的可重复性,该研究被撤回。
核心方法
最初的研究可能涉及对大戟效果的实验分析。然而,所提供的证据中并未详细说明具体方法。
主要结果
该文章最初报道的研究结果表明,大戟提取物通过 α 丝氨酸/苏氨酸蛋白激酶和 Caspase-3 抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的肝细胞癌。然而,由于担心可重复性,这些结果现在被撤回。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为表明大戟提取物对 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌的 治疗 具有潜在的治疗作用。然而,撤回意味着支持这种解释的证据不再被认为是可靠的。
科研与公卫意义
如果该发现有效,可能会为未来针对 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌的 治疗 天然化合物的研究提供信息。然而,撤回限制了该研究的科学和公共卫生意义。
局限与证据强度
由于原始研究的撤回,证据受到限制,这引发了人们对研究结果的可靠性和可重复性的担忧。因此,结果的可信度较低。

Further studies are required to clarify the relationship between Euphorbia helioscopia L. and hepatocellular carcinoma. The Authors no longer have confidence in the reliability of the conclusions. Retraction Note: Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract suppress...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential of Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract in suppressing hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma through specific molecular mechanisms.
Design and population
This retraction notice pertains to a previously published article in the Journal of Cancer Research and Clinical Oncology, dated 2024, which claimed to demonstrate the effects of Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract on hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma. The study was retracted due to concerns about the reproducibility of its results.
Core methods
The original study likely involved experimental analysis of the effects of Euphorbia helioscopia L. However, the exact methods are not detailed in the provided evidence.
Main results
The article originally reported findings suggesting that Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract suppresses hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma via alpha serine/threonine-protein kinase and Caspase-3. However, these results are now retracted due to concerns about reproducibility.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as indicating a potential therapeutic role for Euphorbia helioscopia L. extract in the treatment of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma. However, the retraction implies that the evidence supporting this interpretation is no longer considered reliable.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings, if valid, could have informed future research on natural compounds for the treatment of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma. However, the retraction limits the scientific and public health significance of the study.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the retraction of the original study, which raises concerns about the reliability and reproducibility of the findings. The confidence in the results is therefore low.

IL-35 rs583911多态性与慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染患者肝癌风险的相关性

Association between the IL-35 rs583911 polymorphism and hepatocellular carcinoma risk in patients infected with chronic hepatitis B virus
作者： Hui Dong, Duo Zuo, Yuxuan Li, Zhengkun Zhu, Xia Sun, Weishan Meng, Xuan Liu, Wantong Wu, Li Ren
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 免疫调节细胞因子白细胞介素 (IL)-35 与肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的 发病机制 有关。在这里,我们探讨了 IL-35 基因中的单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 与 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 患者发生 HCC 之间的关联。方法选取39例慢性乙型肝炎(HBV)患者和52例HBV相关肝细胞癌(HCC)患者作为病例组,42例健康人作为对照组。我们对 5 个 IL-35 相关单核苷酸多态性 (SNP) 进行了基因分型,并分析了它们与 HCC 风险的关联。此外,还测量了所有组外周血中的血清 IL-35 水平、淋巴细胞计数和调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 频率。结果 rs583911 AA基因型的频率从健康对照(4.8%)到慢性乙型肝炎患者(10.3%)再到肝癌患者(21.2%)呈增加趋势(趋势P = 0.03)。当 HCC 患者与非 HCC 对照(健康对照 + 慢性 HBV 携带者)进行比较时,rs583911 AA 基因型与 HCC 风险相关(OR = 3.35;95% CI:1.17–9.61,P = 0.020)。调整年龄、性别、肝硬化状态和 抗病毒药物 治疗后,相关性不再具有统计学意义(调整后的 P = 0.085)。与对照组相比,HBV 组和 HCC 组血清 IL-35 水平均显着升高( P < 0.001),而 HBV 组和 HCC 组之间差异无统计学意义( P = 0.434)。在 HCC 亚组中,AA 基因型患者的 IL-35 水平往往较高(P = 0.047)。对一组样本(6 名对照、6 名 HBV 患者和 28 名 HCC 患者)进行流式细胞术分析显示,HCC 患者的 Treg 细胞数量高于 HBV 患者(P = 0.038)。结论 我们的研究结果表明 rs583911 AA 基因型与 HBV 相关 HCC 风险之间存在潜在关联。然而,在对多重比较和潜在混杂因素进行严格调整后,这些探索性发现并没有保持统计显着性;因此,需要在更大、独立的 队列研究 中进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了免疫调节细胞因子 IL-35 基因的多态性是否与 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染患者患肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的风险增加相关,考虑到先前的证据表明 IL-35 与 HCC 发病机制 相关。
设计与对象
这项病例对照研究纳入了 39 名慢性 HBV 患者、52 名 HBV 相关 HCC 患者和 42 名健康对照者,所有患者均进行了 5 个 IL-35 相关 SNP 的基因分型,并评估了血清 IL-35 水平、淋巴细胞计数和调节性 T 细胞频率。
核心方法
对各组中 5 个与 IL-35 相关的 SNP 进行基因分型,并测量外周血中血清 IL-35 水平、淋巴细胞计数和调节性 T 细胞频率;对 Treg 的子集进行流式细胞术定量。
主要结果
rs583911 AA 基因型频率从对照组的 4.8% 增加到慢性 HBV 中的 10.3% 和 HCC 中的 21.2%(趋势 P = 0.03),并且与非 HCC 对照组相比与 HCC 风险相关(OR = 3.35;95% CI:1.17–9.61,P = 0.020),但调整后显着性消失(调整后的 P = 0.085)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明 rs583911 AA 基因型与 HBV 相关 HCC 风险之间存在潜在关联,并指出 HBV 和 HCC 组中 IL-35 水平升高,HCC 中 Tregs 增加,尽管这种关联并未经受严格调整。
科研与公卫意义
如果得到验证,这些发现可以为慢性 HBV 患者中 HCC 的遗传风险分层提供信息,并指导未来关于 HBV 相关致癌作用中 IL-35 通路的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小、缺乏多重比较调整以及潜在混杂因素的限制,导致对观察到的关联的置信度较低;验证需要更大的独立 队列研究。

Association between the IL-35 rs583911 polymorphism and hepatocellular carcinoma risk in patients infected with chronic hepatitis B virus

Authors: Hui Dong, Duo Zuo, Yuxuan Li, Zhengkun Zhu, Xia Sun, Weishan Meng, Xuan Liu, Wantong Wu, Li Ren
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The immunoregulatory cytokine interleukin (IL)-35 has been implicated in the pathogenesis of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Here, we explored the association between single-nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) in the IL-35 gene and the occurrence of HCC in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV). Methods We selected 39 patients with chronic hepatitis B (HBV) and 52 patients with HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) as the case groups, along with 42 healthy individuals as the control group. We genotyped five IL-35-related single-nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) and analyzed their association with HCC risk. In addition, serum IL-35 levels, lymphocyte counts, and regulatory T cell (Treg) frequencies were measured in peripheral blood across all groups. Results The frequency of the rs583911 AA genotype tended to increase from healthy controls (4.8%) to patients with chronic HBV (10.3%) to patients with HCC (21.2%) (P for trend = 0.03). When patients with HCC were compared to non-HCC controls (healthy controls + chronic HBV carriers), the rs583911 AA genotype was associated with HCC risk (OR = 3.35; 95% CI: 1.17–9.61, P = 0.020). After adjusting for age, sex, cirrhosis status, and antiviral therapy, the association was no longer statistically significant (adjusted P = 0.085). Serum IL-35 levels were significantly elevated in both the HBV and the HCC groups compared with those in the control group ( P < 0.001), with no significant difference between the HBV and the HCC groups ( P = 0.434). Within the HCC subgroup, patients with the AA genotype tended to have higher IL-35 levels ( P = 0.047). Flow cytometry analysis of a subset of samples (6 controls, 6 patients with HBV, and 28 patients with HCC) revealed that the number of Treg cells was greater in patients with HCC than in those with HBV ( P = 0.038). Conclusion Our findings suggest a potential association between the rs583911 AA genotype and HBV-related HCC risk. However, these exploratory findings did not remain statistically significant after rigorous adjustment for multiple comparisons and potential confounders; therefore, validation in larger, independent cohorts is needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether polymorphisms in the immunoregulatory cytokine IL-35 gene are associated with increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) among patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, given prior evidence implicating IL-35 in HCC pathogenesis.
Design and population
This case-control study enrolled 39 patients with chronic HBV, 52 patients with HBV-related HCC, and 42 healthy controls, all genotyped for five IL-35-related SNPs and assessed for serum IL-35 levels, lymphocyte counts, and regulatory T cell frequencies.
Core methods
Five IL-35-related SNPs were genotyped across groups, and serum IL-35 levels, lymphocyte counts, and regulatory T cell frequencies were measured in peripheral blood; flow cytometry was performed on a subset for Treg quantification.
Main results
The rs583911 AA genotype frequency increased from 4.8% in controls to 10.3% in chronic HBV and 21.2% in HCC (P for trend = 0.03), and was associated with HCC risk versus non-HCC controls (OR = 3.35; 95% CI: 1.17–9.61, P = 0.020), though significance was lost after adjustment (adjusted P = 0.085).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as suggesting a potential association between the rs583911 AA genotype and HBV-related HCC risk, noting elevated IL-35 levels in HBV and HCC groups and increased Tregs in HCC, though the association did not withstand rigorous adjustment.
Scientific and public-health significance
If validated, these findings could inform genetic risk stratification for HCC among chronic HBV patients and guide future studies on IL-35 pathways in HBV-related carcinogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by small sample size, lack of multiple comparison adjustment, and potential confounding, yielding low confidence in the observed association; larger, independent cohorts are required for validation.

印度消除病毒性肝炎的实施差距和政策重点

Implementation gaps and policy priorities for viral hepatitis elimination in India
作者： Trina Sengupta, Saurav Basu
摘要中文翻译病毒性肝炎仍然是全球主要的公共卫生挑战,印度的慢性感染病例占全球的近 11.6%。为了配合世界卫生组织到 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎这一公共卫生威胁的目标,印度启动了国家病毒性肝炎控制计划 (NVHCP),该计划采用针对病毒的战略,包括消除丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV)、减少 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 负担以及加强肠道传播肝炎的预防。通过服务分散化和免费提供仿制药直接作用 抗病毒药物 (DAA),该计划取得了实质性进展,接受治疗的个体丙肝治愈率超过 90%。尽管取得了这些进展,但一些实施挑战仍然阻碍着消除目标的进展。诊断级联中仍然存在关键差距,包括综合反射病毒载量检测的可用性有限、HBV DNA 检测的机会不足以及 HBsAg 阳性个体中缺乏常规丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 筛查。限制性 HBV 治疗 资格标准、次优的乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗 覆盖范围、支离破碎的 监测 系统以及持续的耻辱进一步削弱了计划的有效性。这篇前瞻性文章探讨了印度消除病毒性肝炎的主要障碍,并概述了优先政策行动。其中包括将单次就诊反射分子检测制度化、根据 2024 年 WHO 指南扩大 HBV 监测 资格、加强孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防和出生剂量 疫苗接种 策略、扩大对边缘化人群的覆盖范围,以及整合数字 监测 和患者跟踪系统以增强护理的连续性。旁遮普省肝炎计划等成功的分散模式为扩大有效干预措施和加快印度实现 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎目标的进程提供了宝贵的经验教训。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病毒性肝炎仍然是全球主要的公共卫生挑战,印度的慢性感染病例占全球的近 11.6%。该研究指出需要与世界卫生组织的目标保持一致,即到 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎这一公共卫生威胁。
设计与对象
该研究是一篇透视文章,探讨了印度消除病毒性肝炎的主要障碍。它概述了应对这些挑战和提高计划有效性的优先政策行动。
核心方法
该计划采用针对病毒的策略,包括服务分散化和提供免费通用直接作用 抗病毒药物 (DAA),这些策略已在旁遮普邦肝炎计划等成功的分散模式中实施。
主要结果
尽管取得了这些进展,但一些实施挑战仍然阻碍着消除目标的进展。诊断级联中仍然存在关键差距,包括综合反射病毒载量检测的可用性有限、HBV DNA 检测的机会不足以及 HBsAg 阳性个体中缺乏常规丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 筛查。
解释与创新
作者解释说,限制性 HBV 治疗 资格标准、次优的乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗 覆盖率、支离破碎的 监测 系统和持续的耻辱进一步削弱了计划的有效性。这篇观点文章概述了解决这些障碍的优先政策行动。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要将单次就诊反射分子检测制度化,根据 2024 年 WHO 指南扩大 HBV 抗病毒药物 资格,加强孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防和出生剂量 疫苗接种 策略,扩大对边缘化人群的覆盖范围,并整合数字 监测 和患者跟踪系统以增强护理的连续性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。该研究基于一篇观点文章,可能无法提供严格的实证数据。

Implementation gaps and policy priorities for viral hepatitis elimination in India

Authors: Trina Sengupta, Saurav Basu
Original abstract (English)
Viral hepatitis remains a major public health challenge globally, with India bearing nearly 11.6% of the world’s chronic infections. To align with the World Health Organization’s target of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat by 2030, India launched the National Viral Hepatitis Control Programme (NVHCP), which employs virus-specific strategies including elimination of hepatitis C virus (HCV), reduction of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) burden, and strengthened prevention of enterically transmitted hepatitis. Through decentralization of services and provision of free generic direct-acting antivirals (DAAs), the programme has achieved substantial progress, with HCV cure rates exceeding 90% among treated individuals. Despite these gains, several implementation challenges continue to impede progress toward elimination targets. Critical gaps persist in the diagnostic cascade, including limited availability of integrated reflex viral load testing, inadequate access to HBV DNA testing, and the absence of routine hepatitis D virus (HDV) screening among HBsAg-positive individuals. Restrictive HBV treatment eligibility criteria, suboptimal hepatitis B birth-dose vaccine coverage, fragmented surveillance systems, and persistent stigma further undermine programme effectiveness. This persepective article examines the key barriers to viral hepatitis elimination in India and outlines priority policy actions. These include institutionalizing single-visit reflex molecular testing, expanding HBV treatment eligibility in accordance with the 2024 WHO guidelines, strengthening maternal antiviral prophylaxis and birth-dose vaccination strategies, improving outreach to marginalized populations, and integrating digital surveillance and patient-tracking systems to enhance continuity of care. Successful decentralized models, such as Punjab’s hepatitis programme, offer valuable lessons for scaling up effective interventions and accelerating India’s progress toward achieving its 2030 viral hepatitis elimination goals.erspective revi
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Viral hepatitis remains a major public health challenge globally, with India bearing nearly 11.6% of the world’s chronic infections. The study addresses the need to align with the World Health Organization’s target of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat by 2030.
Design and population
The study is a perspective article examining the key barriers to viral hepatitis elimination in India. It outlines priority policy actions to address these challenges and improve programme effectiveness.
Core methods
The programme employs virus-specific strategies, including decentralization of services and provision of free generic direct-acting antivirals (DAAs), which have been implemented in successful decentralized models such as Punjab’s hepatitis programme.
Main results
Despite these gains, several implementation challenges continue to impede progress toward elimination targets. Critical gaps persist in the diagnostic cascade, including limited availability of integrated reflex viral load testing, inadequate access to HBV DNA testing, and the absence of routine hepatitis D virus (HDV) screening among HBsAg-positive individuals.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that restrictive HBV treatment eligibility criteria, suboptimal hepatitis B birth-dose vaccine coverage, fragmented surveillance systems, and persistent stigma further undermine programme effectiveness. This perspective article outlines priority policy actions to address these barriers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for institutionalizing single-visit reflex molecular testing, expanding HBV treatment eligibility in accordance with the 2024 WHO guidelines, strengthening maternal antiviral prophylaxis and birth-dose vaccination strategies, improving outreach to marginalized populations, and integrating digital surveillance and patient-tracking systems to enhance continuity of care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessments. The study is based on a perspective article, which may not provide rigorous empirical data.

巴西里约热内卢国家参考门诊诊所在 COVID-19 大流行期间罕见的 HBV 基因型以及 HBV 和 HCV 的临床相关 突变

Rare HBV Genotypes and Clinically Relevant Mutations of HBV and HCV During the COVID-19 Pandemic in a National Reference Outpatient Clinic in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil
作者： Lucas Lima da Silva, Bárbara Vieira do Lago, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Viviane Brandão Gomes de Sousa, Lia Laura Lewis-Ximenez, Vanessa Salete de Paula, Livia Melo Villar
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 和 C 是全世界肝硬化、肝细胞癌和肝脏相关 死亡率 的主要原因。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的遗传多样性影响疾病进展、诊断、疫苗 反应和 抗病毒药物 治疗。医疗保健中断时期,例如 COVID-19 大流行,强化了分子 监测 对监测病毒基因型和临床相关 突变 的重要性。这项研究描述了 COVID-19 大流行期间巴西里约热内卢的 HBV 和 HCV 基因型以及 突变 的分布。一项横断面研究包括 2020 年至 2022 年间招募的 25 名患者(15 名乙肝患者和 10 名丙肝患者)。通过 Sanger 方法对病毒核酸进行扩增和测序,并使用 Geno2pheno 平台对 突变 进行分析。HBV 基因型 A 占主导地位 (67%),包括亚基因型 A1 (40%) 和 A2 (27%),其次是罕见的基因型 B1 (13%)、F2 (13%) 和 G (7%)。在HCV感染者中,基因型1a(40%)和1b(30%)占主导地位,其次是基因型4(20%)和2(10%)。鉴定出 HBV 免疫逃逸 突变(Y100C 和 T126S)以及 S 和 RT 结构域中的异常插入。在 HCV 中,检测到 C316Y、C316N 和 S282R 取代。这些发现强调了分子 监测 对于检测临床相关病毒变异的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 仍然是全球肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要原因,其遗传多样性影响疾病进展、诊断 和 治疗 反应。这项研究的动机是需要评估 COVID-19 大流行期间的 HBV 基因型分布和临床相关的 突变,这段医疗保健中断的时期可能会影响分子 监测。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究在巴西里约热内卢的国家参考门诊诊所进行,2020年至2022年间招募了25名患者(15名乙肝患者和10名丙肝患者)。使用Sanger方法对病毒核酸进行扩增和测序,并使用Geno2pheno平台对突变进行分析。

该研究重点关注 HBV 和 HCV 基因型以及临床相关的 突变。
主要结果
HBV 基因型 A 占主导地位 (67%),包括亚基因型 A1 (40%) 和 A2 (27%),其次是罕见基因型 B1 (13%)、F2 (13%) 和 G (7%)。鉴定出 HBV 免疫逃逸 突变(Y100C 和 T126S)以及 S 和 RT 结构域中的异常插入。
解释与创新
作者强调了罕见 HBV 基因型和临床相关 监测 的检测,强调了分子 监测 在医疗保健中断期间的重要性。免疫逃逸 突变 和异常插入的鉴定代表了对局部 HBV 分子 流行病学 的新贡献。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持需要持续进行 HBV 分子 监测 来为 诊断、疫苗 策略和 抗病毒药物 治疗 决策提供信息,特别是在先前基因型数据有限的情况下。
局限与证据强度
小样本量(n = 15 HBV 病例)和单中心设计限制了普遍性和统计功效。由于研究的描述性和缺乏纵向随访,证据强度中等。

Rare HBV Genotypes and Clinically Relevant Mutations of HBV and HCV During the COVID-19 Pandemic in a National Reference Outpatient Clinic in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil

Authors: Lucas Lima da Silva, Bárbara Vieira do Lago, Vanessa Duarte da Costa, Viviane Brandão Gomes de Sousa, Lia Laura Lewis-Ximenez, Vanessa Salete de Paula, Livia Melo Villar
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B and C are major causes of cirrhosis, hepatocellular carcinoma, and liver-related mortality worldwide. The genetic diversity of hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) influences disease progression, diagnosis, vaccine response, and antiviral treatment. Periods of healthcare disruption, such as the COVID-19 pandemic, reinforce the importance of molecular surveillance to monitor viral genotypes and clinically relevant mutations. This study described the distribution of HBV and HCV genotypes and mutations in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, during the COVID-19 pandemic. A cross-sectional study included 25 patients (15 HBV and 10 HCV) recruited between 2020 and 2022. Viral nucleic acids were amplified and sequenced by Sanger methodology, and mutations were analyzed using the Geno2pheno platform. HBV genotype A predominated (67%), comprising subgenotypes A1 (40%) and A2 (27%), followed by the rare genotypes B1 (13%), F2 (13%), and G (7%). Among HCV-infected individuals, genotypes 1a (40%) and 1b (30%) predominated, followed by genotypes 4 (20%) and 2 (10%). HBV immune escape mutations (Y100C and T126S) and unusual insertions in the S and RT domains were identified. In HCV, the substitutions C316Y, C316N, and S282R were detected. These findings highlight the importance of molecular surveillance for detecting clinically relevant viral variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B remains a leading cause of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma globally, and its genetic diversity affects disease progression, diagnosis, and treatment response. The study was motivated by the need to assess HBV genotype distribution and clinically relevant mutations during the COVID-19 pandemic, a period of healthcare disruption that may have impacted molecular surveillance.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in a national reference outpatient clinic in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, enrolling 25 patients (15 HBV and 10 HCV) between 2020 and 2022. Viral nucleic acids were amplified and sequenced using Sanger methodology, and mutations were analyzed with the Geno2pheno platform.
Core methods
The study focused on HBV and HCV genotypes and clinically relevant mutations.
Main results
HBV genotype A predominated (67%), comprising subgenotypes A1 (40%) and A2 (27%), followed by rare genotypes B1 (13%), F2 (13%), and G (7%). HBV immune escape mutations (Y100C and T126S) and unusual insertions in the S and RT domains were identified.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight the detection of rare HBV genotypes and clinically relevant mutations, underscoring the importance of molecular surveillance during periods of healthcare disruption. The identification of immune escape mutations and unusual insertions represents a novel contribution to local HBV molecular epidemiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the need for ongoing molecular surveillance of HBV to inform diagnosis, vaccine strategies, and antiviral treatment decisions, particularly in settings with limited prior genotype data.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (n=15 HBV cases) and single-center design limit generalizability and statistical power. The evidence strength is moderate due to the descriptive nature of the study and absence of longitudinal follow-up.

沙特阿拉伯肾移植受者 疫苗接种 后乙型肝炎血清保护:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究

Post-vaccination hepatitis B seroprotection among kidney transplant recipients in Saudi Arabia: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Rakan A. Aljaafary, Mohammed A. Abdulmogith, Ammar H. S. Abbas
摘要中文翻译背景:关于沙特阿拉伯肾移植受者在接种乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)疫苗后的血清保护证据仍有限。本研究估计了血清保护的发生率,并评估了与疫苗接种无应答相关的因素。方法:本单中心回顾性队列研究纳入了在沙特阿拉伯利雅得王子苏丹军事医疗城接受随访的成年肾移植受者,这些受者具有明确的乙型肝炎病毒感染史,并在之后进行了乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原抗体(抗-HBs)的定量检测。血清保护定义为抗-HBs浓度至少10 mIU/ml。根据血清保护状态对受者特征进行了比较。采用多变量逻辑回归评估未血清保护与乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种总剂次数之间的关联。结果:在100名肾移植受者中,平均(标准差)年龄为41.4(15.2)岁,66名受者(66.0%)为男性。整体而言,69名受者达到血清保护(69.0%;95%置信区间,59.4-77.2%),而31名(31.0%)未达到血清保护。乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种剂次分类在血清保护状态上存在显著差异(p=0.014);未血清保护受者中,80.6%仅接种了1-2剂,而血清保护受者中这一比例为50.7%。在调整后模型中,无变量与未血清保护独立相关。每增加10岁年龄,未血清保护的优势比虽有升高但未达到统计学显著性(调整优势比1.44;95%置信区间,0.96-2.16;p=0.079)。结论:近三分之一的接种疫苗肾移植受者缺乏针对HBV的血清学保护。应考虑常规接种后抗-HBs检测及个体化加强或再次疫苗接种策略,特别是针对年龄较大的受者及接种疫苗剂次较少者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于沙特阿拉伯肾移植受者 疫苗接种 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 血清保护作用的证据仍然有限,提示需要估计 患病率 血清保护作用并确定与 疫苗 无反应相关的因素。
设计与对象
这项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了沙特阿拉伯利雅得苏丹王子军事医学城的 100 名成年肾移植受者,他们记录了乙型肝炎 队列研究 以及随后对乙型肝炎表面抗原(抗 HBs)抗体的定量测量。
核心方法
血清保护被定义为抗-HBs浓度至少为10 mIU/ml,通过血清保护状态比较参与者特征,多变量逻辑回归评估非血清保护与乙型肝炎疫苗剂量总数之间的关联。
主要结果
在100名受者中,69名达到血清保护(69.0%;95%置信区间,59.4-77.2%),而31名未达到血清保护;疫苗剂次类别在血清保护状态上存在显著差异(p=0.014),其中未达到血清保护的受者中有80.6%仅接种1至2剂,而达到血清保护的受者中有50.7%接种1至2剂。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,近三分之一的肾移植受者缺乏针对 HBV 的血清学保护,这凸显了 疫苗接种 后常规抗 HBs 检测和个体化加强疫苗或重新疫苗接种策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,应考虑常规 疫苗接种 后抗 HBs 检测和个体化加强免疫或重新接种策略,特别是对于老年受者和接受较少 疫苗 剂量的人。
局限与证据强度
单中心回顾性设计和调整模型中缺乏独立关联限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要中等强度的证据。

Post-vaccination hepatitis B seroprotection among kidney transplant recipients in Saudi Arabia: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Rakan A. Aljaafary, Mohammed A. Abdulmogith, Ammar H. S. Abbas
Original abstract (English)
Background: Evidence regarding post-vaccination hepatitis B virus (HBV) seroprotection among kidney transplant recipients in Saudi Arabia remains limited. This study estimated the prevalence of seroprotection and evaluated factors associated with vaccine nonresponse. Methods: This single-center retrospective cohort study included adult kidney transplant recipients followed at Prince Sultan Military Medical City, Riyadh, Saudi Arabia, who had documented hepatitis B vaccination and subsequent quantitative measurement of antibodies to hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs). Seroprotection was defined as an anti-HBs concentration of at least 10 mIU/ml. Participant characteristics were compared according to seroprotection status. Multivariable logistic regression was used to evaluate associations between non-seroprotection and total number of hepatitis B vaccine doses. Results: Among 100 kidney transplant recipients, the mean (SD) age was 41.4 (15.2) years, and 66 participants (66.0%) were male. Overall, 69 recipients achieved seroprotection (69.0%; 95% CI, 59.4-77.2%), whereas 31 (31.0%) remained non-seroprotected. Vaccine dose category differed significantly by seroprotection status (p=0.014); 80.6% of non-seroprotected recipients had received only 1 to 2 doses compared with 50.7% of seroprotected recipients. In the adjusted model, no variable was independently associated with non-seroprotection. Each 10-year increase in age was associated with higher, but not statistically significant, odds of non-seroprotection (aOR, 1.44; 95% CI, 0.96-2.16; p=0.079). Conclusions: Nearly one-third of vaccinated kidney transplant recipients lacked serological protection against HBV. Routine post-vaccination anti-HBs testing and individualized booster or revaccination strategies should be considered, particularly for older recipients and those who have received fewer vaccine doses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Evidence regarding post-vaccination hepatitis B virus (HBV) seroprotection among kidney transplant recipients in Saudi Arabia remains limited, prompting the need to estimate seroprotection prevalence and identify factors associated with vaccine nonresponse.
Design and population
This single-center retrospective cohort study included 100 adult kidney transplant recipients followed at Prince Sultan Military Medical City, Riyadh, Saudi Arabia, who had documented hepatitis B vaccination and subsequent quantitative measurement of antibodies to hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs).
Core methods
Seroprotection was defined as an anti-HBs concentration of at least 10 mIU/ml, participant characteristics were compared by seroprotection status, and multivariable logistic regression evaluated associations between non-seroprotection and total number of hepatitis B vaccine doses.
Main results
Among 100 recipients, 69 achieved seroprotection (69.0%; 95% CI, 59.4-77.2%), while 31 remained non-seroprotected; vaccine dose category differed significantly by seroprotection status (p=0.014), with 80.6% of non-seroprotected recipients having received only 1 to 2 doses versus 50.7% of seroprotected recipients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating that nearly one-third of vaccinated kidney transplant recipients lacked serological protection against HBV, highlighting the need for routine post-vaccination anti-HBs testing and individualized booster or revaccination strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that routine post-vaccination anti-HBs testing and individualized booster or revaccination strategies should be considered, particularly for older recipients and those who have received fewer vaccine doses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The single-center retrospective design and lack of independent associations in adjusted models limit causal inference and generalizability, warranting a moderate strength of evidence.

重新审视 HBeAg 阴性的灰色地带 慢性乙型肝炎:来自多中心肝脏活检的见解 队列研究

Revisiting the Gray Zone in HBeAg-Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights From a Multicenter Liver Biopsy Cohort
作者： Özlem Gül, Hüseyin Köseoğlu, Selim Yalçın, Barış Yılmaz, Dilek Oğuz, Sema Zergeroğlu, Bilal Ergül, Özlem Gül Utku
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:对具有中等病毒血症和持续正常丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 水平的 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的治疗仍存在争议。尽管不符合传统的 治疗 标准,但这种“灰色地带”(GZ) 人群可能存在严重的组织学肝损伤。本研究描述了该 GZ 人群的组织学特征,并比较了他们的临床和实验室特征。材料和方法:在这项多中心回顾性研究中,纳入了 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 水平在 2000 至 20 000 IU/mL 之间且 ALT 水平持续正常且在 2014 年至 2022 年间接受肝活检的 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者。使用改良的 Ishak 评分系统进行组织学评估。显着纤维化定义为 Ishak 阶段为 2 级或更高。分析了临床、病毒学和非侵袭性纤维化参数。结果:总共纳入 145 名患者(平均年龄:46.3 ± 12.6 岁;54.5% 为女性)。在 109 名患者 (75.1%) 中发现了需要 抗病毒药物 治疗的显着纤维化和/或中度至重度坏死性炎症活动。尽管 ALT 和天冬氨酸转氨酶水平保持在正常参考范围内,但 治疗 组的 ALT 和天冬氨酸转氨酶水平均显着高于未治疗组(分别为 P = .003 和 P = .018)。接受治疗的患者中天冬氨酸转氨酶与血小板比率指数 (APRI) 评分也显着升高 (P = .02),而 Fibrorsis-4 (FIB-4) 评分没有显着差异。各组之间的 HBV DNA 和定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 水平相当。结论:广州地区相当一部分 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者尽管 ALT 水平持续正常,但仍表现出临床上显着的组织学疾病。仅依赖传统的生化阈值可能会低估纤维化的负担。对于广州地区的选定患者,应考虑早期组织学评估和更主动的 治疗 策略。引用本文为:Gül Ö、Köseoğlu H、Yalçın S 等。重新审视 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 中的灰色地带:来自多中心肝活检 队列研究 的见解。土耳其胃肠病学杂志。2026 年 8 月 11 日在线发布。doi:10.5152/tjg.2026.26367。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对于具有中等病毒血症和持续正常丙氨酸氨基转移酶 (ALT) 水平的 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的治疗仍存在争议。尽管不符合传统的 治疗 标准,但这种“灰色地带”(GZ) 人群可能存在严重的组织学肝损伤。
设计与对象
在这项多中心回顾性研究中,纳入了 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 水平在 2000 至 20 000 IU/mL 之间且 ALT 水平持续正常且在 2014 年至 2022 年间接受肝活检的 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者。总共纳入 145 名患者(平均年龄:46.3 ± 12.6 岁;54.5% 为女性)。
核心方法
使用改良的 Ishak 评分系统进行组织学评估。显着纤维化定义为 Ishak 阶段为 2 级或更高。分析了临床、病毒学和非侵袭性纤维化参数。
主要结果
在 109 名患者 (75.1%) 中发现了需要 抗病毒药物 治疗的显着纤维化和/或中度至重度坏死性炎症活动。尽管 ALT 和天冬氨酸转氨酶水平保持在正常参考范围内,但 治疗 组的 ALT 和天冬氨酸转氨酶水平均显着高于未治疗组(分别为 P = .003 和 P = .018)。接受治疗的患者中天冬氨酸转氨酶与血小板比率指数 (APRI) 评分也显着升高 (P = .02),而 FIB-4 评分没有显着差异。各组之间的 HBV DNA 和定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 水平相当。
解释与创新
尽管 ALT 水平持续正常,广州地区相当大比例的 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者仍表现出临床上显着的组织学疾病。仅依赖传统的生化阈值可能会低估纤维化的负担。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,应考虑对广州的选定患者进行早期组织学评估和更积极主动的 治疗 策略,以更好地管理疾病负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于肝活检数据,这可能会引入偏差并限制普遍性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于大型多中心 队列研究 和直接组织学评估。

Revisiting the Gray Zone in HBeAg-Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights From a Multicenter Liver Biopsy Cohort

Authors: Özlem Gül, Hüseyin Köseoğlu, Selim Yalçın, Barış Yılmaz, Dilek Oğuz, Sema Zergeroğlu, Bilal Ergül, Özlem Gül Utku
Original abstract (English)
Background/Aims: The management of patients with HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (CHB) with intermediate viremia and persistently normal alanine aminotransferase (ALT) levels remains controversial. This “gray-zone” (GZ) population may harbor significant histological liver injury despite not meeting conventional treatment criteria. This study characterized the histologic features of this GZ population and compared their clinical and laboratory characteristics. Materials and Methods: In this multicenter retrospective study, patients with HBeAg negative CHB who had hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA levels between 2000 and 20 000 IU/mL and persistently normal ALT levels and who underwent liver biopsy between 2014 and 2022 were included. Histological assessment was performed using the modified Ishak scoring system. Significant fibrosis was defined as Ishak stage of 2 or greater. Clinical, virological, and noninvasive fibrosis parameters were analyzed. Results: A total of 145 patients were included (mean age: 46.3 ± 12.6 years; 54.5% female). Significant fibrosis and/or moderate-tosevere necroinflammatory activity requiring antiviral therapy was identified in 109 patients (75.1%). Although ALT and aspartate aminotransferase levels remained within normal reference range, both were significantly higher in the treatment group compared than in the nontreatment group (P = .003 and P = .018, respectively). Aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ration index (APRI) scores were also significantly elevated among treated patients (P = .02), whereas Fibrorsis-4 (FIB-4) scores did not differ significantly. HBV DNA and quantitative Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) levels were comparable between groups. Conclusion: A substantial proportion of patients with HBeAg-negative CHB in the GZ exhibited clinically significant histologic disease despite persistently normal ALT levels. Reliance solely on conventional biochemical thresholds may underestimate the burden of fibrosis. Early histological evaluation and more proactive treatment strategies should be considered for selected patients in the GZ. Cite this article as: Gül Ö, Köseoğlu H, Yalçın S, et al. Revisiting the gray zone in HBeAg negative chronic hepatitis B: Insights from a multicenter liver biopsy cohort. Turk J Gastroenterol. Published online August 11, 2026. doi: 10.5152/tjg.2026.26367.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The management of patients with HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (CHB) with intermediate viremia and persistently normal alanine aminotransferase (ALT) levels remains controversial. This "gray-zone" (GZ) population may harbor significant histological liver injury despite not meeting conventional treatment criteria.
Design and population
In this multicenter retrospective study, patients with HBeAg negative CHB who had hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA levels between 2000 and 20 000 IU/mL and persistently normal ALT levels and who underwent liver biopsy between 2014 and 2022 were included. A total of 145 patients were included (mean age: 46.3 ± 12.6 years; 54.5% female).
Core methods
Histological assessment was performed using the modified Ishak scoring system. Significant fibrosis was defined as Ishak stage of 2 or greater. Clinical, virological, and noninvasive fibrosis parameters were analyzed.
Main results
Significant fibrosis and/or moderate-to-severe necroinflammatory activity requiring antiviral therapy was identified in 109 patients (75.1%). Although ALT and aspartate aminotransferase levels remained within normal reference range, both were significantly higher in the treatment group compared than in the nontreatment group (P = .003 and P = .018, respectively). Aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index (APRI) scores were also significantly elevated among treated patients (P = .02), whereas FIB-4 scores did not differ significantly. HBV DNA and quantitative Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) levels were comparable between groups.
Interpretation and novelty
A substantial proportion of patients with HBeAg-negative CHB in the GZ exhibited clinically significant histologic disease despite persistently normal ALT levels. Reliance solely on conventional biochemical thresholds may underestimate the burden of fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that early histological evaluation and more proactive treatment strategies should be considered for selected patients in the GZ to better manage the disease burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on liver biopsy data, which may introduce bias and limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on a large multicenter cohort and direct histological assessment.

定量 HBsAg 作为未经治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者疾病分期的生物标志物:一项横断面研究

Quantitative HBsAg as a biomarker for disease staging in untreated chronic hepatitis B patients: a cross-sectional study
作者： Soumaya Mrabet, Imene Handous, Oussama Trabelsi, Yassmine Maatouk, Raida Harbi, Imen Akkari, Neila Hannachi, Elhem Ben Jazia
摘要中文翻译背景和目标 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染是北非的一个主要问题,该地区 HBeAg 阴性的情况占主导地位。使用常规标记物区分慢性感染和活动性慢性肝炎有时很困难。我们的研究评估了 HBsAg 与病毒载量和肝纤维化的相关性,及其在区分慢性 HBV 感染不同演变阶段的表现。方法和结果 我们进行了一项横断面分析研究,纳入了 90 名 HBeAg 阴性 治疗 初治患者。所有患者均接受了生物学评估、HBsAg 定量、病毒载量和 FibroScan®。在90名患者中(平均年龄:47.5±11岁;52.6%为女性),65.6%处于低复制慢性感染阶段。中位病毒载量为 328.5 IU/mL,中位 HBsAg 水平为 213.5 IU/mL。HBsAg 和 HBV DNA 水平之间存在很强的正相关性(r = 0.721,p < 0.001)。HBsAg 水平与肝纤维化存在微弱但显着的关联(p = 0.007,AUC = 0.631)。HBsAg 在区分低复制期和高复制期方面的表现非常出色(AUC = 0.819;p < 0.001),最佳阈值为 450 IU/mL(敏感性:81.3%;特异性:77.6%)。在多变量分析中,HBV DNA 和 AST 与疾病阶段独立相关,而 qHBsAg 并不是独立的预测因子。结论 定量 HBsAg 测量是一种相关工具,可作为补充替代标志物,用于细化未经治疗的 HBeAg 阴性患者的分层。当与病毒载量、转氨酶和 FibroScan® 结合使用时,HBsAg 可以有助于优化监测和更好的资源分配。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染在 HBeAg 阴性病例占主导地位的北非造成了重大的公共卫生负担,传统标记有时无法区分慢性感染和活动性慢性肝炎。
设计与对象
一项横断面分析研究招募了 90 名 HBeAg 阴性、未接受过 治疗 治疗的患者(平均年龄 47.5 ± 11 岁;52.6% 为女性),并进行了生物学评估、HBsAg 定量、病毒载量测量和 FibroScan®。
核心方法
对 HBsAg 和 HBV DNA 进行定量,并通过 FibroScan® 评估肝纤维化情况;使用统计测试和接受者操作特征分析来评估相关性和诊断性能。
主要结果
HBsAg 与 HBV DNA 呈强正相关(r = 0.721,p < 0.001),与肝纤维化呈弱但显着的相关性(p = 0.007,AUC = 0.631),并且在区分低复制期与高复制期方面表现出色(AUC = 0.819,p < 0.001),最佳阈值为 450 IU/mL(灵敏度81.3%,特异性 77.6%)。
解释与创新
作者将定量 HBsAg 解释为相关的补充替代标记,用于对未经治疗的 HBeAg 阴性慢性 HBV 患者进行精细分层,强调了其与病毒载量、转氨酶和 FibroScan® 结合使用时的附加值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明 HBsAg 定量可以改善慢性 HBV 感染管理中的监测策略和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计和单中心设置限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要前瞻性验证;总体证据强度中等。

Quantitative HBsAg as a biomarker for disease staging in untreated chronic hepatitis B patients: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Soumaya Mrabet, Imene Handous, Oussama Trabelsi, Yassmine Maatouk, Raida Harbi, Imen Akkari, Neila Hannachi, Elhem Ben Jazia
Original abstract (English)
Background and aims Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major issue in North Africa, where HBeAg-negative profiles are predominant. Distinguishing between chronic infection and active chronic hepatitis using conventional markers is sometimes difficult. Our study assessed the correlation of HBsAg with viral load and liver fibrosis, as well as its performance in distinguishing the different evolving phases of chronic HBV infection. Approach and results We conducted an analytical cross-sectional study that included 90 HBeAg-negative treatment-naïve patients. All patients underwent biological assessment, HBsAg quantification, viral load, and FibroScan®. Among the 90 patients (mean age: 47.5 ± 11 years; 52.6% women), 65.6% were in the low-replicative chronic infection phase. The median viral load was 328.5 IU/mL, and the median HBsAg level was 213.5 IU/mL. A strong positive correlation was observed between HBsAg and HBV DNA levels ( r = 0.721, p < 0.001). HBsAg levels showed a weak but significant association with liver fibrosis ( p = 0.007, AUC = 0.631). The performance of HBsAg in differentiating low versus high replicative phases was excellent (AUC = 0.819; p < 0.001), with an optimal threshold of 450 IU/mL (sensitivity: 81.3%; specificity: 77.6%). In multivariate analysis, HBV DNA and AST were independently associated with disease phase, whereas qHBsAg was not an independent predictor. Conclusion Quantitative HBsAg measurement is a relevant tool for refining the stratification of untreated HBeAg-negative patients as a complementary surrogate marker. When combined with viral load, transaminases, and FibroScan®, HBsAg could contribute to optimized monitoring and better resource allocation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses a significant public-health burden in North Africa where HBeAg-negative profiles predominate, and conventional markers sometimes fail to distinguish chronic infection from active chronic hepatitis.
Design and population
An analytical cross-sectional study enrolled 90 HBeAg-negative, treatment-naïve patients (mean age 47.5 ± 11 years; 52.6% women) and performed biological assessment, HBsAg quantification, viral load measurement, and FibroScan®.
Core methods
HBsAg and HBV DNA were quantified, and liver fibrosis was assessed by FibroScan®; correlation and diagnostic performance were evaluated using statistical tests and receiver operating characteristic analysis.
Main results
HBsAg showed a strong positive correlation with HBV DNA (r = 0.721, p < 0.001), a weak but significant association with liver fibrosis (p = 0.007, AUC = 0.631), and excellent performance in differentiating low- versus high-replicative phases (AUC = 0.819, p < 0.001) with an optimal threshold of 450 IU/mL (sensitivity 81.3%, specificity 77.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret quantitative HBsAg as a relevant complementary surrogate marker for refining stratification of untreated HBeAg-negative chronic HBV patients, highlighting its added value when combined with viral load, transaminases, and FibroScan®.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that HBsAg quantification could improve monitoring strategies and resource allocation in chronic HBV infection management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and single-centre setting limit causal inference and generalisability, warranting prospective validation; overall evidence strength is moderate.

慢性丁型肝炎病毒感染对健康相关生活质量和疲劳的影响:一项真实的国际研究。

The Impact of Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection on Health-Related Quality of Life and Fatigue: A Real-World International Study.
作者： P Lampertico, R G Gish, N Reau, H Wedemeyer, M Buti, A Kaushik, A Yehoshua, C Burk, L Mirams, H Ellis
摘要中文翻译由于人们对慢性丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染的疾病负担知之甚少,因此本研究检查了不同疾病阶段的健康相关生活质量(HRQoL)和疲劳。这是对德国、意大利、西班牙和美国患者的真实世界定量横断面调查。患者是通过治疗医生招募的。接受过治疗乙型肝炎的 bulevivirtide 或治疗 HDV 的 治疗 的患者被排除在外。211 名患者中,41% 为非肝硬化(F0-F3);20%患有肝硬化但功能正常(代偿);20% 患有肝硬化(F4)且功能受损(失代偿);19% 患有肝细胞癌 (HCC)。平均(标准差;SD)年龄为 54.5(11.5)岁,75% 为男性。方差分析显示,随着疾病阶段恶化和严重程度增加,EQ-5D-3L 视觉模拟量表 (VAS) 评分和指数值显着下降(表明 HRQoL 较差)(所有 p < 0.0001)。肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 的所有领域评分(除了积极的健康状况)随着疾病的进展和症状严重程度的增加而恶化(所有 p ≤ 0.0001)。平均疲劳严重程度量表 (FSS) 总分也增加,表明疲劳程度更大,疾病恶化,症状严重程度增加(均 p < 0.0001)。丁型肝炎病毒感染显着影响患者的 HRQoL 和疲劳,特别是对于疾病后期的患者。需要减缓疾病进展、减轻丁型肝炎病毒感染负担的治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染造成的疾病负担人们知之甚少,促使本研究量化其对各个疾病阶段的健康相关生活质量 (HRQoL) 和疲劳的影响。
设计与对象
这项现实世界的横断面调查通过治疗医生招募了来自德国、意大利、西班牙和美国的患者,不包括那些接受乙型肝炎或 HDV​​ 特异性治疗的布列韦肽治疗的患者。
核心方法
该研究使用 EQ-5D-3L 视觉模拟量表和指数值以及肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 评估 HRQoL,并使用疲劳严重程度量表测量疲劳程度,按疾病阶段比较结果。
主要结果
方差分析显示,随着疾病阶段的恶化,EQ-5D-3L 评分和指数值显着降低(所有 p < 0.0001),并且除积极幸福感外,所有肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 领域评分均随着严重程度的增加而下降(所有 p ≤ 0.0001)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,丁型肝炎病毒感染会严重损害 HRQoL 并增加疲劳,特别是在疾病晚期阶段,这凸显了对减缓进展的治疗的需求尚未得到满足。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了早期检测和干预对于缓解 HDV 感染患者 HRQoL 恶化和疲劳的重要性。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计和治疗患者的排除限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要对证据有一定的信心。

The Impact of Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection on Health-Related Quality of Life and Fatigue: A Real-World International Study.

Authors: P Lampertico, R G Gish, N Reau, H Wedemeyer, M Buti, A Kaushik, A Yehoshua, C Burk, L Mirams, H Ellis
Original abstract (English)
As little is known about the disease burden of chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection, this study examined health-related quality of life (HRQoL) and fatigue at different disease stages. This was a real-world quantitative cross-sectional survey of patients in Germany, Italy, Spain and the USA. Patients were recruited via their treating physicians. Patients who had received bulevirtide for hepatitis B or treatment licensed for HDV were excluded. Of 211 patients, 41% were noncirrhotic (F0-F3); 20% had liver cirrhosis with normal function (compensated); 20% had liver cirrhosis (F4) with impaired function (decompensated); 19% had hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Mean (standard deviation; SD) age was 54.5 (11.5) years, 75% were male. ANOVA showed a significant decrease (indicating poorer HRQoL) in EQ-5D-3L visual analogue scale (VAS) scores and index values with worsening disease stage and increasing severity (all p < 0.0001). All domain scores of the Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2 (except positive wellbeing) worsened with progressing disease and increasing symptom severity (all p ≤ 0.0001). Mean Fatigue Severity Scale (FSS) total scores also increased, indicating greater fatigue, with worsening of disease and increasing symptom severity (both p < 0.0001). HDV infection significantly impacts patients' HRQoL and fatigue, particularly for patients at later disease stages. Treatments that slow disease progression, reducing the burden of HDV infection, are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection imposes a poorly understood disease burden, prompting this study to quantify its impact on health-related quality of life (HRQoL) and fatigue across disease stages.
Design and population
This real-world cross-sectional survey enrolled patients from Germany, Italy, Spain and the USA via treating physicians, excluding those treated with bulevirtide for hepatitis B or HDV-specific therapy.
Core methods
The study assessed HRQoL using EQ-5D-3L visual analogue scale and index values and the Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2, and measured fatigue with the Fatigue Severity Scale, comparing outcomes by disease stage.
Main results
ANOVA revealed significantly lower EQ-5D-3L scores and index values with worsening disease stage (all p < 0.0001), and all Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2 domain scores except positive wellbeing declined with increasing severity (all p ≤ 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that HDV infection substantially impairs HRQoL and increases fatigue, particularly in advanced disease stages, highlighting an unmet need for therapies that slow progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the importance of early detection and intervention to mitigate HRQoL deterioration and fatigue in HDV-infected patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and exclusion of treated patients limit causal inference and generalisability, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

Journal: Journal of Clinical Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-13  |  DOI: 10.3390/jcm15166257  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 15, 16, 6257
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

替诺福韦艾拉酚胺与恩替卡韦作为 慢性乙型肝炎 患者富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯后的转换治疗

Tenofovir Alafenamide Versus Entecavir as Switch Therapy After Tenofovir Disoproxil Fumarate in Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Hsin-Ju Tsai, Cheng-Hao Wu, Po-Yueh Chen, Chia-Chang Chen, Ying-Cheng Lin, Shou-Wu Lee, Yen-Chun Peng, Teng-Yu Lee
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 需要安全有效的 抗病毒药物 治疗,但富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 会带来肾脏风险,促使改用替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 或恩替卡韦 (ETV)。该研究解决了从 TDF 转换为 TAF 与 ETV 后有关长期肾脏安全性和 抗病毒药物 疗效的知识差距。
设计与对象
一项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2012 年 1 月至 2021 年 12 月期间从 TDF 转为 TAF 或 ETV 的连续 CHB 患者。
核心方法
使用倾向得分平衡基线特征的 治疗 加权的逆概率。使用线性混合效应模型分析估计肾小球滤过率 (eGFR) 的变化,肾功能障碍定义为至少一种 GFR 类别的下降。
主要结果
在 235 名患者(TAF,n=168;ETV,n=67)中,36 个月内 TAF 的平均 eGFR 下降为 -3.21 mL/min/1.73 m2,而 ETV 为 -3.49 mL/min/1.73 m2(p=0.856)。TAF 的 3 年累积肾功能障碍 发病率 为 16.6%,而 ETV 为 7.8% (p=0.135)。丙氨酸转氨酶正常化率分别为 83.0% 和 78.8% (p=0.575),病毒学抑制率分别为 97.8% 和 97.1% (p=1.000)。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,从 TDF 转换后,TAF 和 ETV 显示出相似的长期肾脏安全性和 抗病毒药物 疗效,支持这两种药物作为因肾脏问题需要停用 TDF 的患者的合理替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,对于需要从 TDF 转换的 CHB 患者,可以考虑 TAF 或 ETV,以减轻肾脏风险,同时又不影响 抗病毒药物 的有效性。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计和有限的样本量可能会降低精确度和普遍性,需要谨慎的解释和更大规模的前瞻性研究来确认长期结果。

Tenofovir Alafenamide Versus Entecavir as Switch Therapy After Tenofovir Disoproxil Fumarate in Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Hsin-Ju Tsai, Cheng-Hao Wu, Po-Yueh Chen, Chia-Chang Chen, Ying-Cheng Lin, Shou-Wu Lee, Yen-Chun Peng, Teng-Yu Lee
Original abstract (English)
Background: Tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) and entecavir (ETV) are recommended alternatives to tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) for patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) at increased risk of kidney injury. However, data from direct head-to-head comparisons of their long-term renal safety and antiviral efficacy after switching from TDF are lacking. We aimed to compare these outcomes between TAF and ETV. Methods: This multicenter retrospective cohort study included consecutive CHB patients who switched from TDF to either TAF or ETV between January 2012 and December 2021. Inverse probability of treatment weighting using the propensity score was applied to balance the baseline characteristics. Changes in estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) were assessed using a linear mixed-effects model. Renal dysfunction was defined as a decline of at least one GFR category. Results: A total of 235 patients were included (TAF, n = 168; ETV, n = 67). After adjustment for key risk factors, the mean eGFR decline over 36 months was not significantly different between the TAF and ETV groups (−3.21 mL/min/1.73 m2 [95% CI, −4.98 to −1.44] vs. −3.49 mL/min/1.73 m2 [95% CI, −9.49 to −2.50]; p = 0.856). The 3-year cumulative incidence of renal dysfunction was also not significantly different between groups (16.6% [95% CI, 10.5–23.3] vs. 7.8% [95% CI, 2.7–16.2]; p = 0.135). Alanine aminotransferase normalization rates (83.0% vs. 78.8%; p = 0.575) and virological suppression rates (97.8% vs. 97.1%; p = 1.000) were similarly high in both groups. Conclusions: TAF and ETV demonstrated similar long-term renal safety and antiviral efficacy, supporting both agents as reasonable switch options for CHB patients requiring TDF discontinuation because of renal safety concerns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) requires safe and effective antiviral therapy, but tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) poses renal risks prompting switches to tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) or entecavir (ETV). The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding long-term renal safety and antiviral efficacy after switching from TDF to TAF versus ETV.
Design and population
A multicenter retrospective cohort study enrolled consecutive CHB patients who switched from TDF to either TAF or ETV between January 2012 and December 2021.
Core methods
Inverse probability of treatment weighting using propensity scores balanced baseline characteristics. Changes in estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) were analyzed with a linear mixed-effects model, and renal dysfunction was defined as a decline of at least one GFR category.
Main results
Among 235 patients (TAF, n=168; ETV, n=67), the mean eGFR decline over 36 months was −3.21 mL/min/1.73 m2 for TAF versus −3.49 mL/min/1.73 m2 for ETV (p=0.856). The 3-year cumulative incidence of renal dysfunction was 16.6% for TAF versus 7.8% for ETV (p=0.135). Alanine aminotransferase normalization rates were 83.0% versus 78.8% (p=0.575) and virological suppression rates were 97.8% versus 97.1% (p=1.000).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that TAF and ETV showed similar long-term renal safety and antiviral efficacy after switching from TDF, supporting both as reasonable alternatives for patients needing TDF discontinuation due to renal concerns.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest either TAF or ETV can be considered for CHB patients requiring a switch from TDF to mitigate renal risk without compromising antiviral effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design and limited sample size may reduce precision and generalizability, warranting cautious interpretation and larger prospective studies to confirm long-term outcomes.

尼日利亚阿布贾大学教学医院瓜瓦格瓦拉达医院清洁工对乙型肝炎感染的了解 疫苗接种 现状、风险认知和障碍

Knowledge of Hepatitis B Infection Vaccination Status, Risk Perception and Barriers among Hospital Cleaners at the University of Abuja Teaching Hospital Gwagwalada, Nigeria
作者： Adam Omotosho Abdulquadri, Biyaya Nwankwo, Saheed Ayodeji Adekola, Doyinsola Faiza Akano, Hassanat Taiye Aliyu, Al-Ghazali Etudaiye Muhammed
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 医院清洁工在医疗保健感染控制中处于前线地位,但仍然是乙型肝炎研究中研究最少的职业群体之一。本研究评估了尼日利亚瓜瓦拉达阿布贾大学教学医院 (UATH) 清洁工对乙型肝炎感染及其 疫苗 的了解、风险认知、疫苗接种 状况以及 疫苗接种 的障碍。方法对通过系统随机抽样抽取的145名清洁工进行描述性横断面研究。使用结构化、访谈员管理的问卷收集数据,涵盖社会人口学特征、乙型肝炎知识、风险认知、疫苗接种 状态以及 疫苗接种 障碍。使用 SPSS 23 版进行分析,使用描述性统计和卡方检验。获得了 UATH 健康研究伦理委员会的伦理批准。结果 145份问卷全部收回(有效率为100%)。平均年龄为 40.63 ± 9.96 岁;73.1%为女性。135 名参与者 (93.1%) 的乙型肝炎知识良好,111 名参与者 (76.6%) 的乙型肝炎 疫苗 知识良好。106 名参与者 (73.1%) 具有良好的风险认知。尽管如此,只有 49 名清洁工 (33.8%) 至少接受了一剂 疫苗,并且只有 19 名 (13.1%) 完成了完整的三剂系列。在那些不完全或未接种疫苗的人中,缺乏认识(66.2%)、缺乏适当的医院 疫苗接种 政策(57.9%)、新员工培训不足(53.8%)和负担不起(37.2%)是确定的主要障碍。工作经验年数 (p = 0.005) 和年龄 (p = 0.023) 与 疫苗接种 状态显着相关。结论 尽管普遍具备足够的知识和风险意识,UATH 医院清洁工的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然极低。系统性制度缺陷,特别是缺乏可执行的 疫苗接种 政策和结构化的定向计划,是比个人知识差距更直接的障碍。迫切需要有针对性的政策改革和工作场所 疫苗接种 计划。

问题与背景
本研究旨在评估尼日利亚瓜瓦拉达阿布贾大学教学医院的医院清洁工对乙型肝炎感染及其 疫苗 的了解、风险认知、疫苗接种 状态以及 疫苗接种 的障碍。医院清洁工在医疗保健感染控制中处于前线地位,但仍然是乙型肝炎研究中研究最少的职业群体之一。

对尼日利亚瓜瓦拉达阿布贾大学教学医院通过系统随机抽样选出的 145 名清洁工进行了一项描述性横断面研究。共收回 145 份问卷,平均年龄为 40.63 ± 9.96 岁,其中 73.1% 为女性。
核心方法
使用结构化、访谈员管理的问卷收集数据,涵盖社会人口学特征、乙型肝炎知识、风险认知、疫苗接种 状态以及 疫苗接种 障碍。使用 SPSS 23 版进行分析,使用描述性统计和卡方检验。
主要结果

科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解决系统性制度缺陷的重要性,例如缺乏可执行的 疫苗接种 政策和结构化的定向计划,以加强医院清洁工的乙型肝炎预防工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,由于缺乏详细的统计分析以及基于问卷的数据可能存在自我报告偏差,因此证据强度中等。

Knowledge of Hepatitis B Infection Vaccination Status, Risk Perception and Barriers among Hospital Cleaners at the University of Abuja Teaching Hospital Gwagwalada, Nigeria

Authors: Adam Omotosho Abdulquadri, Biyaya Nwankwo, Saheed Ayodeji Adekola, Doyinsola Faiza Akano, Hassanat Taiye Aliyu, Al-Ghazali Etudaiye Muhammed
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hospital cleaners occupy a frontline position in healthcare infection control, yet remain among the least studied occupational groups in hepatitis B research. This study assessed knowledge of hepatitis B infection and its vaccine, risk perception, vaccination status, and barriers to vaccination among cleaners at the University of Abuja Teaching Hospital (UATH), Gwagwalada, Nigeria. Methods A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 145 cleaners selected by systematic random sampling. Data were collected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics, hepatitis B knowledge, risk perception, vaccination status, and barriers to vaccination. Analysis was performed with SPSS version 23, using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests. Ethical approval was obtained from the Health Research Ethics Committee of UATH. Results All 145 questionnaires were returned (response rate 100%). Mean age was 40.63 ± 9.96 years; 73.1% were female. Knowledge of hepatitis B was good in 135 participants (93.1%), and knowledge of the hepatitis B vaccine was good in 111 (76.6%). Good risk perception was recorded in 106 participants (73.1%). Despite this, only 49 cleaners (33.8%) had received at least one vaccine dose, and only 19 (13.1%) had completed the full three-dose series. Among those incompletely or unvaccinated, lack of awareness (66.2%), absence of an appropriate hospital vaccination policy (57.9%), inadequate orientation for new staff (53.8%), and unaffordability (37.2%) were the principal barriers identified. Years of work experience (p = 0.005) and age (p = 0.023) were significantly associated with vaccination status. Conclusion Despite generally adequate knowledge and risk awareness, hepatitis B vaccination coverage among hospital cleaners at UATH remains critically low. Systemic institutional shortcomings, particularly the absence of enforceable vaccination policies and structured orientation programs, are more proximate barriers than individual knowledge gaps. Targeted policy reform and workplace vaccination programs are urgently needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess knowledge of hepatitis B infection and its vaccine, risk perception, vaccination status, and barriers to vaccination among hospital cleaners at the University of Abuja Teaching Hospital, Gwagwalada, Nigeria. Hospital cleaners occupy a frontline position in healthcare infection control, yet remain among the least studied occupational groups in hepatitis B research.
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 145 cleaners selected by systematic random sampling at the University of Abuja Teaching Hospital, Gwagwalada, Nigeria. All 145 questionnaires were returned, with a mean age of 40.63 ± 9.96 years and 73.1% of participants being female.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics, hepatitis B knowledge, risk perception, vaccination status, and barriers to vaccination. Analysis was performed with SPSS version 23, using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests.
Main results
Knowledge of hepatitis B was good in 135 participants (93.1%), and knowledge of the hepatitis B vaccine was good in 111 (76.6%). Good risk perception was recorded in 106 participants (73.1%). Despite this, only 49 cleaners (33.8%) had received at least one vaccine dose, and only 19 (13.1%) had completed the full three-dose series.
Interpretation and novelty
Years of work experience (p = 0.005) and age (p = 0.023) were significantly associated with vaccination status. The study highlights the need for targeted policy reform and workplace vaccination programs to improve hepatitis B vaccination coverage.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of addressing systemic institutional shortcomings, such as the absence of enforceable vaccination policies and structured orientation programs, to enhance hepatitis B prevention efforts among hospital cleaners.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis and the potential for self-reporting bias in the questionnaire-based data.

AHB-137(一种未缀合的反义寡核苷酸)在健康受试者和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中的首次人体研究

A First-in-Human study of AHB-137, an unconjugated antisense oligonucleotide, in healthy subjects and patients with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Edward J. Gane, Yao-Chun Hsu, Chi-Yi Chen, Shyamasundaran Kottilil, Eric Lawitz, Ira M. Jacobson, Paul Yien Kwo, Lung-Yi Mak, Wai-Kay Seto, Joel V. Chua
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:AHB-137 是一种新型 ASO,针对所有 HBV mRNA 3' 端附近的保守区域。这项首次人体 1 期研究评估了健康受试者和 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的安全性、耐受性、药代动力学 (PK) 和 抗病毒药物 疗效。方法:40 名健康受试者被随机分为 4 名安慰剂对照单剂量递增剂量(100-450 mg,6:2 AHB-137:安慰剂)队列研究 和 1 名多剂量(MD;300 mg,6:2)队列研究,接受每周皮下注射四次,第 4 天负荷剂量(5 剂)。24 名接受稳定核苷(酸)类似物治疗的病毒抑制的 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者入组:4 名接受开放标签 300 mg MD 队列研究(5 剂),20 名接受两种安慰剂对照 300 mg MD 队列研究(4:1,按基线 HBsAg 分层),在第 11 天接受额外的负荷剂量(6 剂)。结果:治疗 相关不良事件发生在 73% 的健康受试者和 71% 的慢性乙型肝炎患者中,主要是轻度或中度注射部位反应和头痛。没有观察到与 治疗 相关的严重不良事件、停药或死亡。PK 曲线显示吸收迅速(T max 2.96-5.50 小时)、暴露量与剂量成比例、无显着蓄积、终末半衰期长(150-220 小时)和最小的肾排泄。在 CHB 患者中,AHB-137 治疗 导致 HBsAg 快速下降(平均 0.7-1.0 log 10 IU/mL)。HBsAg 消失(结论:在这项 1 期研究中,AHB-137 表现出可接受的安全性、可预测的 PK 以及快速且持久的 HBsAg 降低,支持对 CHB 的剂量和 治疗 持续时间的进一步评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 AHB-137(一种针对所有 HBV mRNA 3' 端附近保守区域的反义寡核苷酸)在健康受试者和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中的安全性、耐受性、药代动力学 (PK) 和 抗病毒药物 疗效。
设计与对象
一项首次人体 1 期研究在 40 名健康受试者和 24 名接受稳定核苷(酸)类似物治疗的病毒抑制、HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中进行,参与者随机分为安慰剂对照 队列研究 和开放标签多剂量 队列研究。
核心方法
健康受试者被随机分为四名安慰剂对照单剂量递增剂量 队列研究 (100-450 mg) 和一名多剂量 队列研究,而 CHB 患者被纳入两名安慰剂对照 300 mg 多剂量 队列研究 和一名开放标签 队列研究,所有患者均在第 4 天或第 11 天接受皮下剂量和负荷剂量。
主要结果
73% 的健康受试者和 71% 的 CHB 患者发生 治疗 相关不良事件,主要是轻度或中度注射部位反应和头痛。PK 曲线显示吸收快、暴露量与剂量成比例、无蓄积、终末半衰期长(150-220 小时)和最小的肾排泄。
解释与创新
作者解释说,AHB-137 表现出可接受的安全性、可预测的 PK 以及快速且持久的 HBsAg 降低,这是新颖的,因为它支持进一步评估 CHB 的剂量和 治疗 持续时间。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现告知 AHB-137 作为 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗剂的潜在用途,强调了其安全性和 抗病毒药物 活性,这可能有助于未来的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小样本量和较短的 治疗 持续时间,但证据充足,因为它基于一项控制良好的 1 期试验,具有明确的安全性和 PK 结果。

A First-in-Human study of AHB-137, an unconjugated antisense oligonucleotide, in healthy subjects and patients with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Edward J. Gane, Yao-Chun Hsu, Chi-Yi Chen, Shyamasundaran Kottilil, Eric Lawitz, Ira M. Jacobson, Paul Yien Kwo, Lung-Yi Mak, Wai-Kay Seto, Joel V. Chua
Original abstract (English)
Background & Aims: AHB-137 is a novel ASO targeting a conserved region near the 3′ end of all HBV mRNA. This first-in-human phase 1 study evaluated the safety, tolerability, pharmacokinetics (PK), and antiviral efficacy in healthy subjects and chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients. Methods: Forty healthy subjects were randomized into four placebo-controlled single ascending dose (100-450 mg, 6:2 AHB-137:placebo) cohorts and one multiple-dose (MD; 300 mg, 6:2) cohort receiving four weekly subcutaneous doses with a Day 4 loading dose (5 doses). Twenty-four virally suppressed, HBeAg-negative CHB patients on stable nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy were enrolled: four in an open-label 300-mg MD cohort (5 doses) and 20 in two placebo-controlled 300-mg MD cohorts (4:1, stratified by baseline HBsAg), receiving an additional loading dose on Day 11 (6 doses). Results: Treatment-related adverse events occurred in 73% of healthy subjects and 71% of CHB patients and were primarily mild or moderate injection-site reactions and headaches. No treatment-related serious adverse events, discontinuations, or deaths were observed. PK profiles showed rapid absorption (T max 2.96-5.50 h), dose-proportional exposure, no significant accumulation, long terminal half-life (150-220 h), and minimal renal excretion. In CHB patients, AHB-137 treatment led to a rapid HBsAg decline (mean 0.7-1.0 log 10 IU/mL). HBsAg loss ( Conclusions: In this Phase 1 study, AHB-137 demonstrated an acceptable safety profile, predictable PK, and rapid and prolonged HBsAg reductions, supporting further evaluation of dosing and treatment duration in CHB.

Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the safety, tolerability, pharmacokinetics (PK), and antiviral efficacy of AHB-137, an antisense oligonucleotide targeting a conserved region near the 3′ end of all HBV mRNA, in healthy subjects and patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
A first-in-human phase 1 study was conducted in 40 healthy subjects and 24 virally suppressed, HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B patients on stable nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, with participants randomized into placebo-controlled cohorts and an open-label multiple-dose cohort.
Core methods
Healthy subjects were randomized into four placebo-controlled single ascending dose cohorts (100-450 mg) and one multiple-dose cohort, while CHB patients were enrolled into two placebo-controlled 300-mg multiple-dose cohorts and one open-label cohort, all receiving subcutaneous doses with a loading dose on Day 4 or Day 11.
Main results
Treatment-related adverse events occurred in 73% of healthy subjects and 71% of CHB patients, primarily mild or moderate injection-site reactions and headaches. PK profiles showed rapid absorption, dose-proportional exposure, no accumulation, long terminal half-life (150-220 h), and minimal renal excretion.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that AHB-137 demonstrated an acceptable safety profile, predictable PK, and rapid and prolonged HBsAg reductions, which is novel as it supports further evaluation of dosing and treatment duration in CHB.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the potential use of AHB-137 as a therapeutic agent for chronic hepatitis B, highlighting its safety and antiviral activity, which could contribute to future treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small sample size and short treatment duration, but the evidence is strong as it is based on a well-controlled phase 1 trial with clear safety and PK outcomes.

外周血免疫基因组细胞因子受体特征(CXCR1、IL11RA、IL13RA2、CD19)预测 HBV 相关肝硬化:公共 PBMC 转录组挖掘和列线图开发

Peripheral Blood Immunogenomic Cytokine-receptor Signature (CXCR1, IL11RA, IL13RA2, CD19) Predicts HBV-related Cirrhosis: Public PBMC Transcriptome Mining and Nomogram Development
作者： Guifang Jiang, Junping Pan, Jiang Guifang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 进展为肝硬化与免疫失调和细胞因子信号传导改变有关,但用于风险分层的非侵入性免疫生物标志物仍然很少。该研究通过寻找基于外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 的细胞因子基因特征来预测 HBV 相关肝硬化,从而弥补了知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 GSE114783 微阵列数据集,以确定 CHB 和 HBV 相关肝硬化之间的差异表达基因。它使用免疫相关候选基因选择,整合策划的免疫资源和 KEGG 细胞因子-细胞因子受体相互作用途径。
核心方法
差异表达分析确定了 3169 个区分肝硬化和 CHB 的基因,其中优先考虑了 86 个免疫/细胞因子相关基因。使用 LASSO 回归和多变量逻辑回归构建预测模型,然后进行接受者操作特征分析、留一法交叉验证和列线图开发来评估性能。
主要结果
所构建的模型达到的曲线下面积(AUC)为0.935(95%置信区间,0.882-0.988);在最优截点0.832时,敏感性为88.6%,特异性为84.3%。留一法交叉验证支持模型性能稳健,且列线图显示预测风险与观察风险间具有良好的一致性。
解释与创新
作者将 4 基因特征(CXCR1、IL11RA、IL13RA2、CD19)解释为捕获与慢性炎症和纤维发生相关的关键免疫轴,代表了一种用于对 HBV 相关肝硬化进行免疫学知情风险分层的无创工具。
科研与公卫意义
基于 PBMC 的免疫细胞因子受体基因特征可能支持 HBV 相关肝硬化的免疫监测和早期干预策略,为临床实践中的风险分层提供无创方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖单一公共数据集且缺乏前瞻性、多队列验证而受到限制,需要进一步研究以确认普遍性和临床实用性。

Peripheral Blood Immunogenomic Cytokine-receptor Signature (CXCR1, IL11RA, IL13RA2, CD19) Predicts HBV-related Cirrhosis: Public PBMC Transcriptome Mining and Nomogram Development

Authors: Guifang Jiang, Junping Pan, Jiang Guifang
Original abstract (English)
Progression from chronic hepatitis B (CHB) to cirrhosis is closely associated with immune dysregulation and altered cytokine signaling, yet effective noninvasive immune biomarkers remain limited. This study aimed to develop a peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC)-based cytokine gene signature for predicting HBV-related cirrhosis. The GSE114783 microarray dataset was analyzed to identify differentially expressed genes between CHB and cirrhosis. Immune-related candidate genes were selected by integrating curated immune resources and the Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes cytokine-cytokine receptor interaction pathway. Least absolute shrinkage and selection operator regression and multivariable logistic regression were used to construct the predictive model. Receiver operating characteristic analysis, leave-one-out cross-validation, and nomogram development were performed to evaluate model performance and support clinical translation. Differential expression analysis identified 3169 genes distinguishing cirrhosis from CHB, from which 86 immune/cytokine-related genes were prioritized. LASSO selected a parsimonious 4-gene signature-CXCR1, IL11RA, IL13RA2, and CD19-capturing key immune axes relevant to chronic inflammation and fibrogenesis (chemokine receptor signaling, IL-11/IL-13 receptor pathways, and B-cell–associated immunity). The resulting model achieved an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.935 (95% CI, 0.882-0.988); at an optimal cutoff of 0.832, sensitivity was 88.6% and specificity 84.3%. LOOCV supported robust performance, and the nomogram demonstrated good agreement between predicted and observed risk. A PBMC-based immune cytokine-receptor gene signature (CXCR1/IL11RA/IL13RA2/CD19) provides a noninvasive tool for immunologically informed risk stratification of HBV-related cirrhosis and may support immune monitoring and early intervention strategies. Prospective, multicohort validation and mechanistic studies are warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) progression to cirrhosis is linked to immune dysregulation and altered cytokine signaling, yet noninvasive immune biomarkers for risk stratification remain scarce. The study addressed the knowledge gap by seeking a peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC)-based cytokine gene signature to predict HBV-related cirrhosis.
Design and population
The study analyzed the GSE114783 microarray dataset to identify differentially expressed genes between CHB and HBV-related cirrhosis. It used immune-related candidate gene selection integrating curated immune resources and the KEGG cytokine-cytokine receptor interaction pathway.
Core methods
Differential expression analysis identified 3169 genes distinguishing cirrhosis from CHB, from which 86 immune/cytokine-related genes were prioritized. LASSO regression and multivariable logistic regression were used to construct a predictive model, followed by receiver operating characteristic analysis, leave-one-out cross-validation, and nomogram development to evaluate performance.
Main results
The resulting model achieved an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.935 (95% CI, 0.882-0.988); at an optimal cutoff of 0.832, sensitivity was 88.6% and specificity 84.3%. Leave-one-out cross-validation supported robust performance, and the nomogram demonstrated good agreement between predicted and observed risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the 4-gene signature (CXCR1, IL11RA, IL13RA2, CD19) as capturing key immune axes relevant to chronic inflammation and fibrogenesis, representing a noninvasive tool for immunologically informed risk stratification of HBV-related cirrhosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The PBMC-based immune cytokine-receptor gene signature may support immune monitoring and early intervention strategies for HBV-related cirrhosis, offering a noninvasive approach to risk stratification in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single public dataset and the absence of prospective, multicohort validation, warranting further research to confirm generalizability and clinical utility.

基于 乙型肝炎病毒 前基因组 RNA 的小基因转录本利用多嘧啶束结合蛋白调节 NXF1-NXT1 核输出途径

A Hepatitis B virus pregenomic RNA based minigene transcript utilizes polypyrimidine tract binding protein regulated NXF1-NXT1 nuclear export pathway
作者： Debasis Pradhan, Sagarmoy Ghosh
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒 是一种副逆转录病毒,属于嗜肝DNA病毒科成员,含有部分双链DNA 基因组,由宿主 RNA 聚合酶II 转录,生成含有内含子的前基因组RNA (pgRNA)。利用经典 宿主 途径的 pgRNA 核输出仍然是病毒成功复制的核心,因为它是由细胞质核衣壳内的病毒 P 蛋白逆转录的。我们通过定量 RNA-FISH 提供了聚嘧啶束结合蛋白 (PTB) 介导的基于 pgRNA 的含内含子前体 mRNA (CPS-PRE) 核输出抑制的直接微观证据。我们还通过 RNA 免疫沉淀实验证明,CPS-PRE 小基因转录物与 NXF1-NXT1 途径的成员稳定地相互作用,并且没有看到与核蛋白 CRM1 的交联。我们的数据强化了 PTB 在前 mRNA 核输出中主动保留含内含子转录物的一个新方面,并直接显示了 pgRNA 成分与 NXF1-NXT1 异二聚体相互作用的能力。类似内容被其他人查看 缩写 - ALY/REF: - ALYREF 输出因子 - ARS2: - 砷抗性蛋白 2 - CRM1: - 染色体区域维护 1 - DAPI: - 4’,6-二脒基-2-苯基吲哚 - eIF4A3: - 真核翻译起始因子 - ELAVL1: - ELAV 样 RNA 结合蛋白 1 - FISH: - 荧光原位杂交 - FITC: - 异硫氰酸荧光素 - 5-IAF: - 5-(碘乙酰胺)荧光素 - MLP1: - 肌球蛋白样蛋白 1 - NTF2: - 核转运因子 2 - NXF1: - 核 RNA 输出因子 1 - NXT1: - NTF2 样输出因子 1 - Pml39: - 前 mRNA 渗漏蛋白 39 - THO 复合物: - THOC 复合物的基本成分- Tpr: - 易位启动子区域,核篮蛋白 - TREX: -...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 复制依赖于其前基因组 RNA (pgRNA) 的核输出,但介导含内含子 pgRNA 转录物输出的 宿主 途径仍未完全确定。该研究解决了关于包含内含子的 HBV pgRNA 如何访问 宿主 核输出机制的知识差距,尽管 宿主 机制通常保留未剪接的转录本。
设计与对象
该研究使用细胞培养模型来检查含有 HBV 转录后调控元件的基于 pgRNA 的小基因转录本 (CPS-PRE) 的核输出。该实验系统涉及人源细胞,并采用 RNA 荧光原位杂交 (RNA-FISH) 和 RNA 免疫沉淀来可视化和量化转录本定位和蛋白质相互作用。
核心方法
RNA-FISH 用于可视化 CPS-PRE 小基因转录本的亚细胞定位,并进行 RNA 免疫沉淀测定来评估这些转录本与 宿主 核输出因子(特别是 NXF1-NXT1 通路成员)之间的相互作用。评估了聚嘧啶束结合蛋白(PTB)在调节含内含子转录本的核输出中的作用。
主要结果
显微镜检查显示,PTB 抑制含有内含子的 CPS-PRE 小基因转录本的核输出,导致其核保留。RNA 免疫沉淀证明 CPS-PRE 转录本与 NXF1-NXT1 输出机制存在稳定的相互作用,而未检测到与核转运蛋白 CRM1 途径的相互作用。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了一种新的调节机制,其中 PTB 主动将含有内含子的 pgRNA 衍生转录物保留在细胞核中,抵消 NXF1-NXT1 介导的输出途径。这表明,尽管存在内含子,但 HBV pgRNA 成分仍可以参与 宿主 NXF1-NXT1 输出系统,挑战了未剪接的病毒 RNA 被排除在该途径之外的假设。
科研与公卫意义
该研究将 PTB 和 NXF1-NXT1 通路确定为治疗干预的潜在目标,通过损害 pgRNA 核输出来破坏 HBV 复制。这些见解可能会为调节 宿主 病毒相互作用的策略提供信息,并改进 抗病毒药物 对抗慢性 HBV 感染的方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外细胞培养模型,并未评估原代人肝细胞或体内的机制。虽然证据强烈支持 PTB 介导的核滞留和 NXF1-NXT1 相互作用,但自然 HBV 感染的普遍性以及对病毒复制的影响需要在生理相关系统中进一步验证。

A Hepatitis B virus pregenomic RNA based minigene transcript utilizes polypyrimidine tract binding protein regulated NXF1-NXT1 nuclear export pathway

Authors: Debasis Pradhan, Sagarmoy Ghosh
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus, a pararetrovirus and member of hepadnaviridae family, harbours a partially double-stranded DNA genome that is transcribed by the host RNA polymerase II to generate an intron-containing pregenomic RNA (pgRNA). Nuclear export of pgRNA utilizing the canonical host pathways remains central to successful virus replication since it is reverse transcribed by the viral P protein within the nucleocapsid in cytoplasm. We provide direct microscopic evidence of Polypyrimidine tract binding protein (PTB) mediated inhibition of nuclear export of a pgRNA based intron-containing pre-mRNA (CPS-PRE) with quantitative RNA-FISH. We also demonstrate through RNA immunoprecipitation experiments that CPS-PRE minigene transcripts stably interact with members of NXF1-NXT1 pathway and no crosslinking with karyopherin CRM1 could be seen. Our data reinforces a novel aspect of PTB in pre-mRNA nuclear export for active retention of intron-containing transcripts and directly shows the ability of pgRNA components to interact with NXF1-NXT1 heterodimer. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) replication depends on nuclear export of its pregenomic RNA (pgRNA), yet the host pathways mediating export of intron-containing pgRNA transcripts remain incompletely defined. The study addresses a knowledge gap regarding how HBV pgRNA, which contains introns, accesses the host nuclear export machinery despite host mechanisms that typically retain unspliced transcripts.
Design and population
The study used cell culture models to examine nuclear export of a pgRNA-based minigene transcript (CPS-PRE) containing HBV post-transcriptional regulatory elements. The experimental system involved human-derived cells and employed RNA fluorescence in situ hybridization (RNA-FISH) and RNA immunoprecipitation to visualize and quantify transcript localization and protein interactions.
Core methods
RNA-FISH was used to visualize the subcellular localization of CPS-PRE minigene transcripts, and RNA immunoprecipitation assays were performed to assess interactions between these transcripts and host nuclear export factors, specifically members of the NXF1-NXT1 pathway. Polypyrimidine tract-binding protein (PTB) was evaluated for its role in modulating nuclear export of the intron-containing transcripts.
Main results
Microscopy showed that PTB inhibits nuclear export of the intron-containing CPS-PRE minigene transcript, resulting in its nuclear retention. RNA immunoprecipitation demonstrated stable interaction of CPS-PRE transcripts with the NXF1-NXT1 export machinery, while no interaction was detected with the karyopherin CRM1 pathway.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a novel regulatory mechanism in which PTB actively retains intron-containing pgRNA-derived transcripts in the nucleus, counteracting the NXF1-NXT1 mediated export pathway. This demonstrates that HBV pgRNA components can engage the host NXF1-NXT1 export system despite the presence of introns, challenging the assumption that unspliced viral RNAs are excluded from this pathway.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies PTB and the NXF1-NXT1 pathway as potential targets for therapeutic intervention to disrupt HBV replication by impairing pgRNA nuclear export. These insights may inform strategies to modulate host-virus interactions and improve antiviral approaches against chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro cell culture models and does not evaluate the mechanism in primary human hepatocytes or in vivo. While the evidence strongly supports PTB-mediated nuclear retention and NXF1-NXT1 interaction, the generalizability to natural HBV infection and the impact on viral replication require further validation in physiologically relevant systems.
