A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯治疗的晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化患者出现低水平病毒血症的危险因素

Risk factors for low-level viremia in tenofovir disoproxil fumarate-treated chronic hepatitis B patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis
作者： Run-Ling Zhang, Hai-Ying Zhang, Yu-Yuan Jia, Zhen-Yu Wang, Feng Liu, Hong-Song Chen, Hui-Ying Rao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
诊断为慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)并发晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化的患者面临着重大的疾病进展风险,而针对该患者组低水平病毒血症(LLV)准确预后指标的临床研究仍然相对较少。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 141 名患有晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化的 CHB 患者,他们接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 治疗,根据第 48 周时的 HBV DNA 水平将患者分为 LLV 组和完全病毒学缓解 (CVR) 组。
核心方法
进行单变量和多变量 Logistic 回归分析以确定 LLV 发生的危险因素,LLV 定义为 HBV DNA 水平为 20-2000 IU/mL,CVR 定义为 HBV DNA 水平低于 20 IU/mL。
主要结果
A total of 141 patients were included, with 17.0% (24/141) developing LLV, and multivariate logistic regression confirmed that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL at baseline was associated with LLV (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019).
解释与创新
作者解释说,对于接受 TDF 治疗的晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化 CHB 患者,HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL 是 LLV 的高危因素,为这一特定人群提供了一个新的预后指标。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为患有晚期纤维化的 CHB 患者的临床监测策略提供信息,从而可以对 LLV 及其相关并发症(如加速肝纤维化进展和肝细胞癌)风险较高的患者进行早期干预。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性设计和单中心研究,证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性,尽管样本量和统计分析对已确定的风险因素提供了合理的信心。

Risk factors for low-level viremia in tenofovir disoproxil fumarate-treated chronic hepatitis B patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis

Authors: Run-Ling Zhang, Hai-Ying Zhang, Yu-Yuan Jia, Zhen-Yu Wang, Feng Liu, Hong-Song Chen, Hui-Ying Rao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Patients diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) complicated by advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis face a significant risk of disease progression. Despite first-line nucleos(t)ide analog therapy, some patients continues to exhibit low-level viremia (LLV). Furthermore, LLV is closely associated with accelerated liver fibrosis progression and a higher likelihood of hepatocellular carcinoma. Clinical studies on accurate prognostic indicators for LLV in this patient group are still comparatively rare. AIM To identify the high-risk factors linked to the occurrence of LLV among CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective study enrolled 141 CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis who received tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF). Patients were allocated into two groups according to their virological response at week 48 after treatment initiation: LLV, defined as hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA levels of 20-2000 IU/mL, and complete virological response (CVR), defined as HBV DNA levels below 20 IU/mL. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analysis were carried out to determine risk factors for LLV development. RESULTS A total of 141 patients were included in this study, among whom 17.0% (24/141) developed LLV. Significant differences were identified between the LLV and CVR groups regarding baseline HBV DNA, HBV RNA, hepatitis B e antigen positivity, HBV DNA ≥ 5.0 Log10 IU/mL, 6.0 Log10 IU/mL, 7.0 Log10 IU/mL, HBV RNA ≥ 5.0 Log10 copies/mL, and baseline alpha-fetoprotein. Multivariate logistic regression confirmed that that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019) at baseline was associated with the occurrence of LLV. CONCLUSION In TDF-treated CHB with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated liver cirrhosis, HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL is a high-risk factor associated with LLV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Patients diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) complicated by advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis face a significant risk of disease progression, and clinical studies on accurate prognostic indicators for low-level viremia (LLV) in this patient group are still comparatively rare.
Design and population
This retrospective study enrolled 141 CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis who received tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF), with patients allocated into LLV and complete virological response (CVR) groups based on HBV DNA levels at week 48.
Core methods
Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analysis were carried out to determine risk factors for LLV development, with LLV defined as HBV DNA levels of 20-2000 IU/mL and CVR as HBV DNA levels below 20 IU/mL.
Main results
A total of 141 patients were included, with 17.0% (24/141) developing LLV, and multivariate logistic regression confirmed that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL at baseline was associated with LLV (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL is a high-risk factor for LLV in TDF-treated CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis, providing a novel prognostic indicator for this specific population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform clinical monitoring strategies for CHB patients with advanced fibrosis, allowing for earlier intervention in those at higher risk of LLV and its associated complications like accelerated liver fibrosis progression and hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective design and single-center study, which may limit generalizability, though the sample size and statistical analyses provide reasonable confidence in the identified risk factor.

治疗乙型肝炎和疟疾的多机制 治疗 方法

Multi-Mechanistic Treatment Approaches for Hepatitis B and Malaria
作者： Mojisola Ogunnaike
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎 (HBV) 和疟疾是全球传染病面临的最大挑战。两者都是全球健康和社会经济面临的重大挑战,导致疾病和过早死亡的风险更高。治愈和疗法不存在且有限。延长 治疗 方案通常会导致患者依从性不佳和耐药。所有这些都强调需要能够实现持久病原体控制的新治疗方法。为此,我们研究了组合长效(LA)治疗方法来改善慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)感染的治疗结果的可行性,慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)感染仍然是进行性肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要危险因素。尽管现有的核苷(酸)类似物疗法可以有效抑制病毒复制,但它们需要长期每日给药。我们采用了三管齐下的治疗策略,使用替诺福韦(TFV)(一种有效的核苷(酸)类似物)和替唑尼特(TIZ)(一种先天免疫增强剂)的LA前药制剂,以维持病毒抑制并改善免疫稳态。我们在 CHB 小鼠模型中共同施用 LA 药物(称为 M5TFV 和 M2TIZ)。由此产生的多机制策略提供了持续的 抗病毒药物 抑制并减少炎症和纤维化。论文的另一部分重点是改善现有疟疾疗法在生物利用度、半衰期、耐药性和功效方面的局限性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于缺乏治愈性疗法以及长期核苷(酸)类似物 治疗 的局限性,慢性乙型肝炎 病毒(HBV)感染仍然是全球主要的健康挑战。该研究解决了对创新治疗策略的需求,这些策略可以实现持久的病毒抑制并改善临床结果。
设计与对象
该研究在 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 小鼠模型中采用了三管齐下的治疗策略,使用替诺福韦 (TFV) 和替唑尼特 (TIZ) 的长效 (LA) 前药制剂。该研究的重点是联合使用 LA 药物(M5TFV 和 M2TIZ)来评估 抗病毒药物 的持续效果。
核心方法
作者使用替诺福韦和替唑尼特的长效前药制剂(以 M5TFV 和 M2TIZ 形式给药),在 CHB 小鼠模型中实现了 抗病毒药物 的持续抑制。该研究评估了多机制策略对病毒抑制、炎症和纤维化的影响。
主要结果
多机制策略在 CHB 小鼠模型中提供了持续的 抗病毒药物 抑制并减少了炎症和纤维化。该研究报告称,组合长效治疗方法改善了结果。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为多机制、长效治疗策略可以增强慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的病毒抑制和免疫稳态的证据。新颖之处在于针对病毒复制和免疫增强的 LA 前药的组合使用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过解决与延长治疗方案相关的依从性挑战和耐药性,治疗 有可能改善慢性 HBV 感染的结果。这种方法可能会为未来持久病原体控制的治疗开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖小鼠模型而受到限制,该模型可能无法完全重现人类乙型肝炎病毒感染动态。由于研究的临床前性质和缺乏人体数据,证据强度中等。

Multi-Mechanistic Treatment Approaches for Hepatitis B and Malaria

Authors: Mojisola Ogunnaike
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B (HBV) and malaria are highest global challenges for infectious diseases. Both are substantial global health and socioeconomic challenges for higher risks of disease and early death. Cure and therapies are absent and limited. Prolonged treatment regimens are mandated with common suboptimal patient adherence and drug resistance. All underscore the need for newer therapeutic approaches that can achieve durable pathogen control. To this end, we examined the feasibility of combinatorial long-acting (LA) therapeutic approaches to improve the treatment outcomes of chronic HBV (CHB) infection which remains a major risk factor for progressive cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Although available nucleos(t)ide analog therapies effectively suppress viral replication, they require long-term daily administration. We employed a three-pronged therapeutic strategy using LA prodrug formulations of tenofovir (TFV), a potent nucleos(t)ide analog, and tizoxanide (TIZ), an innate immune enhancer, to sustain viral suppression and improve immune homeostasis. We co-administered LA medicines (termed M5TFV and M2TIZ) in a CHB mouse model. The resulting multi-mechanistic strategy provided sustained antiviral suppression and reduced inflammation and fibrosis. An added part of the dissertation focused on improving limitations in bioavailability, half-life, resistance, and efficacy for existing malaria therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health challenge due to the absence of curative therapies and the limitations of long-term nucleos(t)ide analog treatment. The study addresses the need for innovative therapeutic strategies that can achieve durable viral suppression and improve clinical outcomes.
Design and population
The study employed a three-pronged therapeutic strategy using long-acting (LA) prodrug formulations of tenofovir (TFV) and tizoxanide (TIZ) in a chronic hepatitis B (CHB) mouse model. The research focused on co-administering LA medicines (M5TFV and M2TIZ) to evaluate sustained antiviral effects.
Core methods
The authors used long-acting prodrug formulations of tenofovir and tizoxanide, administered as M5TFV and M2TIZ, to achieve sustained antiviral suppression in a CHB mouse model. The study evaluated the multi-mechanistic strategy for its impact on viral suppression, inflammation, and fibrosis.
Main results
The multi-mechanistic strategy provided sustained antiviral suppression and reduced inflammation and fibrosis in the CHB mouse model. The study reported improved outcomes with the combinatorial long-acting therapeutic approach.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that a multi-mechanistic, long-acting therapeutic strategy can enhance viral suppression and immune homeostasis in chronic HBV infection. The novelty lies in the combinatorial use of LA prodrugs targeting both viral replication and immune enhancement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest potential for improved treatment outcomes in chronic HBV infection by addressing adherence challenges and drug resistance associated with prolonged regimens. This approach may inform future therapeutic development for durable pathogen control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a mouse model, which may not fully recapitulate human HBV infection dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the preclinical nature of the research and the absence of human data.

乙型肝炎病毒 预防和筛查的知识、态度和预防实践:尼日利亚阿布贾 AMAC 的社区研究

Knowledge, Attitude and Preventive Practices Towards Hepatitis B Virus Prevention and Screening: A Community-Based Study in AMAC, Abuja, Nigeria
作者： A. E. Ebireri, E. C. Adegor, K. Babagana
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 在全球范围内构成了重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在尼日利亚等发展中国家。本研究评估了阿布贾市地区委员会 (AMAC) 居民有关 HBV 预防和筛查的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。方法:对 AMAC 的 401 名居民进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。参与者是从城乡家庭中系统挑选的。使用结构化、封闭式问卷收集数据,该问卷涵盖社会人口学特征和有关 HBV 的 KAP。使用 SPSS 25 版进行描述性和推理性分析。结果:受访者中,51.6% 为女性,48.4% 为男性,其中大多数 (60.1%) 居住在城市地区。总体知晓率为56.6%,对乙型肝炎病毒传播的了解及预防存在显着差距。对乙型肝炎预防的态度主要是积极的(68.1%),强烈支持 疫苗接种 和健康做法。实践率为66.4%,表明疫苗接种等预防措施的遵守情况良好,避免危险行为。在知识、态度和实践水平之间观察到显着的正相关(知识-态度:r = .656,p < .01;态度-实践:r = .616,p < .01)。与较高 KAP 分数显着相关的因素包括教育水平、婚姻状况和年龄。结论:研究结果揭示了乙型肝炎相关知识、态度和实践方面的差距。高等教育和积极的婚姻生活经历有助于提高意识和积极主动的健康行为。公共卫生干预措施必须通过促进全面教育和针对性的宣传来解决这些差异,以改善乙型肝炎的预防和筛查结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 在全球范围内构成了重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在尼日利亚等发展中国家。本研究评估了阿布贾市地区委员会 (AMAC) 居民有关 HBV 预防和筛查的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
对 AMAC 的 401 名居民进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。参与者是从城乡家庭中系统挑选的。总体知晓率为56.6%,对乙型肝炎病毒传播的了解及预防存在显着差距。
核心方法
使用结构化、封闭式问卷收集数据,该问卷涵盖社会人口学特征和有关 HBV 的 KAP。使用 SPSS 25 版进行描述性和推理性分析。与较高 KAP 分数显着相关的因素包括教育水平、婚姻状况和年龄。
主要结果
受访者中,女性占 51.6%,男性占 48.4%,其中大多数(60.1%)居住在城市地区。在知识、态度和实践水平之间观察到显着的正相关(知识-态度:r = .656,p < .01;态度-实践:r = .616,p < .01)。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了乙肝相关知识、态度和实践方面的差距。高等教育和积极的婚姻生活经历有助于提高意识和积极主动的健康行为。
科研与公卫意义
公共卫生干预措施必须通过促进全面教育和针对性的宣传来解决这些差异,以改善乙型肝炎的预防和筛查结果。该研究强调了解决知识差距和社会经济因素对于加强乙型肝炎预防工作的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。证据强度中等,因为该研究基于自我报告的数据,不包括对具体干预措施或结果的详细分析。

Knowledge, Attitude and Preventive Practices Towards Hepatitis B Virus Prevention and Screening: A Community-Based Study in AMAC, Abuja, Nigeria

Authors: A. E. Ebireri, E. C. Adegor, K. Babagana
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) poses a significant public health challenge globally, particularly in developing countries such as Nigeria. This study assessed the knowledge, attitude, and practice (KAP) of residents in the Abuja Municipal Area Council (AMAC) regarding HBV prevention and screening. Methods: A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 401 residents of AMAC. Participants were systematically selected from households in urban and rural areas. Data were collected using a structured, closed-ended questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics and KAP regarding HBV. Descriptive and inferential analyses were performed using SPSS version 25. Results: Of the respondents, 51.6% were female and 48.4% were male, with the majority (60.1%) residing in urban areas. The overall knowledge rate was 56.6%, with notable gaps in understanding HBV transmission and prevention. Attitude toward HBV prevention was predominantly positive (68.1%), with strong support for vaccination and healthy practices. The practice rate was 66.4%, indicating good adherence to preventive measures such as vaccination and avoidance of risky behaviors. Significant positive correlations were observed among knowledge, attitude, and practice levels (knowledge-attitude: r = .656, p < .01; attitude-practice: r = .616, p < .01). Factors significantly associated with higher KAP scores included education level, marital status, and age. Conclusion: The findings reveal gaps in HBV-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices. Higher education and positive marital life experiences contribute to better awareness and proactive health behaviors. Public health interventions must address these disparities by promoting comprehensive education and tailored outreach to improve HBV prevention and screening outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) poses a significant public health challenge globally, particularly in developing countries such as Nigeria. This study assessed the knowledge, attitude, and practice (KAP) of residents in the Abuja Municipal Area Council (AMAC) regarding HBV prevention and screening.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 401 residents of AMAC. Participants were systematically selected from households in urban and rural areas. The overall knowledge rate was 56.6%, with notable gaps in understanding HBV transmission and prevention.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured, closed-ended questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics and KAP regarding HBV. Descriptive and inferential analyses were performed using SPSS version 25. Factors significantly associated with higher KAP scores included education level, marital status, and age.
Main results
Of the respondents, 51.6% were female and 48.4% were male, with the majority (60.1%) residing in urban areas. Significant positive correlations were observed among knowledge, attitude, and practice levels (knowledge-attitude: r = .656, p < .01; attitude-practice: r = .616, p < .01).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal gaps in HBV-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices. Higher education and positive marital life experiences contribute to better awareness and proactive health behaviors.
Scientific and public-health significance
Public health interventions must address these disparities by promoting comprehensive education and tailored outreach to improve HBV prevention and screening outcomes. The study highlights the importance of addressing knowledge gaps and socioeconomic factors to enhance HBV prevention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence strength is moderate as the study is based on self-reported data and does not include detailed analysis of specific interventions or outcomes.

宏基因组测序在尼日利亚未分化发热性疾病的横断面临床 队列研究 中检测到病毒和细菌

Metagenomic sequencing detects viruses and bacteria in a cross-sectional clinical cohort of undifferentiated febrile illness in Nigeria
作者： Grace J. Vaziri, Julia C. Pritchard, Jillian I Howard, Grace E. Stamm, David H O'Connor, Christina M. Newman, Matthew T. Aliota, Asabe Dzikwi-Emennaa, David H. O’Connor
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过探索宏基因组测序在发热性疾病病例中识别病原体的效用,解决当前诊断工具在资源匮乏环境中的局限性。这一背景凸显了西非的诊断能力不足,该地区感染性发热流行但诊断困难。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究在尼日利亚高原州乔斯进行,涉及 343 名患者,其中许多人出现不明原因发烧。该研究重点关注这些患者的临床样本,以评估宏基因组测序在病原体检测中的有效性。
核心方法
研究人员采用无偏倚的宏基因组测序来分析患者血清样本中的 RNA。这种方法使他们能够识别病毒和细菌遗传物质,包括与发热性疾病相关的潜在病原体。
主要结果
11%的样本中检测到五种人类感染病毒,包括乙型肝炎病毒和几种指环病毒。这些病毒的存在,即使不会引起症状,也证明了宏基因组测序方法的可行性。
解释与创新
该研究提供的证据表明,即使在资源匮乏的环境中,宏基因组测序也可以有效识别临床样本中的病原体。这种方法为了解病原体状况和检测新出现的疾病提供了一种新工具。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,宏基因组测序可以增强病原体 监测 的能力,并改善发烧感染原因的识别,这对于诊断资源有限的地区的公共卫生应对措施至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认宏基因组测序的局限性,例如易受污染和假阳性,但为临床样本中的病原体检测提供了一种高可信度的方法。

Metagenomic sequencing detects viruses and bacteria in a cross-sectional clinical cohort of undifferentiated febrile illness in Nigeria

Authors: Grace J. Vaziri, Julia C. Pritchard, Jillian I Howard, Grace E. Stamm, David H O'Connor, Christina M. Newman, Matthew T. Aliota, Asabe Dzikwi-Emennaa, David H. O’Connor
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Molecular and microscopy-based diagnostic capacity is often insufficient or unavailable in places where infectious disease burdens are highest, such as in West Africa. Rapid diagnostic testing (RDT) can provide quick and affordable diagnoses of common infections but is an imperfect solution due to limitations around detecting and dealing with false-negative and false-positive results. An alternative to RDT is unbiased metagenomic sequencing for pathogen surveillance. Here, we present data from unbiased metagenomic sequencing used to identify causes of undiagnosed febrile illness in Jos, Plateau State, Nigeria. Proof of concept for this approach has been demonstrated by several groups who have identified epidemic and endemic viral diseases like Lassa fever, yellow fever, and chikungunya. We show that unbiased deep sequencing and metagenomic analysis can be used to identify RNA viruses in clinical samples. We sequenced RNA from sera of patients (n = 343), many of whom were acutely febrile (76%), in a survey of clinics in Jos. We detected five human-infecting viruses in 39 (11 %) specimens. Among these were hepatitis B virus, human pegivirus, and several anelloviruses. While most of the viruses identified are unlikely to cause clinical symptoms in the patients we sampled, their presence demonstrates the validity of our approach. Additionally, our sequencing data allowed us to identify genetic material from potentially pathogenic bacteria, another possible etiological agent of febrile illness. IMPORTANCE: In low-resource areas, fevers due to infectious pathogens are a major source of illness, but tools for detecting and identifying such pathogens are often limited. Unbiased approaches for identifying genetic material from all potentially infectious organisms in a sample represent an opportunity for discovering sources of fever. Metagenomic sequencing can improve insight into pathogen landscapes in low-resource settings, potentially providing early detection of disease outbreaks. However, unbiased metagenomic sequencing (mNGS) is no panacea; it is susceptible to contamination and false positives. We used mNGS to evaluate serum from >300 Nigerian clinic-goers in Jos, Nigeria, most of whom (>70%) had fevers of unknown origin. Our goal was to understand arbovirus prevalence in Jos, Nigeria, and identify the sources of infection not routinely monitored for at clinics. We detected hepatitis B virus, as well as nonpathogenic anelloviruses. Our study provides insight into the utility and limitations of mNGS for pathogen surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of current diagnostic tools in low-resource settings by exploring the utility of metagenomic sequencing for identifying pathogens in febrile illness cases. The background highlights the insufficient diagnostic capacity in West Africa, where infectious fevers are prevalent but difficult to diagnose.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted in Jos, Plateau State, Nigeria, involving 343 patients, many of whom had fevers of unknown origin. The study focused on clinical samples from these patients to evaluate the effectiveness of metagenomic sequencing in pathogen detection.
Core methods
The researchers employed unbiased metagenomic sequencing to analyze RNA from serum samples of patients. This approach allowed them to identify both viral and bacterial genetic material, including potential pathogens associated with febrile illness.
Main results
Five human-infecting viruses were detected in 11% of the specimens, including hepatitis B virus and several anelloviruses. The presence of these viruses, even if not causing symptoms, demonstrated the feasibility of the metagenomic sequencing approach.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides evidence that metagenomic sequencing can effectively identify pathogens in clinical samples, even in low-resource settings. This approach offers a new tool for understanding the pathogen landscape and detecting emerging diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that metagenomic sequencing can enhance pathogen surveillance and improve the identification of infectious causes of fever, which is critical for public health responses in regions with limited diagnostic resources.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges the limitations of metagenomic sequencing, such as susceptibility to contamination and false positives, but presents a high-confidence approach for pathogen detection in clinical samples.

全球肝硬化住院患者 队列研究 中与 30 天和 90 天再入院相关的因素比较。

Comparison of Factors Associated With 30-Day and 90-Day Readmission Among a Global Cohort of Patients Hospitalised With Cirrhosis.
作者： Ramazan Idilman, Busra Haktaniyan, Ashok Choudhury, Florence Wong, Patrick S Kamath, Qing Xie, Mark Topazian, Peter C Hayes, Mario Reis Alvares-da-Silva, Aldo Torre
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:尚不清楚不同因素是否影响 30 天和 90 天的再入院率。我们在肝硬化住院患者的全球 队列研究 中确定了 90 天 死亡率 的预测因子。方法:在前瞻性入组、非选择性住院的成年肝硬化患者中,比较与出院后 30 天和 90 天再入院、肝移植 (LT) 和 死亡率 相关的变量。结果:我们招募了来自 37 个国家 125 个中心的 4208 名患者。酒精相关性肝病是肝硬化最常见的病因(40%),其次是 乙型肝炎病毒 感染(21%);47% 的患者再次入院,8% 的患者接受肝移植 (LT),23% 的患者在出院后 90 天内死亡。在多变量分析中,国家收入与 30 天和 90 天的再入院率、LT 的接受情况以及出院后 30 和 90 天内的 死亡率 显着相关(所有 p 值 < 0.001)。30 天和 90 天再入院率较高的独立预测因素分别是疾病严重程度 (p < 0.001,p = 0.003)、既往腹水 (p = 0.025,p = 0.016)、既往住院情况 (p < 0.001)、低钠血症 (p < 0.001,p = 0.008) 和住院情况血管加压药的使用(p = 0.002,p = 0.011)。既往静脉曲张出血独立预测较高的 90 天再入院率,而年龄、疾病严重程度、机械通气和院内血管加压药的使用(全部,p < 0.001)增加出院后 30 天和 90 天的死亡几率。此外,入院感染 (p = 0.011) 和院内感染 (p = 0.022) 会增加出院后 90 天的死亡几率。结论:在全球肝硬化住院患者 队列研究 中,高收入国家患者的 30 天和 90 天再入院率最高,同时 死亡率 率较低,LT 发病率 较高。预测 30 天再入院的变量也预测 90 天再入院。了解这些差异对于实现健康公平至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了一个尚未解决的问题,即不同的临床或人口因素是否对全球肝硬化住院患者的 30 天和 90 天再入院有不同的影响。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性、多中心、非选择性 队列研究 研究,招募了 37 个国家 125 个中心的 4208 名成年肝硬化患者。
核心方法
使用多变量分析比较与出院后 30 天和 90 天再入院、肝移植和 死亡率 相关的变量,以确定独立的预测因素。
主要结果
在4208名患者中,47%再次入院,8%接受肝移植,23%在出院后90天内死亡。国家收入水平与 30 天和 90 天再入院、移植和 死亡率 显着相关(所有 p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,30 天再入院的预测因素也预测了 90 天再入院的情况,强调了不同时间范围内的共同风险因素以及与国家收入水平相关的差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对疾病严重程度、既往腹水、低钠血症和感染控制的针对性干预措施可能会减少早期和晚期再入院,特别是在低收入环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的仅抽象证据限制了对方法严谨性、潜在偏差和住院肝硬化人群之外的普遍性的详细评估,需要适度的信心。

Comparison of Factors Associated With 30-Day and 90-Day Readmission Among a Global Cohort of Patients Hospitalised With Cirrhosis.

Authors: Ramazan Idilman, Busra Haktaniyan, Ashok Choudhury, Florence Wong, Patrick S Kamath, Qing Xie, Mark Topazian, Peter C Hayes, Mario Reis Alvares-da-Silva, Aldo Torre
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: It remains unclear whether different factors influence 30- and 90-day readmission rates. We identified predictors of 90-day mortality in a global cohort of hospitalised patients with cirrhosis. METHODS: Variables associated with 30- and 90-day post-discharge readmission, liver transplantation (LT), and mortality were compared in prospectively enrolled, non-electively hospitalised adult patients with cirrhosis. RESULTS: We enrolled 4208 patients from 125 centres across 37 countries. Alcohol-related liver disease was the most common aetiology of cirrhosis (40%), followed by hepatitis B virus infection (21%); 47% were readmitted, 8% received liver transplantation (LT), and 23% died within 90 days post-discharge. In multivariable analysis, country income was significantly associated with both 30- and 90-day readmission rates, the receipt of LT, and mortality within 30 and 90 days post-discharge (all p-values < 0.001). Independent predictors of higher rates of 30- and 90-day readmissions, respectively, were disease severity (p < 0.001, p = 0.003), prior ascites (p = 0.025, p = 0.016), prior hospitalisation (p < 0.001), hyponatremia (p < 0.001, p = 0.008), and in-hospital vasopressor use (p = 0.002, p = 0.011). Prior variceal bleeding independently predicted higher 90-day readmission rates, whereas age, disease severity, mechanical ventilation, and in-hospital vasopressor use (all, p < 0.001) increased odds of 30- and 90-day post-discharge death. Additionally, admission (p = 0.011) and nosocomial (p = 0.022) infections increased the odds of 90-day post-discharge death. CONCLUSION: In a global cohort of hospitalised patients with cirrhosis, patients in high-income countries exhibited the highest rates of 30- and 90-day readmission, alongside lower mortality rates and a higher incidence of LT. Variables that predict 30-day readmission also predicted 90-day readmission. Understanding these disparities is essential for achieving health equity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the unresolved question of whether distinct clinical or demographic factors differentially influence 30-day versus 90-day hospital readmission among patients hospitalised with cirrhosis globally.
Design and population
This was a prospective, multicentre, non-elective cohort study enrolling 4208 adult patients with cirrhosis across 125 centres in 37 countries.
Core methods
Variables associated with 30- and 90-day post-discharge readmission, liver transplantation, and mortality were compared using multivariable analyses to identify independent predictors.
Main results
Among 4208 patients, 47% were readmitted, 8% received liver transplantation, and 23% died within 90 days post-discharge. Country income level was significantly associated with both 30- and 90-day readmission, transplantation, and mortality (all p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that predictors of 30-day readmission also predicted 90-day readmission, highlighting shared risk factors across time horizons and disparities linked to country income levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted interventions addressing disease severity, prior ascites, hyponatremia, and infection control may reduce early and late readmissions, particularly in lower-income settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s abstract-only evidence limits detailed assessment of methodological rigor, potential biases, and generalisability beyond hospitalised cirrhosis populations, warranting moderate confidence.

刺激 STING 通路可抑制 HBV 复制:深入了解肝巨噬细胞的作用。

Stimulation of the STING pathway leads to inhibition of HBV replication: insights into the role of hepatic macrophages.
作者： Mingyuan Zhang, Xiaomei Wang, Hongqin Xu, Hao Meng, Jie Zhou, Xiaoyu Wen, Yingyu Zhang, Jinglan Jin, Qinlong Jin, Guan Huei Lee
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 激活 宿主 免疫系统是治疗乙型肝炎 (HBV) 的一种有前景的策略。干扰素基因刺激剂 (STING) 信号通路充当病毒 DNA 的先天免疫传感器。我们的目的是确定 STING 激活是否可以抑制肝脏中的 HBV 复制。方法 我们评估了 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 和肝活检标本中 STING 的表达。我们在具有持续 HBV 复制的小鼠模型 (rAAV8-HBV1.3) 中检查了 STING 激动剂 DMXAA 对 HBV 复制和 免疫应答 的影响。此外,我们还耗尽了体内巨噬细胞,以评估它们在 DMXAA 的 抗病毒药物 效应中的作用。结果 CHB 患者的 STING 表达在免疫活跃期高于非活跃期或未感染的健康对照,表明 STING 表达升高与免疫激活之间存在关联。在持续 HBV 复制的小鼠模型中,DMXAA 以剂量依赖性方式降低血清 HBV DNA,而重复给药则降低血清 HBV DNA、HBsAg 和 HBeAg 水平,并增强肝脏 免疫应答。肝组织的转录组测序揭示了涉及免疫相关信号通路的 856 个差异表达基因 (DEG)。在肝巨噬细胞耗尽的小鼠中,DMXAA 治疗 后 HBV 病毒学标志物的降低不太明显。结论 STING 通路激活通过上调免疫相关基因的表达增强 免疫应答 抗 HBV 的能力。肝巨噬细胞在 STING 介导的 HBV 复制抑制中发挥重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
摘要 背景 激活 宿主 免疫系统是治疗乙型肝炎 (HBV) 的一种有前景的策略。
设计与对象
我们评估了 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 和肝活检标本中的 STING 表达。
核心方法
肝组织的转录组测序揭示了涉及免疫相关信号通路的 856 个差异表达基因 (DEG)。
主要结果
结果 CHB 患者的 STING 表达在免疫活跃期高于非活跃期或未感染的健康对照,表明 STING 表达升高与免疫激活之间存在关联。
解释与创新
结论 STING 通路激活通过上调免疫相关基因的表达增强 免疫应答 抗 HBV 的能力。
科研与公卫意义
肝巨噬细胞在 STING 介导的 HBV 复制抑制中发挥重要作用。
局限与证据强度
在肝巨噬细胞耗尽的小鼠中,DMXAA 治疗 后 HBV 病毒学标志物的降低不太明显。

Stimulation of the STING pathway leads to inhibition of HBV replication: insights into the role of hepatic macrophages.

Authors: Mingyuan Zhang, Xiaomei Wang, Hongqin Xu, Hao Meng, Jie Zhou, Xiaoyu Wen, Yingyu Zhang, Jinglan Jin, Qinlong Jin, Guan Huei Lee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Activation of the host immune system is a promising strategy for treating hepatitis B (HBV). The stimulator of interferon genes (STING) signaling pathway acts as an innate immune sensor for viral DNA. We aimed to determine whether STING activation can inhibit HBV replication in the liver. Methods We evaluated STING expression in peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) and liver biopsy specimens from patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). We examined the effect of the STING agonist DMXAA on HBV replication and the immune response in a mouse model with persistent HBV replication (rAAV8-HBV1.3). Additionally, we depleted macrophages in vivo to assess their role in DMXAA’s antiviral effects. Results STING expression in CHB patients was higher in the immune-active phase than in the inactive phase or uninfected healthy controls, suggesting an association between elevated STING expression and immune activation. In a mouse model with persistent HBV replication, DMXAA reduced serum HBV DNA in a dose-dependent manner, whereas repeated administration reduced serum HBV DNA, HBsAg, and HBeAg levels and enhanced hepatic immune responses. Transcriptome sequencing of liver tissues revealed 856 differentially expressed genes (DEGs) involved in immune-related signaling pathways. In hepatic macrophage-depleted mice, the reductions in HBV virological markers following DMXAA treatment were less pronounced. Conclusions STING pathway activation enhances the immune response against HBV by upregulating the expression of immune-related genes. Hepatic macrophages play an important role in STING-mediated inhibition of HBV replication.

Question and background
Abstract Background Activation of the host immune system is a promising strategy for treating hepatitis B (HBV).
Design and population
We evaluated STING expression in peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) and liver biopsy specimens from patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB).
Core methods
Transcriptome sequencing of liver tissues revealed 856 differentially expressed genes (DEGs) involved in immune-related signaling pathways.
Main results
Results STING expression in CHB patients was higher in the immune-active phase than in the inactive phase or uninfected healthy controls, suggesting an association between elevated STING expression and immune activation.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions STING pathway activation enhances the immune response against HBV by upregulating the expression of immune-related genes.
Scientific and public-health significance

第 6 年的 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平并不能预测长期接受恩替卡韦治疗的肝硬化患者的 HCC 风险。

HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels at year 6 do not predict HCC risk in patients with cirrhosis on long-term entecavir therapy.
作者： Hung-Hsuan Pan, Tung-Hung Su, Tsung-Hui Hu, Wan-Long Chuang, Wei-Wen Su, Cheng-Kuan Lin, Cheng-Yuan Peng, Chia-Chi Wang, Yao-Chun Hsu, Yi-Wen Huang
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:治疗 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) RNA、乙型肝炎核心相关抗原 (HBcrAg) 和定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险的预后价值仍不确定。我们评估这些病毒标志物对接受长期恩替卡韦治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者的预测作用。方法:这项前瞻性、多中心研究纳入了在台湾接受长期恩替卡韦 治疗 和 HCC 监测 治疗的 CHB 相关肝硬化患者。入组时测量血清 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平。多变量回归确定了 HBV RNA 阳性和 HCC 发展的预测因素。结果:总体而言,纳入了 451 名患者。抗病毒药物治疗中位6.2年后,HBV RNA、HBcrAg和HBsAg的中位水平分别为0.9 log10拷贝/mL、3.2 log10 U/mL和2.4 log10 IU/mL。在中位 3.3 年的随访中,有 55 人发展为 HCC。57.2% 的患者可检测到 HBV RNA。HBV RNA 可检测性与年龄较大、丙氨酸氨基转移酶 (ALT) 较低、HBeAg 阳性以及较高的 HBsAg 和 HBcrAg 水平相关。在多变量分析中,较高的纤维化 4 (FIB-4) 指数(调整后的亚分布风险比 [aSHR],1.10;95% 置信区间 [CI] 1.00-1.21)和升高的甲胎蛋白 (AFP)(aSHR,1.01;95% CI 1.00-1.01)独立预测 HCC 的发展,而三种病毒标志物则不能独立预测 HCC 的发展。结论:经过 6 年恩替卡韦治疗后,57.2% 的 CHB 相关肝硬化患者可检测到 HBV RNA。第 6 年 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平不能预测 HCC,而 FIB-4 指数和 AFP 仍然是独立的预测因子。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
治疗 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) RNA、乙型肝炎核心相关抗原 (HBcrAg) 和定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险的预后价值仍不确定,这造成了关于它们在风险分层中的实用性的知识差距。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性、多中心研究在台湾入组了接受长期恩替卡韦 治疗 和 HCC 监测 治疗的 CHB 相关肝硬化患者,并在入组时测量了血清病毒标记物。
核心方法
在入组时测量了血清 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平,并通过多变量回归确定了 HBV RNA 阳性和 HCC 发展的预测因子。
主要结果
在 451 名患者中,经过中位 6.2 年的治疗后,中位 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平分别为 0.9 log10 拷贝/mL、3.2 log10 U/mL 和 2.4 log10 IU/mL;55 名患者在中位 3.3 年的随访期间发展为 HCC,多变量分析显示,较高的 FIB-4 指数和升高的 AFP 独立预测 HCC,而三种病毒标志物则不然。
解释与创新
作者解释说,6 年 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 水平并不能预测接受长期恩替卡韦治疗的 CHB 相关肝硬化患者的 HCC 风险,强调了 FIB-4 和 AFP 作为预测因子的持续相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,常规监测 HBV RNA、HBcrAg 和 HBsAg 可能无法改善该人群的 HCC 风险预测,支持继续依赖已建立的临床和实验室标志物。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和单一国家背景的限制,这可能会限制普遍性,并且由于缺乏针对特定目标的随机比较和潜在的残留混杂因素,证据被评估为中等。

HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels at year 6 do not predict HCC risk in patients with cirrhosis on long-term entecavir therapy.

Authors: Hung-Hsuan Pan, Tung-Hung Su, Tsung-Hui Hu, Wan-Long Chuang, Wei-Wen Su, Cheng-Kuan Lin, Cheng-Yuan Peng, Chia-Chi Wang, Yao-Chun Hsu, Yi-Wen Huang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: The prognostic value of on-treatment hepatitis B virus (HBV) RNA, hepatitis B core-related antigen (HBcrAg), and quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains uncertain. We evaluate the predictive roles of these viral markers in patients with CHB receiving long-term entecavir therapy. METHODS: This prospective, multicenter study enrolled patients with CHB-related cirrhosis undergoing long-term entecavir treatment and HCC surveillance in Taiwan. Serum HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels were measured at enrollment. Multivariable regression identified predictors of HBV RNA positivity and HCC development. RESULTS: Overall, 451 patients were included. After a median of 6.2 years of antiviral therapy, the median levels of HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg were 0.9 log10 copies/mL, 3.2 log10 U/mL, and 2.4 log10 IU/mL, respectively. During a median 3.3-year follow-up, 55 developed HCC. 57.2% of patients had detectable HBV RNA. HBV RNA detectability was associated with older age, lower alanine aminotransferase (ALT), HBeAg positivity, and higher HBsAg and HBcrAg levels. In multivariable analysis, higher fibrosis-4 (FIB-4) index (adjusted subdistribution hazard ratio [aSHR], 1.10; 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.00-1.21) and elevated alpha-fetoprotein (AFP) (aSHR, 1.01; 95% CI 1.00-1.01) independently predicted HCC development, whereas the three viral markers did not. CONCLUSION: After a 6-year entecavir therapy, 57.2% of patients with CHB-related cirrhosis had detectable HBV RNA. Year-6 HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels were not predictive of HCC, while FIB-4 index and AFP remained independent predictors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The prognostic value of on-treatment hepatitis B virus (HBV) RNA, hepatitis B core-related antigen (HBcrAg), and quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains uncertain, creating a knowledge gap regarding their utility in risk stratification.
Design and population
This prospective, multicenter study enrolled patients with CHB-related cirrhosis undergoing long-term entecavir treatment and HCC surveillance in Taiwan, with serum viral markers measured at enrollment.
Core methods
Serum HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels were measured at enrollment, and multivariable regression identified predictors of HBV RNA positivity and HCC development.
Main results
Among 451 patients, after a median 6.2 years of therapy, median HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels were 0.9 log10 copies/mL, 3.2 log10 U/mL, and 2.4 log10 IU/mL, respectively; 55 developed HCC during a median 3.3-year follow-up, and multivariable analysis showed that higher FIB-4 index and elevated AFP independently predicted HCC, whereas the three viral markers did not.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that year-6 HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg levels are not predictive of HCC risk in patients with CHB-related cirrhosis on long-term entecavir therapy, highlighting the continued relevance of FIB-4 and AFP as predictors.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that routine monitoring of HBV RNA, HBcrAg, and HBsAg may not improve HCC risk prediction in this population, supporting continued reliance on established clinical and laboratory markers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and single-country setting, which may restrict generalizability, and the evidence is assessed as moderate due to the absence of target-specific randomized comparisons and potential residual confounding.

利妥昔单抗治疗的 CKD 患者中 HBV 再激活的时间模式:基线定量 HBsAg 和预防持续时间的探索性分析。

Temporal Patterns of HBV Reactivation in Rituximab-Treated CKD Patients: An Exploratory Analysis of Baseline Quantitative HBsAg and Prophylaxis Duration.
作者： Jing Fang, Yue Yang, Xu Wang, Wenge Li, Xinzhen Guo
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是接受利妥昔单抗 (RTX) 治疗慢性肾脏病 (CKD) 的 HBsAg 阳性患者的严重并发症。我们评估了再激活的临床预测因素、最佳预防持续时间和停药后风险动力学。方法 我们回顾性分析了 105 例接受 RTX 治疗的 HBsAg 阳性 CKD 患者。根据 抗病毒药物 预防持续时间对参与者进行分层:A 组(RTX 后≥ 12-18 个月,n=41)、B 组(6-12 个月,n=47)和 C 组(结果 总体 HBV 再激活率为 7.6% (8/105)。观察到显着的“持续时间依赖性”保护作用,再激活率为 2.4%、6.4% 和A、B 和 C 组分别为 23.5%(P = 0.012)。基线 HBsAg > 2.4 log10 IU/mL(OR = 12.5,P = 0.005)和 抗病毒药物 预防持续时间 结论 延长 12-18 个月的 抗病毒药物 预防方案为 CKD 患者提供了针对基线 HBV 再激活的最佳保护。HBsAg > 2.4 log10 IU/mL 代表了风险升高的一个值得注意的标志。由于事件规模较小,这些发现仍然是探索性的和假设性的,至关重要的是,与非肾脏人群相比,观察到的 4 至 10 个月之间的时间聚类表明了不同的预防后风险时间线,这凸显了进一步定制的前瞻性验证的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
接受利妥昔单抗 (RTX) 治疗的慢性肾病 (CKD) 患者存在严重的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活风险,但临床预测因子和最佳 抗病毒药物 预防策略仍不确定。
设计与对象
这项回顾性分析包括 105 名接受 RTX 治疗的 HBsAg 阳性 CKD 患者,根据 抗病毒药物 预防持续时间分为三组:A 组(≥12-18 个月,n=41)、B 组(6-12 个月,n=47)和 C 组(<6 个月,n=17)。
核心方法
该研究利用回顾性数据评估了 HBV 再激活的临床预测因素、最佳预防持续时间和停药后风险动力学。
主要结果
总体 HBV 再激活发生率为 7.6% (8/105),其中 A 组再激活率为 2.4%,B 组为 6.4%,C 组为 23.5%(P=0.012)。基线 HBsAg >2.4 log10 IU/mL 与风险增加相关(OR=12.5,P=0.005)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明延长 12-18 个月的 抗病毒药物 预防可以为 CKD 患者提供针对 HBV 再激活的最佳保护,基线 HBsAg >2.4 log10 IU/mL 作为显着的风险标记。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果表明,长期 抗病毒药物 预防可能会降低接受 RTX 治疗的 CKD 患者的 HBV 再激活风险,特别是那些基线 HBsAg 水平较高的患者,值得前瞻性验证。
局限与证据强度
该研究的小事件规模和回顾性设计限制了因果推理和普遍性,作者将证据分为探索性证据和假设生成证据。

Temporal Patterns of HBV Reactivation in Rituximab-Treated CKD Patients: An Exploratory Analysis of Baseline Quantitative HBsAg and Prophylaxis Duration.

Authors: Jing Fang, Yue Yang, Xu Wang, Wenge Li, Xinzhen Guo
Original abstract (English)
Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a severe complication in HBsAg-positive patients receiving rituximab (RTX) for chronic kidney disease (CKD). We evaluated clinical predictors of reactivation, optimal prophylaxis duration, and post-discontinuation risk kinetics. Methods We retrospectively analyzed 105 HBsAg-positive CKD patients treated with RTX. Participants were stratified by antiviral prophylaxis duration: Group A (≥ 12-18 months post-RTX, n=41), Group B (6-12 months, n=47), and Group C ( Results The overall HBV reactivation was 7.6% (8/105). A significant "duration-dependent" protective effect was observed, with reactivation rates of 2.4%, 6.4%, and 23.5% in Groups A, B, and C, respectively (P = 0.012). Baseline HBsAg > 2.4 log10 IU/mL (OR = 12.5, P = 0.005) and antiviral prophylaxis duration Conclusions An extended 12-18 months antiviral prophylaxis regimen provides optimal protection against HBV reactivation in CKD patients. Baseline HBsAg > 2.4 log10 IU/mL represents a noteworthy marker of heightened risk. Due to the limitations of a small event size, these findings remain exploratory and hypothesis-generating. Crucially, the observed temporal clustering between 4 and 10 months indicates a distinct post-prophylaxis risk timeline compared to non-renal populations, highlighting the need for further tailored, prospective validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic kidney disease (CKD) patients receiving rituximab (RTX) are at risk of severe Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation, yet clinical predictors and optimal antiviral prophylaxis strategies remain uncertain.
Design and population
This retrospective analysis included 105 HBsAg-positive CKD patients treated with RTX, stratified by antiviral prophylaxis duration into three groups: Group A (≥12–18 months, n=41), Group B (6–12 months, n=47), and Group C (<6 months, n=17).
Core methods
The study evaluated clinical predictors of HBV reactivation, optimal prophylaxis duration, and post-discontinuation risk kinetics using retrospective data.
Main results
Overall HBV reactivation occurred in 7.6% (8/105) of patients, with reactivation rates of 2.4% in Group A, 6.4% in Group B, and 23.5% in Group C (P=0.012). Baseline HBsAg >2.4 log10 IU/mL was associated with increased risk (OR=12.5, P=0.005).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that extended 12–18 months of antiviral prophylaxis provides optimal protection against HBV reactivation in CKD patients, with baseline HBsAg >2.4 log10 IU/mL serving as a notable risk marker.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that prolonged antiviral prophylaxis may reduce HBV reactivation risk in RTX-treated CKD patients, particularly those with high baseline HBsAg levels, warranting prospective validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small event size and retrospective design limit causal inference and generalizability, and the authors classify the evidence as exploratory and hypothesis-generating.

血清流行率 和新诊断 HIV 患者中 HBV 和 HCV 合并感染的早期指标:来自印度的一项横断面研究。

Seroprevalence and Early Indicators of HBV and HCV Co-infection Among Newly Diagnosed HIV Patients: A Cross-Sectional Study from India.
作者： Nawaid Hussain Khan, Shashi Nandar Kumar, Vishwajeet Singh, Gaurav Kaushik, Hafiz Ahmad, Raj Narayan Yadav, Richa Vashishtha, Rohit Kumar Tiwari, Ankush Kumar Rana, Vinay Kumar
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HIV-HBV 合并感染对 HIV 感染者的肝脏相关发病率和 诊断 产生显着影响,而 HIV 诊断 的早期检测对于选择适当的抗逆转录病毒治疗以改善长期健康结果至关重要。
设计与对象
在印度 282 名新诊断的 HIV-1 成年患者中进行了一项横断面研究,这些患者使用免疫测定法筛查了 HBV (HBsAg) 和 HCV (抗 HCV),并分析了社会人口学、行为、血液学、生化、免疫学和病毒学参数。
核心方法
使用多项逻辑回归来确定参与者中 HBV 和 HCV 合并感染的相对风险比 (RRR)。
主要结果
HIV-HBV 和 HIV-HCV 合并感染的 血清流行率 分别为 3.9% 和 2.5%,之前或当前的注射吸毒被认为是最强的预测因子(HIV-HBV 的 RRR = 74.86,HIV-HCV 的 RRR = 174.67,均 p < 0.001)。与仅感染 HIV 的患者相比,合并感染的患者表现出显着升高的胆红素、SGOT、SGPT 和 ALP 水平、较低的血小板计数和血清白蛋白、较高的 ESR 和较高的 HIV 病毒载量。
解释与创新
该研究强调,基线实验室异常和高危行为可能作为 HBV 合并感染的早期指标,支持对新诊断的 HIV 患者进行常规肝炎筛查,并指出观察到的 HBV 合并感染 患病率 低于 HIV/AIDS 患者的全球估计值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在 HIV 诊断 进行常规 HBV 筛查可以及时做出治疗决策,特别是对于具有注射吸毒或非异性恋 传播 等高危行为的人群,以减轻肝病进展。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且该研究没有报告 HBV 基因型分布或长期临床结果,这将证据的普遍性和强度限制为中等。

Seroprevalence and Early Indicators of HBV and HCV Co-infection Among Newly Diagnosed HIV Patients: A Cross-Sectional Study from India.

Authors: Nawaid Hussain Khan, Shashi Nandar Kumar, Vishwajeet Singh, Gaurav Kaushik, Hafiz Ahmad, Raj Narayan Yadav, Richa Vashishtha, Rohit Kumar Tiwari, Ankush Kumar Rana, Vinay Kumar
Original abstract (English)
HIV-HBV and HIV-HCV co-infections contribute significantly to liver-associated morbidity and mortality among people living with HIV (HIV/AIDS). Detection of these hepatitis co-infections at the time of HIV diagnosis is critical for the selection of the most suitable HAART regimen to improve long-term health outcomes. A cross-sectional study was conducted among 282 newly diagnosed, HIV-1 adult patients and screened for HBV (HBsAg) and HCV (anti-HCV) using immunoassays. Sociodemographic, behavioral, hematological, biochemical, immunological, and virological parameters were analyzed. Multinomial logistic regression was used to determine relative risk ratios (RRR) for co-infections. Among all participants HIV-HBV and HIV-HCV co-infections had 3.9% and 2.5% seroprevalence respectively. Previous or present injection drug use was the best predictor, with RRR = 74.86 (95% CI 11.7-478.9, p < 0.001) for HIV-HBV and RRR = 174.67 (95% CI 22.6-1348.1, p < 0.001) for HIV-HC Non-heterosexual HIV transmission modes were also significant predictors (RRR = 8.30, 95% CI 2.2-31.2, p = 0.002 for HIV-HBV; RRR = 10.9, 2.3-52.7, HCV, p = 0.003). Compared to HIV-only patients, co-infected patients had significantly elevated bilirubin, SGOT, SGPT, and ALP levels, lower platelet counts and serum albumin, higher ESR, and higher HIV viral loads. Furthermore, sexual orientation (< 0.001) and substance abuse (< 0.001) significantly contribute to health risk behaviors. These findings highlights the prevalence of HBV and HCV co-infection among newly diagnosed HIV patients is lower than the global prevalence among HIV/AIDS. However, the baseline laboratory abnormalities and high-risk behaviors may serve as early indicators of co-infection, supporting routine hepatitis screening in newly diagnosed HIV patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HIV-HBV co-infection contributes significantly to liver-associated morbidity and mortality among people living with HIV, and early detection at HIV diagnosis is critical for selecting appropriate antiretroviral therapy to improve long-term health outcomes.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 282 newly diagnosed, HIV-1 adult patients in India who were screened for HBV (HBsAg) and HCV (anti-HCV) using immunoassays, with sociodemographic, behavioral, hematological, biochemical, immunological, and virological parameters analyzed.
Core methods
Multinomial logistic regression was used to determine relative risk ratios (RRR) for HBV and HCV co-infections among the participants.
Main results
HIV-HBV and HIV-HCV co-infections had seroprevalences of 3.9% and 2.5%, respectively, with previous or present injection drug use identified as the strongest predictor (RRR = 74.86 for HIV-HBV and RRR = 174.67 for HIV-HCV, both p < 0.001). Co-infected patients exhibited significantly elevated bilirubin, SGOT, SGPT, and ALP levels, lower platelet counts and serum albumin, higher ESR, and higher HIV viral loads compared to HIV-only patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that baseline laboratory abnormalities and high-risk behaviors may serve as early indicators of HBV co-infection, supporting routine hepatitis screening in newly diagnosed HIV patients, and notes that the observed HBV co-infection prevalence is lower than global estimates among HIV/AIDS patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that routine HBV screening at HIV diagnosis could enable timely therapeutic decisions, particularly in populations with high-risk behaviors such as injection drug use or non-heterosexual transmission, to mitigate liver disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the study does not report HBV genotype distribution or long-term clinical outcomes, which constrains the generalizability and strength of evidence to moderate.

喀麦隆 患病率 感染的 患病率:系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.
作者： Iya Maliki, Edward J D Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G Lim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了喀麦隆的人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染情况,重点关注使用 HBsAg 血清阳性作为诊断标志物估计普通人群和亚人群中 HBV 患病率 的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
这是一项系统综述和荟萃分析,检索了 Medline、Global Health、Embase、Web of Science、Cochrane Library、Africa Index Medicus 以及雅温得大学的《健康科学与疾病杂志》。符合条件的研究报告了一般人群和亚人群中的 HBsAg 患病率,使用随机效应荟萃分析进行评估,异质性通过 I2 量化,研究质量通过 Joanna Briggs Institute 关键评估工具进行评估。共纳入 92 篇论文、100 项研究。
共识与结论
喀麦隆的 HBV 患病率 总体汇总率为 9.98% (95% CI 9.00–11.02),异质性较高 (I2 = 98.10%)。与相对较低风险人群(8.94%,95% CI 7.84–10.11)相比,患病率 在相对较高风险人群中较高(12.70%,95% CI 10.28–15.32)。亚组分析将性别、地理区域(区域)、人口风险组和研究设计确定为异质性的主要来源。
争议与缺口
该综述报告了显着的异质性(I2 = 98.10%),尽管进行了亚组分析,但在很大程度上仍然无法解释,这表明纳入的研究之间存在潜在的未测量的混杂因素或方法学上的不一致。高度异质性限制了汇总估计的精度,并使区域和人群特定差异的解释变得复杂。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调需针对男性及血清流行率最高的地区实施有针对性的公共卫生干预措施,以减少乙型肝炎病毒传播并改善喀麦隆的防控措施。未来血清流行率研究应采用标准化的研究设计与报告,以减少异质性并提高可比性。

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Authors: Iya Maliki, Edward J D Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G Lim
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a global public health problem, and many regions of Sub-Saharan Africa are disproportionately affected, including Cameroon, where prevalence has previously been shown to be high. This study aimed to provide up-to-date estimates of HBV prevalence in Cameroon. We searched Medline, Global Health, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane Library, Africa Index Medicus and the Journal of Health Science and Disease at the University of Yaounde. We included studies reporting HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) prevalence in the general population and subpopulations. A random-effects meta-analysis was used to generate pooled estimates of HBsAg prevalence, overall and for population subgroups including those considered at relatively higher-risk and relatively lower-risk of HBV infection. Heterogeneity was assessed using the index of heterogeneity (I 2 ). Quality was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool. Out of 1236 records, 92 papers with 100 studies were included in the meta-analysis. Overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% confidence interval [CI] 9.00-11.02) with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 98.10%). The prevalences for relatively lower-risk and higher-risk populations were8.94% (95% CI 7.84-10.11) and 12.70% (95% CI 10.28-15.32), respectively. In subgroup analyses, we found that sex, geographical area (regions), population risk groups and study design were the main sources of heterogeneity. Overall, HBV prevalence remains high in Cameroon. Our findings suggest an urgent need for health interventions to halt the spread of the disease, with particular attention needed for males and regions with the highest prevalence.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in Cameroon, focusing on the central question of estimating HBV prevalence in the general population and subpopulations using HBsAg seropositivity as the diagnostic marker.
Evidence base and method
This is a systematic review and meta-analysis that searched Medline, Global Health, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane Library, Africa Index Medicus, and the Journal of Health Science and Disease at the University of Yaounde. Eligible studies reported HBsAg prevalence in the general population and subpopulations, assessed using a random-effects meta-analysis with heterogeneity quantified by I2 and study quality evaluated via the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool. A total of 92 papers with 100 studies were included.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled overall HBV prevalence in Cameroon is 9.98% (95% CI 9.00–11.02) with high heterogeneity (I2 = 98.10%). Prevalence is higher in relatively higher-risk populations (12.70%, 95% CI 10.28–15.32) compared to relatively lower-risk populations (8.94%, 95% CI 7.84–10.11). Subgroup analyses identified sex, geographical area (regions), population risk groups, and study design as major sources of heterogeneity.
Controversies and gaps
The review reports substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 98.10%) that remains largely unexplained despite subgroup analyses, indicating potential unmeasured confounders or methodological inconsistencies across included studies. The high heterogeneity limits the precision of pooled estimates and complicates interpretation of regional and population-specific differences.
Research and practice implications
The findings underscore the need for targeted public health interventions, particularly for males and regions with the highest HBV prevalence, to reduce transmission and improve control measures in Cameroon. Standardized study designs and reporting across future prevalence studies are required to address heterogeneity and improve comparability.

乙型肝炎的障碍 治疗:加拿大乙型肝炎网络进行的多中心横断面登记和调查研究。

Barriers to Hepatitis B Treatment: A Multicentre Cross-Sectional Registry and Survey Study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network.
作者： Hin Hin Ko, Jordyn Vienneau, Curtis L Cooper, Pamela Crotty, Stephen E. Congly, Mayur Brahmania, Alnoor Ramji, Erin Kelly, Angela M. Crawley, Karen Doucette
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是全世界肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因,但即使在资源丰富的环境中,治疗 的摄取也不是最佳的。这项泛加拿大研究旨在量化加拿大乙型肝炎网络 (CanHepB) 注册中心内的 治疗 差距,比较符合 治疗 资格但未经治疗的患者与正在接受治疗的患者的特征,并评估患者和专家对 治疗 障碍的看法。方法:对 2018 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间加拿大 18 个乙型肝炎专科诊所的 1983 名成人慢性乙型肝炎患者进行横断面回顾性分析。治疗 资格是根据 2018 年加拿大肝脏研究协会指南确定的。一项匿名调查记录了患者报告的障碍和观点 (n = 191),而另一项调查评估了专家的看法 (n = 54)。结果:在 881 名未经治疗的患者中,46.4% (n = 409) 符合 治疗 资格。与 治疗 组 (n = 1087) 相比,符合 治疗 条件但未接受治疗的患者更年轻(中位年龄 47.3 岁 vs. 54.5 岁),女性更常见(54.2% vs. 39.7%),并且更有可能是黑人/非洲/加勒比裔(18.2% vs. 他们的 患病率 较低)肝硬化(2.4% vs. 20.3%)、肝癌(0.7% vs. 9.3%)和高血压(17.1% vs. 22.9%),但脂肪变性肝病(36.4% vs. 24.6%)和血脂异常(14.7% vs. 未治疗的受访者)的 患病率 较高。在未治疗的受访者中,担忧包括副作用、需要长期治疗以及缺乏治疗。相反,专家认为成本是主要障碍,而副作用是最不令人担忧的。这项全国性研究揭示了慢性乙型肝炎护理中存在显着的 治疗 差距,这是由人口差异以及患者关注点和临床医生认知之间的不匹配造成的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了资源丰富的环境中成人中的人类 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染,重点关注 治疗 吸收障碍、符合 治疗 资格的患者之间的人口统计差异,以及患者报告的担忧与专家对治疗的看法之间的不匹配。
证据基础与方法
该研究对 2018 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间在加拿大 18 个专科诊所入组的 1983 名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的成年人进行了横断面回顾性分析,使用 2018 年加拿大肝脏研究协会指南来确定 治疗 的资格。
共识与结论
CHB 护理中存在显着的 治疗 差距,46.4% (409/881) 的未治疗患者符合资格标准;与接受治疗的患者相比,符合 治疗 资格但未经治疗的患者更年轻,女性居多,并且更有可能是黑人/非洲/加勒比裔。
争议与缺口
患者对副作用、长期治疗和缺乏治疗选择的担忧与专家的优先事项形成鲜明对比,在专家的优先事项中,成本是主要障碍,副作用是最不令人担忧的,这凸显了感知障碍的不匹配。
科研与实践启示
解决人口差异并调整患者和专家对障碍的看法对于提高 HBV 治疗 的吸收率至关重要;有针对性的干预措施应解决成本、长期治疗问题和可察觉的副作用。

Barriers to Hepatitis B Treatment: A Multicentre Cross-Sectional Registry and Survey Study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network.

Authors: Hin Hin Ko, Jordyn Vienneau, Curtis L Cooper, Pamela Crotty, Stephen E. Congly, Mayur Brahmania, Alnoor Ramji, Erin Kelly, Angela M. Crawley, Karen Doucette
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND & AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a leading cause of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) worldwide, yet treatment uptake is suboptimal even in high-resource settings. This pan-Canadian study aimed to quantify treatment gaps within the Canadian Hepatitis B Network (CanHepB) Registry, compare characteristics of treatment-eligible but untreated patients to those receiving therapy, and assess patient and specialist perspectives on barriers to treatment. METHODS: A cross-sectional, retrospective analysis of 1983 adults with CHB followed in 18 Canadian hepatitis B specialty clinics between January 2018 and November 2024. Treatment eligibility was determined using the 2018 Canadian Association for the Study of the Liver guidelines. An anonymous survey captured patient-reported barriers and perspectives (n = 191), while a separate survey evaluated specialist perceptions (n = 54). RESULTS: Among 881 untreated patients, 46.4% (n = 409) were treatment eligible. Compared with the treatment group (n = 1087), the treatment-eligible but untreated patients were younger (median 47.3 vs. 54.5 years), more frequently female (54.2% vs. 39.7%), and more likely to be of Black/African/Caribbean origin (18.2% vs. They had a lower prevalence of cirrhosis (2.4% vs. 20.3%), HCC (0.7% vs. 9.3%), and hypertension (17.1% vs. 22.9%), but a higher prevalence of steatotic liver disease (36.4% vs. 24.6%) and dyslipidemia (14.7% vs. Among untreated respondents, concerns included side effects, the need for long-term therapy, and lack of curative options. Conversely, specialists identified cost as the primary barrier and side effects as the least concerning. CONCLUSION: This nationwide study reveals a significant treatment gap in CHB care, driven by demographic disparities and a mismatch between patient concerns and clinician perceptions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in adults within high-resource settings, focusing on treatment uptake barriers, demographic disparities among treatment-eligible patients, and the mismatch between patient-reported concerns and specialist perceptions regarding therapy.
Evidence base and method
The study is a cross-sectional, retrospective analysis of 1983 adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) enrolled in 18 Canadian specialty clinics from January 2018 to November 2024, using the 2018 Canadian Association for the Study of the Liver guidelines to determine treatment eligibility.
Consensus and conclusions
A significant treatment gap exists in CHB care, with 46.4% (409/881) of untreated patients meeting eligibility criteria; treatment-eligible but untreated patients are younger, more often female, and more likely to be of Black/African/Caribbean origin compared to those receiving therapy.
Controversies and gaps
Patient concerns about side effects, long-term therapy, and lack of curative options contrast with specialist priorities, where cost is the primary barrier and side effects are least concerning, highlighting a mismatch in perceived barriers.
Research and practice implications
Addressing demographic disparities and aligning patient and specialist perspectives on barriers are critical to improving HBV treatment uptake; targeted interventions should address cost, long-term therapy concerns, and perceived side effects.

乙型肝炎患者接受英国二级护理的情况:范围界定审查。

Attendance Among People With Hepatitis B in UK Secondary Care: A Scoping Review.
作者： Emma Victoria Shiel, Bini George, Arpana Verma, Norina Gasteiger
摘要中文翻译摘要 慢性乙型肝炎 疾病需要终身监测,以预防肝硬化和肝细胞癌等并发症。尽管有结构化的护理途径,英国二级护理中的缺勤仍然具有挑战性。本次范围界定审查旨在综合影响不参加二级保健预约的因素的现有证据,以及提高 慢性乙型肝炎 患者预约依从性的策略。资格标准:经过同行评审的资料来源检查了英国二级护理服务中乙型肝炎成人的就诊情况。灰色文献被排除在外。证据来源:在 CINAHL Ultimate、Medline、Embase、Scopus 和 APA PsycINFO 中进行检索。使用结构化数据提取形式绘制数据图表并进行叙述性综合。十一项研究符合纳入标准。已确定的障碍包括与患者相关的因素(例如,有限的健康素养、污名化、相互竞争的优先事项)、系统层面的挑战(例如,预约安排和可及性)以及文化或语言障碍。心理因素,包括对 诊断 和 治疗 副作用的恐惧,也有报道。报告的策略包括患者教育、根据文化量身定制的干预措施、提醒系统(例如短信和电话)以及涉及社区外展的综合护理模式。慢性乙型肝炎 患者不去二级护理机构的情况受到个人、社会和系统因素复杂相互作用的影响。解决多重障碍的干预措施,特别是那些纳入文化敏感方法和提醒系统的干预措施,可能会提高参与度。需要进一步研究与受影响社区共同设计和评估干预措施。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查调查了患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的成年人在英国二级护理服务中的出勤情况,重点关注影响未出勤的因素以及提高预约依从性的策略。核心问题解决了参与 慢性乙型肝炎 管理二级护理的患者、系统和文化障碍。
证据基础与方法
该范围界定综述检索了 CINAHL Ultimate、Medline、Embase、Scopus 和 APA PsycINFO,不包括灰色文献。资格标准包括接受英国二级护理的成人乙型肝炎患者的同行评审来源。数据采用结构化提取形式绘制并以叙述方式进行综合,其中包括 11 项研究。
共识与结论
缺勤的原因包括患者相关因素(例如,健康素养有限、污名化、优先事项相互竞争)、系统层面的挑战(例如时间安排和可及性)以及文化或语言障碍。心理因素如对 诊断 和 治疗 副作用的恐惧也有报道。已报告有效的策略包括患者教育、根据文化量身定制的干预措施、提醒系统(例如短信和电话)以及涉及社区外展的综合护理模式。
争议与缺口
该审查没有报告干预措施的定量汇总估计或比较有效性。研究设计和人群的异质性限制了综合,并且证据基础不能解决出勤率提高或策略成本效益的长期结果。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括与受影响社区共同设计干预措施,评估文化敏感方法和提醒系统,以及评估综合护理模式对出勤率和临床结果的影响。研究还应解决系统层面的障碍以及健康素养在缺勤中的作用。

Attendance Among People With Hepatitis B in UK Secondary Care: A Scoping Review.

Authors: Emma Victoria Shiel, Bini George, Arpana Verma, Norina Gasteiger
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic Hepatitis B disease requires lifelong monitoring to prevent complications such as cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Despite structured care pathways, non‐attendance within UK secondary care remains challenging. This scoping review aimed to synthesise existing evidence on factors influencing non‐attendance at secondary care appointments and strategies to improve appointment adherence among people living with Chronic Hepatitis B. Eligibility Criteria: Peer‐reviewed sources examining adults with Hepatitis B attendance at UK secondary care services. Grey literature was excluded. Sources of Evidence: Searches were performed in CINAHL Ultimate, Medline, Embase, Scopus and APA PsycINFO. Data were charted using a structured data extraction form and synthesised narratively. Eleven studies met the inclusion criteria. Identified barriers included patient‐related factors (e.g., limited health literacy, stigmatisation, competing priorities), system‐level challenges (e.g., appointment scheduling and accessibility) and cultural or language barriers. Psychological factors, including fear of diagnosis and treatment side effects, were also reported. Reported strategies included patient education, culturally tailored interventions, reminder systems (e.g., SMS and telephone calls) and integrated care models involving community outreach. Non‐attendance among people with chronic Hepatitis B in secondary care is influenced by a complex interplay of personal, social and systemic factors. Interventions that address multiple barriers, particularly those that incorporate culturally sensitive approaches and reminder systems, may improve engagement. Further research is needed to co‐design and evaluate interventions with affected communities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines attendance among adults with chronic hepatitis B at UK secondary care services, focusing on factors influencing non-attendance and strategies to improve appointment adherence. The central question addresses patient, system and cultural barriers to engagement in secondary care for chronic hepatitis B management.
Evidence base and method
This scoping review searched CINAHL Ultimate, Medline, Embase, Scopus and APA PsycINFO, excluding grey literature. Eligibility criteria included peer-reviewed sources on adults with hepatitis B attending UK secondary care. Data were charted using a structured extraction form and synthesised narratively, with 11 studies included.
Consensus and conclusions
Non-attendance is driven by patient-related factors (e.g., limited health literacy, stigmatisation, competing priorities), system-level challenges (e.g., scheduling and accessibility) and cultural or language barriers. Psychological factors such as fear of diagnosis and treatment side effects were also reported. Strategies with reported effectiveness include patient education, culturally tailored interventions, reminder systems (e.g., SMS and telephone calls) and integrated care models involving community outreach.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report quantitative pooled estimates or comparative effectiveness of interventions. Heterogeneity in study designs and populations limits synthesis, and the evidence base does not address long-term outcomes of improved attendance or cost-effectiveness of strategies.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include co-designing interventions with affected communities, evaluating culturally sensitive approaches and reminder systems, and assessing the impact of integrated care models on attendance and clinical outcomes. Research should also address system-level barriers and the role of health literacy in non-attendance.

德国跨性别者和非二元社区中自我报告的乙型肝炎诊断和 疫苗接种 病史,TASG 研究* 2022

Self-reported hepatitis B diagnoses and vaccination history in trans and non-binary communities in Germany, the TASG-study* 2022
作者： Renke Biallas, Michael Brandl, Sofia Burdi, Jonas A. Hamm, Chris Spurgat, Kathleen Pöge, Viviane Bremer, Sandra Dudareva, Uwe Koppe, Né Manuel Ricardo Alexander Robin Christoph Fink Garcia Hahne Saalfeld Schuler
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注德国跨性别者和非二元性别人群 (TNBP) 中自我报告的乙型肝炎 (HepB) 诊断和 疫苗接种 病史。核心问题是评估 HepB 诊断的 患病率 和该人群中的 疫苗接种 覆盖率,并在推荐的 HepB 疫苗接种 患者中确定 疫苗接种 阳性病史的潜在预测因子。
证据基础与方法
该研究是 2022 年德国 TNBP 的横断面在线调查。使用 患病率 比率 (PR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 分析数据。使用引导逐步选择程序来识别正 疫苗接种 历史的预测因子。该分析包括 2,358 名参与者的回复。
共识与结论
参与者中自我报告的 HepB 诊断很少,0.6% (13/2,358) 报告之前有过 诊断。自我报告阳性疫苗接种病史的比例为56.3%(1,225/2,175)。不同亚组的积极 疫苗接种 历史各不相同,与农村地区相比,男性、受教育程度较高的参与者以及大城市参与者的比例较高。随着已知肝炎事实数量的增加,疫苗接种 病史阳性的比例也随之增加。
争议与缺口
该研究未提供影响 HepB 感染或 疫苗接种 结果的生物或遗传因素的信息。它还没有解决 HepB 基因型对疾病进展或 疫苗 疗效的潜在影响。对自我报告数据的依赖可能会引入偏差,并且该研究没有探讨 TNBP 中 疫苗接种 率低背后的原因。
科研与实践启示
为了减少未满足 疫苗接种 需求的 TNBP 数量并提高 疫苗 的吸收率,在规划和实施有针对性的干预措施时应考虑 TNBP 知识和生活经验的差距。收集可靠数据对于到 2030 年消除 HepB 至关重要。

Self-reported hepatitis B diagnoses and vaccination history in trans and non-binary communities in Germany, the TASG-study* 2022

Authors: Renke Biallas, Michael Brandl, Sofia Burdi, Jonas A. Hamm, Chris Spurgat, Kathleen Pöge, Viviane Bremer, Sandra Dudareva, Uwe Koppe, Né Manuel Ricardo Alexander Robin Christoph Fink Garcia Hahne Saalfeld Schuler
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In Germany, little is known about hepatitis B (HepB) prevalence and vaccination coverage among transgender and non-binary people (TNBP), a WHO-defined viral hepatitis key population. Our study aimed to assess self-reported HepB diagnoses, self-reported positive vaccination history, and potential predictors for a positive vaccination history in a subset of participants for whom the German standing committee on vaccination recommend HepB vaccination. METHODS: We analysed data from a 2022 cross-sectional online survey of TNBP in Germany. We calculated percentages and prevalence ratios (PR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI). Predictors of a positive vaccination history among participants potentially recommended for HepB vaccination (e.g. > 1 sexual partner in the last 12 months, people who inject drugs) were identified using a bootstrapped stepwise selection procedure. RESULTS: We analysed the answers from 2,358 participants. Of these, 0.6% (13/2,358) reported previous HepB diagnosis and the proportion of self-reported positive vaccination history was 56.3% (1,225/2,175). Positive vaccination history varied across subgroups, with higher proportions among participants identifying on the male spectrum than on the female (PR: 1.29; 95% CI: 1.14, 1.47) or non-binary spectrum (PR: 1.27; 95% CI: 1.13, 1.44), participants with higher education levels compared to lower (PR: 1.44, 95% CI: 1.26, 1.68), and in big cities compared to rural areas (PR: 1.22, 95% CI: 1.07, 1.41). Overall, 550 (21.7%) participants were identified as potentially being recommended for HepB vaccination, with a total of 213 participants reporting a negative HepB vaccination history. 65% of these 213 participants were younger than 30 years and should have received the recommended childhood HepB vaccination. The proportion of positive vaccination history increased with the number of hepatitis facts known. The bootstrapped stepwise selection procedure identified knowledge of hepatitis facts as potential predictor for a positive vaccination history (relative selection frequency: 100%). CONCLUSION: Self-reported HepB diagnoses were rare among participants and comparable to prevalence estimates in the general population in Germany. To reduce the number of TNBP with unmet vaccination needs and to improve vaccine uptake in this population, gaps in knowledge and lived experiences of TNBP need to be considered in the planning and implementation of targeted interventions. The collection of reliable data is essential on the road to achieve elimination by 2030.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on self-reported hepatitis B (HepB) diagnoses and vaccination history among transgender and non-binary people (TNBP) in Germany. The central question is to assess the prevalence of HepB diagnoses and vaccination coverage in this population, as well as identify potential predictors for a positive vaccination history among those recommended for HepB vaccination.
Evidence base and method
The study is a 2022 cross-sectional online survey of TNBP in Germany. Data were analyzed using prevalence ratios (PR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI). Predictors of a positive vaccination history were identified using a bootstrapped stepwise selection procedure. The analysis included responses from 2,358 participants.
Consensus and conclusions
Self-reported HepB diagnoses were rare among participants, with 0.6% (13/2,358) reporting a previous diagnosis. The proportion of self-reported positive vaccination history was 56.3% (1,225/2,175). Positive vaccination history varied across subgroups, with higher proportions among participants identifying on the male spectrum, those with higher education levels, and those in big cities compared to rural areas. The proportion of positive vaccination history increased with the number of hepatitis facts known.
Controversies and gaps
The study does not provide information on the biological or genetic factors influencing HepB infection or vaccination outcomes. It also does not address the potential impact of HepB genotypes on disease progression or vaccine efficacy. The reliance on self-reported data may introduce bias, and the study does not explore the reasons behind the low vaccination rates among TNBP.
Research and practice implications
To reduce the number of TNBP with unmet vaccination needs and improve vaccine uptake, gaps in knowledge and lived experiences of TNBP should be considered in the planning and implementation of targeted interventions. The collection of reliable data is essential to achieve elimination of HepB by 2030.

Journal: Journal of Yeungnam Medical Science  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-10  |  DOI: 10.12701/jyms.2026.43.55  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 43, 55
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

慢性乙型肝炎 中从恩替卡韦到替诺福韦艾拉酚胺的精确切换:将患者表型与疗效、安全性和价值相匹配:叙述性回顾。

Precision switching from entecavir to tenofovir alafenamide in chronic hepatitis B: matching the patient phenotype to efficacy, safety, and value: a narrative review.
作者： Soon Woo Nam
摘要中文翻译在韩国,将 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒学稳定的患者从恩替卡韦换为替诺福韦艾拉酚胺已成为惯例;然而,文献已经确定转换是否在人群水平而不是在患者水平有效。这次审查将这一决定重新定义为候选人选择之一。将 2026 年韩国多中心随机试验与 2023 年至 2025 年真实世界和药物经济学数据相结合表明,在完全抑制的患者中,转换是非劣效且安全的,但增加很少,并且分析将针对富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯生成的证据与转换决策必须依赖的稀疏恩替卡韦对照证据分开。然后定义具有有利获益-风险特征的五种表型——低水平病毒血症或部分病毒学应答、估计肾小球滤过率下降或早期肾小管损伤、骨质流失或骨折风险、恩替卡韦或多药耐药性、计划或确认怀孕——以及两种条件情况:失代偿性肝硬化,除非肾脏或骨疾病共存,否则不需要常规转换;以及高心血管风险,需要血脂监测而不是排除。每种表型都映射到 2026 年韩国肝脏研究协会指南和 2025 年 11 月修订的韩国报销标准,进行确定性分级,并整合到一种可跨卫生系统转移的算法中,因为这些表型得到了国际认可。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了病毒学稳定的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者从恩替卡韦换为替诺福韦艾拉酚胺的精确度,将这一决定重新定义为候选药物选择之一,而不是人群水平疗效(A1、A2)。核心问题是,在排除一般人群水平结果的情况下,转换对于特定患者表型是否安全且非劣质。
证据基础与方法
该综述将 2026 年韩国多中心随机试验与 2023 年至 2025 年现实世界和药物经济学数据整合起来,将针对富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯的证据与稀疏的恩替卡韦对照证据区分开来 (A3)。它将表型映射到 2026 年韩国肝脏研究协会指南和 2025 年 11 月修订的韩国报销标准,对确定性进行分级并将结果合并到算法中 (A5)。
共识与结论
在完全抑制的患者中,从恩替卡韦换为替诺福韦艾拉酚胺并不劣质且安全,但几乎没有增加临床益处 (A3)。该综述确定了五种具有有利获益-风险特征的表型:低水平病毒血症或部分病毒学应答、估计肾小球滤过率下降或早期肾小管损伤、骨质流失或骨折风险、恩替卡韦或多药耐药性以及计划或确认妊娠(A4)。
争议与缺口
证据基础受到恩替卡韦对照试验稀疏性的限制,必须根据这些试验做出转换决定,大多数数据来自富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯比较,而不是恩替卡韦 (A3)。该综述并未涉及长期结果,例如肝细胞癌 发病率 或转换后的肝硬化进展。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑恩替卡韦对照随机试验,以加强转换决策的证据(A3)。临床医生应应用表型特异性算法来指导转换,纳入肾脏、骨骼、抵抗和妊娠考虑因素,同时监测高心血管风险患者的血脂状况 (A4)。

Precision switching from entecavir to tenofovir alafenamide in chronic hepatitis B: matching the patient phenotype to efficacy, safety, and value: a narrative review.

Authors: Soon Woo Nam
Original abstract (English)
Switching patients who are virologically stable with chronic hepatitis B from entecavir to tenofovir alafenamide has become routine in Korea; however, the literature has established whether switching works at the population level rather than at the patient level. This review reframes this decision as one of candidate selection. Integrating a 2026 Korean multicenter randomized trial with 2023 to 2025 real-world and pharmacoeconomic data shows that in patients who are fully suppressed, the switch is noninferior and safe but adds little, and the analysis separates evidence generated against tenofovir disoproxil fumarate from the sparser entecavir-controlled evidence on which switching decisions must rest. Five phenotypes with a favorable benefit-risk profile are then defined-low-level viremia or partial virologic response, declining estimated glomerular filtration rate or early tubular injury, bone loss or fracture risk, entecavir or multidrug resistance, and planned or confirmed pregnancy-alongside two conditional situations: decompensated cirrhosis, where routine switching is unnecessary unless renal or bone disease coexists, and high cardiovascular risk, which requires lipid monitoring rather than exclusion. Each phenotype is mapped to the 2026 Korean Association for the Study of the Liver guideline and to Korean reimbursement criteria revised in November 2025, graded for certainty, and consolidated into one algorithm that transfers across health systems because the phenotypes are internationally recognized.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines precision switching from entecavir to tenofovir alafenamide in patients with chronic hepatitis B who are virologically stable, reframing the decision as one of candidate selection rather than population-level efficacy (A1, A2). The central question is whether switching is safe and noninferior in specific patient phenotypes, excluding general population-level outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The review integrates a 2026 Korean multicenter randomized trial with 2023 to 2025 real-world and pharmacoeconomic data, separating evidence generated against tenofovir disoproxil fumarate from sparser entecavir-controlled evidence (A3). It maps phenotypes to the 2026 Korean Association for the Study of the Liver guideline and Korean reimbursement criteria revised in November 2025, grading certainty and consolidating findings into an algorithm (A5).
Consensus and conclusions
In patients fully suppressed, switching from entecavir to tenofovir alafenamide is noninferior and safe but adds little clinical benefit (A3). The review identifies five phenotypes with a favorable benefit-risk profile: low-level viremia or partial virologic response, declining estimated glomerular filtration rate or early tubular injury, bone loss or fracture risk, entecavir or multidrug resistance, and planned or confirmed pregnancy (A4).
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base is limited by the sparsity of entecavir-controlled trials on which switching decisions must rest, with most data derived from tenofovir disoproxil fumarate comparisons rather than entecavir (A3). The review does not address long-term outcomes such as hepatocellular carcinoma incidence or cirrhosis progression following the switch.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize entecavir-controlled randomized trials to strengthen evidence for switching decisions (A3). Clinicians should apply phenotype-specific algorithms to guide switching, incorporating renal, bone, resistance, and pregnancy considerations, while monitoring lipid profiles in high cardiovascular risk patients (A4).

BEAT-B 研究(行为、愿望和 治疗 需求):一项针对南亚和东南亚 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的患者价值观、偏好和护理障碍的混合方法研究。

BEAT-B Study (Behavior, Aspirations, and Treatment Needs): A Mixed-Methods Study of Patient Values, Preferences, and Barriers to Care Among People Living With Chronic Hepatitis B in South and Southeast Asia.
作者： Manya Prasad, Tushar Prabhakar, Priyanka Shenoy, Priyanka Aggarwal, Tawesak Tanwandee, K. C. Sudhamshu, M. Golam Azam, Zaigham Abbas, G. Vaishnavi, Vivekanandan Shanmugam
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 由于社会文化障碍、医疗保健系统效率低下和经济限制,慢性乙型肝炎 (HBV) 感染在 治疗 的吸收和依从性方面面临重大挑战。决策框架的证据强调了了解患者价值观和偏好的重要性,以使循证政策与“治疗所有人”的方法保持一致。方法 这项混合方法研究包括对五个南亚国家的横断面调查以及通过焦点小组讨论和访谈收集的定性数据。定性数据采用主题分析,探讨了患者在护理连续体中的看法、障碍和促进因素。定量数据分析了 治疗 摄取和依从性的人口、社会经济和临床决定因素。结果 定性分析确定了关键主题,例如乙型肝炎病毒感染者的心理负担、治疗 保护亲人的愿望,以及明确的指导和社会支持在促进护理方面的关键作用。定量研究结果显示,高等教育水平(OR:2.0;95% CI:1.3-3.1)、家庭支持(OR:1.9;95% CI:1.3-2.8)和城市居住(OR:1.7;95% CI:1.1-2.5)是 治疗 吸收的显着预测因素。人们注意到,患者对乙型肝炎慢性性质的认识和理解存在显着差距,只有 20.5% 的患者认为这是一种长期疾病,需要家庭咨询。解释 这些发现强调了将患者价值观和生活经历纳入乙肝护理模式和政策制定的重要性。简化护理途径、加强咨询、增强家庭参与和提高负担能力可能有助于解决 治疗 接受和保留护理的障碍。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究的重点是了解南亚和东南亚 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的患者价值观、偏好和护理障碍。核心问题是如何通过纳入患者的观点,使循证政策与“治疗所有人”的方法保持一致。
证据基础与方法
这项混合方法研究包括对五个南亚国家的横断面调查以及通过焦点小组讨论和访谈收集的定性数据。使用主题分析对定性数据进行分析,而定量数据则探讨 治疗 摄取和依从性的人口、社会经济和临床决定因素。
共识与结论
定性分析确定了一些关键主题,例如乙型肝炎病毒感染者的心理负担、治疗 保护亲人的愿望,以及明确的指导和社会支持在促进护理方面的关键作用。定量研究结果表明,高等教育水平、家庭支持和城市居住是 治疗 吸收的重要预测因素。
争议与缺口
患者对乙型肝炎慢性性质的了解存在显着差距,只有 20.5% 的患者认为这是一种需要家庭咨询的长期疾病。
科研与实践启示
简化护理途径、加强咨询、增强家庭参与和提高负担能力可能有助于解决 治疗 接受和保留护理的障碍。

BEAT-B Study (Behavior, Aspirations, and Treatment Needs): A Mixed-Methods Study of Patient Values, Preferences, and Barriers to Care Among People Living With Chronic Hepatitis B in South and Southeast Asia.

Authors: Manya Prasad, Tushar Prabhakar, Priyanka Shenoy, Priyanka Aggarwal, Tawesak Tanwandee, K. C. Sudhamshu, M. Golam Azam, Zaigham Abbas, G. Vaishnavi, Vivekanandan Shanmugam
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Chronic hepatitis B (HBV) infections have significant challenges in treatment uptake and adherence due to sociocultural barriers, healthcare system inefficiencies, and economic constraints. The evidence to decision framework highlights the importance of understanding patient values and preferences to align evidence‐based policies with the “treat all” approach. Methods This mixed‐methods study included a cross‐sectional survey in five South Asian countries and qualitative data collection through focus group discussions and interviews. Qualitative data explored patient perceptions, barriers, and facilitators in the care continuum, employing thematic analysis. Quantitative data analyzed demographic, socioeconomic, and clinical determinants of treatment uptake and adherence. Findings Qualitative analysis identified key themes, such as the psychological burden of living with HBV, the aspiration for treatment to protect loved ones, and the critical role of clear guidance and social support in facilitating care. Quantitative findings revealed that higher education levels (OR: 2.0; 95% CI: 1.3–3.1), family support (OR: 1.9; 95% CI: 1.3–2.8), and urban residence (OR: 1.7; 95% CI: 1.1–2.5) were significant predictors of treatment uptake. A significant gap in the knowledge and understanding of the chronic nature of HBV among patients, with only 20.5% recognizing it as a long‐term condition and need for family counseling, was noted. Interpretation These findings highlight the importance of incorporating patient values and lived experiences into HBV care models and policy development. Simplified care pathways, strengthened counseling, enhanced family engagement, and improved affordability may help address barriers to treatment uptake and retention in care.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on understanding patient values, preferences, and barriers to care among people living with chronic hepatitis B in South and Southeast Asia. The central question is how to align evidence-based policies with the "treat all" approach by incorporating patient perspectives.
Evidence base and method
This mixed-methods study included a cross-sectional survey in five South Asian countries and qualitative data collection through focus group discussions and interviews. Qualitative data were analyzed using thematic analysis, while quantitative data explored demographic, socioeconomic, and clinical determinants of treatment uptake and adherence.
Consensus and conclusions
Qualitative analysis identified key themes such as the psychological burden of living with HBV, the aspiration for treatment to protect loved ones, and the critical role of clear guidance and social support in facilitating care. Quantitative findings showed that higher education levels, family support, and urban residence were significant predictors of treatment uptake.
Controversies and gaps
There is a significant gap in patients' understanding of the chronic nature of HBV, with only 20.5% recognizing it as a long-term condition requiring family counseling.
Research and practice implications
Simplified care pathways, strengthened counseling, enhanced family engagement, and improved affordability may help address barriers to treatment uptake and retention in care.
